*(Obtain Styxia Fables)*

### Discuss your next steps with Aglaea and the others

> *An unexpected encounter with the Flame Reaver led to the premature extraction of Oronyx's Coreflame. The Chrysos Heirs hope that (Trailblazer) will take on the authority of Time. According to Mem, (Trailblazer) had died halfway through the Express's descent, and now is just a walking memory. Thanks to the unique life-death laws of Amphoreus, (Trailblazer) still has a slim chance of survival, and finding Thanatos has become the priority task. (Trailblazer) will be just fine, I sincerely pray so.*

*(Talk to Castorice again, optional)*

**Castorice:** Lady Aglaea and Lord Phainon are currently discussing some matters in the baths. They're worried about you, too. If possible, please go there and let them know that you're well. Although I don't actually know whether your current situation can be described as "well"...

> **遐蝶：**以及，这本斯缇科西亚寓言，请收下吧…人在阅读时可以集中精神，放松身心…希望这能帮助阁下度过最艰难的时光。

*(Approach Aglaea and Phainon)*

**Aglaea:** ...They're here.

> **阿格莱雅：**…他们/她们来了。

**Phainon:** (Trailblazer)! Hyacine said that you'd definitely wake up. How... do you feel?

> **白厄：**开拓者！风堇说过你会醒来的。你…感觉还好吗？

- ▸ So, the entire world knows about this now...

**Mem:** I beg you, please forgive me!

> **迷迷：**求求了，原谅人家吧！

- ▸ She can even take the pulse of someone who's dead...?

**Phainon:** That's the medical prowess of the Twilight Courtyard. Amazing, isn't it?

> **白厄：**昏光庭院的医术，很神奇吧？

- ▸ It feels quite cool being a departed spirit.

**Phainon:** Well, at least you look no different from the living. I think that most living people don't even look as good as you do.

> **白厄：**起码你看起来跟活人没什么两样。应该说，大部分活人气色都不如你。

**Aglaea:** You've completed the trial of Time, and I have to admit that the process went better than I expected. Even in the end, that Titan's heart still showed a soft spot for humankind.

> **阿格莱雅：**你们完成了「岁月」的试炼。我必须承认，过程远比预想中顺利。直到最后，那位泰坦的心中仍给人子留有一片温软。

**Aglaea:** However, I never expected that... two people who have cheated death would appear in Okhema at the same time.

> **阿格莱雅：**但我没能料到…奥赫玛居然同时出现了两位「欺瞒死亡之人」。

**Phainon:** That's what we have been talking about. Ugh, Mydei has been in Okhema for such a long time but — has no one ever thought to study his indestructible body? If there were even any minor breakthroughs, we wouldn't be as clueless as we are right now...

> **白厄：**我们刚才就在聊这事。该死，万敌在奥赫玛待了那么久，怎么就没人研究过他的不死之身？但凡能留下一点成果，现在也不至于毫无头绪……

**Aglaea:** Many have had this idea, but so far, there have been no results. If it were successful, Thanatos wouldn't have disappeared without a trace.

> **阿格莱雅：**你的想法早有人尝试过，如果有效，塞纳托斯就不会全无音讯。

**Aglaea:** Nevertheless, now is not the time for us to give up. At the very least, we can still see a glimmer of hope.

> **阿格莱雅：**但还未到放弃的时候。至少，此刻我还能想到一线希望。

**Aglaea:** Cipher... If anyone in this world knows the whereabouts of Death, it would be her.

> **阿格莱雅：**赛飞儿…假如这世上还有人可能掌握了「死亡」的去向，那便是她了。

**Aglaea:** I'll try and get her to return to Okhema but do be prepared for a less-than-ideal outcome. Please understand that throughout the history of Amphoreus, no one has ever managed to escape the clutches of Death. At the end of time, Thanatos silently awaits all.

> **阿格莱雅：**我会设法让她返回奥赫玛，但也请做好希望落空的准备。请你理解，在翁法罗斯的历史上，没有人能逃离「死亡」的掌心。于时光的尽头，塞纳托斯静候着每一个人。

- ▸ Thank you for your help.

**Aglaea:** It is my duty. We too require you to bear the divine position of Time.

> **阿格莱雅：**这是分内之事。退一万步讲，我们也需要你支撑起「岁月」的神位。

- ▸ Is this supposed to be... comforting?

**Phainon:** Don't take it to heart, (Trailblazer). After all, you've already created countless miracles along the way, right?

> **白厄：**别放在心上，开拓者。你这一路上已经创造了无数奇迹，对吧？

**Aglaea:** But before that, just...

> **阿格莱雅：**在那之前，就请好好……

**Phainon:** What's wrong, Aglaea?

> **白厄：**怎么了，阿格莱雅？

**Aglaea:** Omens always come in pairs. A tiny air current will eventually brew into a storm that tears the sky apart.

> **阿格莱雅：**厄兆总是成双，一股股微小的气流，终究要酝织成撕裂天幕的风暴了。

**Aglaea:** It's Anaxagoras. He has initiated contact with the Council of Elders.

> **阿格莱雅：**阿那克萨戈拉斯，他开始和元老院接触了。

**Phainon:** In the end, it has still come down to this...

> **白厄：**果然还是变成了这样……

**Aglaea:** I earnestly hope that your assessment of him is accurate, Phainon.

> **阿格莱雅：**我衷心希望你对他的判断是正确的，白厄。

**Aglaea:** Otherwise, the efforts of my teacher and I over the past millennium... might be reduced to dust because of that "Great Performer"

> **阿格莱雅：**否则，我与吾师千年来的努力…也许将因那位「大表演家」付之一炬呢。

Switching to Anaxa's POV...

The pursuit of knowledge should follow a few simple principles, such as "making the best use of people and things"...

## 115　Olive, Cast to the Conference Chair　橄榄啊，请掷向议厅之主

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Anaxa; Cerces; Lygus; Wizened Priest; Young Scribe; Military Runner; Caenis; Calypso; Gnaeus; Polyxia; Empedocles　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Spindle, Laboring to Weave the Tapestry of Time'' completed

Not long ago...

**Anaxa:** Hehe...

> **那刻夏：**呵……

**Anaxa:** I'm back here again.

> **那刻夏：**又回到这里了。

I remember this dream. This realm is covered in a silk-like fog, and the land is barren and harsh. The crowd of travelers leaves tracks everywhere.

**Anaxa:** Hey! Excuse me — what is this place? Where in Amphoreus are we?

> **那刻夏：**喂！请问——这是什么地方，位于翁法罗斯何处？

**Wandering Traveler:** ...

The mutters of the traveler are in a language that I'm unable to comprehend. Or perhaps, they're just distracted and ignored my question.

**Anaxa:** Tell me, which Titan reigns over this land and how do I find them?

> **那刻夏：**告诉我，是哪位泰坦在统治此地？我该如何找到它？

**Wandering Traveler:** ...

**Wandering Traveler:** ...

> **徘徊的游人：**……

**Wandering Traveler:** Hand of... Shadow...

> **徘徊的游人：**灰黯…之手……

**Anaxa:** Hand of Shadow, Thanatos?

> **那刻夏：**灰黯之手，塞纳托斯？

**Anaxa:** Hmm, could this... possibly be the nether realm?

> **那刻夏：**哼，莫非…这里是冥界？

**Wandering Traveler:** Not... nether realm...

> **徘徊的游人：**并非…冥界……

**Anaxa:** Then where is this? River of Souls? The outskirts of the nether realm? Are you heading downstream toward the nether realm?

> **那刻夏：**那这是哪里？冥界的外围，冥河？你们正顺流而下，向冥界去么？

**Wandering Traveler:** The nether realm... Nothing but an illusion...

> **徘徊的游人：**冥界…子虚乌有……

**Wandering Traveler:** Titan... Rejects... You... Me...

> **徘徊的游人：**泰坦…拒绝…你…我……

**Wandering Traveler:** Listen...

> **徘徊的游人：**听啊……

**Anaxa:** What?

> **那刻夏：**什么？

The traveler lifts their wrinkled fingers and remains silent.

I look in the direction they are pointing and listen closely. There, a massive shadow brought the sound of rushing tides.

**Anaxa:** The sound of tides...?

> **那刻夏：**潮水声…？

I can hear it.

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

I hear it, deep within the tides, beyond the closed gates of the void world...

A voice about to recount something.

**???:** Child of humanity...

> **？？？：**人子啊……

**???:** ...is it death's embrace you yearn for with such urgency?

> **？？？：**…汝竟这样急于加入死者的行列么？

**Anaxa:** ...!

> **那刻夏：**…！

**Cerces:** Oh, you have awoken. Welcome back to the mortal realm.

> **瑟希斯：**喔，醒了。欢迎回到凡间。

**Cerces:** Your consciousness is dissipating much faster than I expected... Ultimately, it seems like the Titan's Coreflame is incompatible with a mortal body.

> **瑟希斯：**汝之意识消散得比吾预想中快了些许…看来，泰坦的火种终究无法为凡胎相容哪。

**Anaxa:** How much longer can my consciousness remain in the mortal realm?

> **那刻夏：**我的意识，还能在人间停留多久？

**Cerces:** In my estimation, at best, you'll see fifteen more Entry Hours.

> **瑟希斯：**依吾所见，至多能见过今起第十五个门扉时吧？

**Anaxa:** Heh, fifteen days and nights...

> **那刻夏：**呵，十五个日夜啊……

**Cerces:** What is it? Are you finally starting to become attached to the mortal world now?

> **瑟希斯：**怎么，事到如今，终于留恋起尘世来了？

**Anaxa:** On the contrary, fifteen days feel a little too long for solving a singular problem.

> **那刻夏：**恰恰相反。就解明一道题而言，十五个日夜未免有些太长了。

**Cerces:** Hehe...

> **瑟希斯：**呵……

**Anaxa:** Stop giggling. It's time for us to go. The messenger from the Council of Elders is almost here.

> **那刻夏：**别傻笑了，走吧。元老院的使者差不多该到了。

**Cerces:** Let me ask you one more question, are you really planning to betray Aglaea's will?

> **瑟希斯：**吾再多嘴问一句：汝当真要背叛阿格莱雅的旨意不成？

**Anaxa:** What betrayal is there to speak of when I was never loyal to her to begin with?

> **那刻夏：**我从未对她忠诚过，谈何背叛？

**Cerces:** What about that white-haired student of yours? Acting so recklessly... Do you not care about what might happen to him?

> **瑟希斯：**汝那位白发的门生呢？如此妄为…就不怕陷其于不义？

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** There will be a price to pay for blind faith. This is also a lesson for Phainon.

> **那刻夏：**盲信总要付出代价，这也是留给白厄的一课。

### Meet with the Council of Elders's messenger

> *Now, the Coreflame of Cerces resides in your chest, which means that Aglaea will eventually extract it for the Flame-Chase Journey. It's easy to guess what will happen to you, who the Coreflame had revived. To survive, and to seek, you need to find a power that can protect you and combat Aglaea and others — The messenger from Council of Elders has been waiting for you for a long time.*

**Cerces:** You truly are a strict teacher.

> **瑟希斯：**…汝真是位严师哪。

**Anaxa:** Of course. This is how I've always been.

> **那刻夏：**当然，我向来如此。

*(Enter the Garden of Life)*

**???:** Sorry to keep you waiting, Lord Anaxagoras.

> **？？？：**阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下，久等了。

*(Approach Lygus)*

**???:** It's been a while, Lord Anaxagoras.

> **？？？：**许久不见，阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下。

**???:** I greet you on behalf of all the citizens of Okhema and express my deepest regret about what happened at the Grove of Epiphany.

> **？？？：**本人谨代表奥赫玛全体公民向你致意，也为神悟树庭的遭遇表示深切遗憾。

**Anaxa:** It's you? I never expected that you would personally come and meet me.

> **那刻夏：**是你？我没想到你会亲自出面接见。

**???:** By Kephale and Talanton above, I have come as a testament to the justice of the Council of Elders.

> **？？？：**刻法勒与塔兰顿在上，我为彰显元老院之公义而来。

**Cerces:** There are Antikytherans here? They have been a rare sight ever since the Chrysos War.

> **瑟希斯：**此地竟有安提基色拉人？黄金战争后可称得上是难得一见了。

**???:** I extend to you my utmost respect, revered Titan.

> **？？？：**也向您献上诚挚的问候，尊贵的泰坦。

**Lygus:** I am Lycurgus, though you may call me Lygus. Holding an honorary position within the Council of Elders in Okhema, and as the Theoros, I stand to protect the right of all upright citizens to express themselves freely.

> **来古士：**我名为吕枯耳戈斯，唤我「来古士」便可。现今是为奥赫玛元老院的名誉元老，以「神礼观众」之名，捍卫每一位正直的公民自我表达的权利。

**Anaxa:** Heh... You can see them, too?

> **那刻夏：**呵…你也能看见它？

**Lygus:** You know about us. We Antikytherans perceive the world through the amplitude and frequency of souls...

> **来古士：**您了解我：安提基色拉人是以灵魂的振幅和频率感知这个世界——

**Lygus:** ...Just as I am conscious that Lady Aglaea is secretly listening in on our private meeting from afar. If necessary, Lord Anaxagoras, I can sever the golden thread to protect your fundamental rights.

> **来古士：**——正如我明白，此刻阿格莱雅女士正在远方探听这场私人会谈。如果您需要，阁下，我可以掐断金线，捍卫您的基本权利。

**Anaxa:** The demigod should know better, for such is her station. Spare those poor threads, and just let her listen to my words. They shall serve only to unveil her own folly.

> **那刻夏：**那女人贵为半神，理应自重。就留着这些可怜的线头吧，让她好好听听我的声音，自取其辱。

**Lygus:** May I interpret this as you having a countermeasure?

> **来古士：**看来您已有对策了？

**Anaxa:** I need not any plan, because she can do nothing other than petty espionage. As the citizens' assembly approaches and with Oronyx's fall, any attempt to capture the Coreflame within me will only usher chaos and calamity — a truth she undoubtedly comprehends.

> **那刻夏：**我不需要任何对策。除了监视，她什么都做不了。公民大会即将召开，又有欧洛尼斯陨落在先，强夺我体内的这枚火种只会带来祸乱，她不会不清楚。

**Anaxa:** While I, Anaxagoras, serving as the Grove's special envoy to Okhema's citizens' assembly, have long known the extent of your abilities...

> **那刻夏：**而我，阿那克萨戈拉斯，身为树庭在奥赫玛公民大会的特派公使，也早已了解你的能耐……

**Anaxa:** Let's forego the formality of asserting authority, Lord Lygus. Lead the way.

> **那刻夏：**不必再下马作威了，来古士阁下。带路吧。

**Lygus:** Very well. Surely Lady Caenis would appreciate such assertiveness in an ally.

> **来古士：**甚好。相信凯妮斯阁下一定会对如此强势的盟友青眼相待。

### Follow Lygus and head to Dawncloud

> *The Council of Elders dispatched a messenger, as you anticipated, to escort you to Dawncloud before Aglaea could make her move. Here, you will be under the Elders' protection. There's still some time before the Messenger returns. Take a stroll around here, and let the curious Cerces, who resides in your mind, calm down a bit.*

**Lygus:** She's been waiting. Follow me, please.

> **来古士：**她已恭候多时，随我来吧。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Lygus:** Is there something on your mind, Lord Anaxagoras?

> **来古士：**怎么了，阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下？

**Anaxa:** Nothing really. It's just a question that I contemplate every time before I ascend Dawncloud: For the Titans, does the falling equate to death?

> **那刻夏：**没什么。只是每次登上黎明云崖前，我都会思考：对泰坦而言，「陨落」是否等同于「死亡」？

**Lygus:** A pondering truly deep. Time is on our side. Do you mind sharing your reflections with me?

> **来古士：**深刻的思考。时间还多，介意与我分享您的见解吗？

**Anaxa:** While all living beings must ultimately face their demise, Kephale has yet to meet theirs. Their journey continues unhurriedly along this long path.

> **那刻夏：**生者必灭。但刻法勒尚未抵达它的终点，而是在这条漫长的道路上缓缓迈进。

**Lygus:** Are you suggesting that Kephale may not have truly passed?

> **来古士：**您是想说，刻法勒并未真正死去？

**Anaxa:** Yes, for dying implies an ongoing process, an action, whereas death signifies a static state, a definitive termination.

> **那刻夏：**正是。「死」是一个动作，一个过程；「死亡」则是一种状态，一种终结。

**Anaxa:** In contrast to mortals, Titans exist on a grander scale of space and time, thus elongating their passage into death. The notion of their "fall" is but a misinterpretation born of mortal ignorance.

> **那刻夏：**相较凡人，泰坦在空间和时间的尺度上更为宏大，因而「死」的过程也更为漫长。所谓「陨落」，不过是凡人出于无知的曲解。

**Lygus:** An intriguing concept, albeit challenging to substantiate. After all, Kephale has maintained a profound silence ever since the Era Erasa, while scholars and priests alike have conveyed the message of the gods' imminent fall.

> **来古士：**有趣的见解，可惜难以证明或证伪。毕竟自幻灭世以来，刻法勒便不再言语。无论学者还是祭司，皆对尊神的陨落作出了宣言。

**Anaxa:** Perhaps the gods, in their arrogance, deemed it beneath them to reveal their perspectives to mortals.

> **那刻夏：**或许只是众神太过傲慢，认为他们没有沟通的资格。

**Lygus:** Could you elaborate on your statement?

> **来古士：**此话怎讲？

**Anaxa:** Cerces, the kin of the sky father as recounted in myths, now resides within my brain. Were I to inquire, Kephale would have no cause to refuse.

> **那刻夏：**瑟希斯，神话中天父的同袍，如今正寄居在我的脑子里。若由我来提问，刻法勒想必没有拒绝的理由。

**Cerces:** Oh...

> **瑟希斯：**喔……

**Lygus:** That could very well be true, but what if the Worldbearing Titan still chooses to remain silent?

> **来古士：**不无道理。可如果负世的尊神仍不应答呢？

**Anaxa:** Heh, in that case, the myths crafted by the people of Amphoreus are nothing more than pitiful jests.

> **那刻夏：**呵，那就说明翁法罗斯人编撰的神话，连笑话都不如。

**Lygus:** It's worth a try, I believe, and I'd love to witness the fruits of your theory and the change it may bring to this imperiled world.

> **来古士：**那不妨试试看吧。我也十分乐见您的理论开花结果，看它将为这濒毁的世界带来何种变革。

**Lygus:** Because I completely concur with you on this aspect: If the Titan has indeed met their end, any response from them would be impossible...

> **来古士：**因为，您有一点说得十分在理：倘若泰坦已至终结，它便绝无可能作出回应——

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Lygus:** Please, professor.

> **来古士：**阁下，请

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Cerces:** Heh... The children of humanity have indeed created a spectacle.

> **瑟希斯：**呵…诸位人子的创造可真是壮观哪。

**Lygus:** Welcome to Dawncloud. The atmosphere here carries a greater sense of solemnity than the mundane clamor of the Marmoreal Palace, with entry permitted only to the invited.

> **来古士：**欢迎来到「黎明云崖」。相较云石天宫的喧嚣凡俗，这里的一切都更为庄重，非受邀者不可往来。

**Lygus:** Only esteemed beings grace this place, and among them, I've received priests, scholars, warriors... And now, we even have the honor to be in the presence of a Titan. What a joyous occasion!

> **来古士：**我曾在此接待过祭司、学者、斗士，往来于此的皆是崇高之人。现如今，我们甚至迎来了一位泰坦，可喜可贺。

**Anaxa:** Hmph... Once deemed a blasphemer, now a surrogate for revered divinity. The honor should rightfully be mine, shouldn't it?

> **那刻夏：**哼…从渎神者摇身一变，成为崇高神性的替身，我倒该为此感到荣幸了。

**Lygus:** I'll notify Caenis and the others of your arrival. Kindly wait for a moment. A soldier will be here to guide you shortly.

> **来古士：**我将召集凯妮斯一派前来会晤，且请阁下稍事等待，随后会有传令士兵为您带路。

**Lygus:** In the meanwhile, you are welcome to explore the vicinity, but kindly remain within the guards' view. Delaying the crucial meeting is something none of us wish to see, I presume.

> **来古士：**期间，阁下可在附近随意游览。但还请不要离开卫兵视线，以免贻误紧要事机。

**Anaxa:** I'm well aware of Caenis' methods. There's no need to emphasize that.

> **那刻夏：**我清楚凯妮斯的做派，不用刻意强调。

**Lygus:** As you wish. Your cooperation is much appreciated.

> **来古士：**谨遵吩咐。感谢您的配合。

**Cerces:** Alas... I had hoped for a brilliant plan from you, but it turns out you just left one cage to enter another.

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀…本以为你有何奇谋，不成想是从一座牢笼进到另一座来了哪。

**Anaxa:** Save your concerns. I know very well what I'm doing.

> **那刻夏：**我自有把握，犯不着操多余的心。

**Cerces:** Hehe, I shall depart to explore the surroundings, then.

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵，那吾便去周围转上一遭好了。

**Cerces:** Now that I look around... I wonder if there are any murals bearing my portrait around here. I'm quite intrigued to see how the children of humanity beyond the Grove perceive another Titan.

> **瑟希斯：**四下望去…此地可有描绘吾的壁画？吾倒是好奇，树庭以外的人子，如何看待别尊泰坦呢。

**Anaxa:** Off you go. I won't be joining you.

> **那刻夏：**快去吧，恕不奉陪。

**Anaxa:** (This Titan appears to be quite at ease... I should investigate the Council of Elders' recent activities first.)

> **那刻夏：**（这泰坦倒是悠哉…先探探元老院最近的动向好了。）

**Cerces:** Be aware, I can hear you perfectly well from here.

> **瑟希斯：**吾可听得一清二楚喔。

**Anaxa:** Tsk...

> **那刻夏：**啧……

### Walk around before the Messenger arrives

*(Approach the Young Scribe)*

**Young Scribe:** Professor Anaxa...

> **年轻的书吏：**那刻夏老师——

**Young Scribe:** Over here...

> **年轻的书吏：**这边——

*(Talk to the Young Scribe)*

**Young Scribe:** Professor Anaxa? Word has it that the scholars of the Grove valiantly perished resisting the black tide. To find you here safe and sound... Thank the fates!

> **年轻的书吏：**那刻夏老师？听说树庭的学者为了抵御黑潮英勇牺牲，想不到您还安然无恙…真是命运垂青！

**Anaxa:** You could say it's the fates toying with us.

> **那刻夏：**倒不如说是命运弄人吧。

**Young Scribe:** Haha, it's only the Kremnoans and Aidonians who chase glory through death. But your survival is a victory in itself! I'd settle for that gladly!

> **年轻的书吏：**哈哈，只有悬锋人和哀地里亚人才会通过死亡追求荣耀，您能幸免于难就是好事，好事！

**Young Scribe:** So, are you here to report the incident to the Council? I heard that after the black tide devoured the Grove, Okhema would be next in line. Even with the demigod of Strife standing alongside humankind...

> **年轻的书吏：**这么说，您此行是来向元老院禀报事件始末的么？我听说，不仅树庭被黑潮吞没，奥赫玛也不远了。就算有「纷争」的半神站在人类这边……

**Anaxa:** Tell me, how much does the Council of Elders know about the black tide?

> **那刻夏：**告诉我，元老院对黑潮了解多少？

**Young Scribe:** Once Caenis and her followers learned of Cerces and Oronyx's fall, they proclaimed in the Council that the Flame-Chasers could no longer stand against the calamity alone...

> **年轻的书吏：**凯妮斯一派此前得知瑟希斯与欧洛尼斯先后陨落，便在元老院中大肆鼓吹逐火派已无力抵抗灾厄，独木难支……

**Young Scribe:** Yet, when the crown prince ascended to godhood and repelled the black tide, she immediately altered her tune, declaring that all was resolved beyond Okhema, with the only remaining menace now being Aglaea and her adherents. They might well make their move at the upcoming citizens' assembly.

> **年轻的书吏：**但等到那位王子登神，阻退黑潮，她又马上调转枪头，声称奥赫玛以外大势已定，唯一的威胁只剩阿格莱雅及其党羽。大概下一次公民大会，她们就该发难了吧。

**Anaxa:** No surprises there, those shifty opportunists... Thank you for your honesty, virtuous student.

> **那刻夏：**不出所料，见风使舵的家伙…感谢你坦诚相告，正直的学生。

**Cerces:** Hehe, being an opportunist could very well be a facet of wisdom...

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵，见风使舵也不失为一种辩论的智慧哪……

**Anaxa:** Shouldn't you be searching for murals of yourself? I've had enough of your prattling.

> **那刻夏：**你不是要去找自己的壁画么？别在我耳旁喋喋不休了。

**Cerces:** I was really curious to see how you would find the middle ground between both factions, so I came to listen in.

> **瑟希斯：**吾实在好奇，汝要如何在双方的矛头下委曲求全，便来旁听了。

**Cerces:** But it seems as though this Caenis is much more ruthless than Aglaea... If so, why do you want to work with her? Could it be that perhaps you've decided to readily surrender this hollow shell of yours just because you feel like death is fast approaching?

> **瑟希斯：**不过乍看来，这位凯妮斯比起阿格莱雅要狠毒得多呢…汝怎会想得寻她做靠山？莫非是觉着死兆将至，索性将这副躯壳拱手相让了？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, I'm afraid you'll be sorely disappointed if that's what you truly think.

> **那刻夏：**哼，那你恐怕要失望了。

*(Approach the Wizened Priest)*

**Wizened Priest:** Hmph, even blasphemous scholars can come here, it seems?

> **年迈的祭司：**哼，渎神的学者也能来到这里？

**Anaxa:** Looks like my presence here is quite unwelcome.

> **那刻夏：**看来我在这里不受欢迎呀。

*(Talk to the Wizened Priest)*

**Wizened Priest:** Hah, Anaxagoras? I thought that you had died to the black tide, just like the other scholars.

> **年迈的祭司：**呵，阿那克萨戈拉斯？我本以为你也跟着其他学者一道，死在那黑潮里了。

**Anaxa:** As Kephale's priest, are you not aware of the decorum of respecting the dead?

> **那刻夏：**身为刻法勒的祭司，竟不知道尊重死者的道理么？

**Wizened Priest:** Surely you jest! You blasphemous heretics are not worthy of respect from the supreme being.

> **年迈的祭司：**笑话！你们这群渎神的异端，配不得至高之神尊重。

**Wizened Priest:** Let me remind you that you're currently in Dawncloud, so you had better keep your deranged language to yourself. I am no longer that altar servant from years ago who could only acquiesce to the commands of others. Now, I have plenty of ways to deal with people like you.

> **年迈的祭司：**好心提醒你，此行在黎明云崖，收起那些谵妄之语吧。我已不是当年那位只能看人眼色的辅祭了，对付你有的是手段。

**Anaxa:** I'm not sure whether I should laugh or cry right now... I can't believe that a follower such as you could have been made by a Creation Titan.

> **那刻夏：**造化之神竟能创造出你这种信徒，真不知是该感到可笑还是可怜。

**Anaxa:** As I see it, a dead Titan can't provide you with this level of confidence, right? Then who is it, exactly? Could it be Caenis?

> **那刻夏：**依我看，已死的泰坦给不了你如此底气吧？那是谁，凯妮斯？

**Wizened Priest:** You...

> **年迈的祭司：**你……

**Wizened Priest:** Hmph... Seeing how fond of you Elder Caenis is, I shall let that slide for now. Leave immediately and don't impede us from receiving the supreme being's blessings.

> **年迈的祭司：**哼…看在凯妮斯元老青睐有加的份上，姑且放你一马。走吧，别再妨碍至高之神降福于我等。

**Anaxa:** (Seeing as we were able to hear of this matter from the mouths of others, it seems that Caenis is indeed quite sincere toward us. Perhaps this affair will proceed smoother than I had anticipated...)

> **那刻夏：**（能从旁人口中听到这件事，看来凯妮斯确实带着些诚意。事情或许比我想象得要顺利些……）

**Cerces:** Oh my, what a thrilling confrontation... As expected of one of Kephale's followers.

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀…好一场精彩的交锋，不愧是刻法勒的信徒。

**Anaxa:** ...Are you praising or insulting him?

> **那刻夏：**…这是褒义还是贬义？

**Cerces:** Praising him, of course. As a believer of the god of Deliverance, it is only natural that he detests evil.

> **瑟希斯：**是褒义喔。身为救世之神的信徒，自当是嫉恶如仇。

**Wizened Priest:** Didn't you hear me? I asked you to leave! Go as far away from here as possible! Don't mutter to yourself like some sort of madman and disturb my meditation!

> **年迈的祭司：**我说了，走，走远点！别像个疯人自言自语，妨碍我冥想！

### Listen to Cerces's evaluations of their murals

> *The Council of Elders dispatched a messenger, as you anticipated, to escort you to Dawncloud before Aglaea could make her move. Here, you will be under the Elders' protection. Will Cerces be curious about how they've been portrayed?*

**Anaxa:** (I've had enough of this... Could the 15th Entry Hour arrive any faster?)

> **那刻夏：**（真是受够了…第十五个门扉时能不能快点来？）

*(Approach Cerces)*

**Cerces:** Oh my! Could it be that the Great Tree painted in this mural... is actually depicting me?

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀！那壁画上的大树…莫非是吾？

*(Talk to Cerces)*

**Debating Scholar:** Every plant, every tendril, no, even every leaf could be the embodiment of Cerces...

> **辩论的学者们：**每一株植物、每一条枝蔓、每一片林叶都可能是瑟希斯的化身……

**Debating Scholar:** As a result, mere mortals who did not know what the Titan actually looked like chose to paint them as a giant tree instead. Their wisdom was portrayed as fruit to symbolize how they shared their knowledge equally among the masses... Such exquisiteness indeed!

> **辩论的学者们：**正因此，凡人无从得知它真实的模样，便将泰坦绘作巨树，智慧描摹成果实，以此象征它将知识平等分享给众人…实在是精妙绝伦啊！

**Cerces:** This mural is quite abstract.

> **瑟希斯：**这壁画，着实有些抽象了。

**Anaxa:** I really can't tell whether you are praising or demeaning yourself. Now I'm quite curious whether the Reason Titan has studied figures of speech before?

> **那刻夏：**真分不清你是在夸赞还是贬低自己。我很好奇，理性之泰坦学过修辞吗？

**Cerces:** Heheh, of course I find such stylistic devices interesting.

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵，哪里的话，吾当然是觉得有趣咯？

**Cerces:** For all we know, this could be the answer to the question, "What exactly are we?"

> **瑟希斯：**说不好，这就是「『我们』究竟为何物」的答案呢。

**Anaxa:** Hmph, I, for one, am not willing to admit that I could look this ugly.

> **那刻夏：**哼，我可不想承认自己是这种丑陋的模样。

**Cerces:** Heheh... Was this the reason why you failed in your attempt to refine my soul when you were ambushed at the Grove?

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵…难道说，汝是出于这种缘由，才在树庭遇袭时妄图炼化吾之灵魂不成？

**Anaxa:** Since you are aware of this, why did you save me back then?

> **那刻夏：**既然你这么清楚，当时又何必出手将我救下？

**Cerces:** From my perspective, this point alone was enough to separate you from the average person.

> **瑟希斯：**在吾看来，汝仅持这点便足以与凡夫划清界限哪。

**Anaxa:** ...How do two lunatics coexist in the same brain?

> **那刻夏：**哎…一颗脑袋里怎么会同时住着两个疯子？

**Cerces:** Therefore, it seems that your journey to Dawncloud this time is not just to seek protection...

> **瑟希斯：**所以，汝此行前来黎明云崖，莫不是仅为了寻求庇护吧……

**Cerces:** ...What, then, is the intention behind your actions?

> **瑟希斯：**…汝究竟意欲何为？

**Anaxa:** The entire plan is stored in my memory, so why don't you look there?

> **那刻夏：**全盘计划就在我的脑子里，你不妨自己找找看。

**Cerces:** Heheh, then don't mind if I do...

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵，那就却之不恭了……

**Cerces:** One year old, two years old...

> **瑟希斯：**一岁、两岁……

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Cerces:** Oh my, even a member of the preeminent Seven Sages must hug his dromas doll to sleep? How interesting...

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀，大名鼎鼎的七贤人竟非得抱着大地兽玩偶入睡哪？有趣……

**Anaxa:** ...That's enough.

> **那刻夏：**…够了。

**Cerces:** How unusual, your soul is actually trembling. This must mean that behind this toy hides a secret that nobody knows, right?

> **瑟希斯：**汝的灵魂在颤抖哪，少见。那玩具背后，想必有一段不为人知的秘辛吧？

**Anaxa:** Hmph... Since our days are both numbered, I don't mind telling you more about it.

> **那刻夏：**哼…反正你我都时日无多了，说说也无妨。

**Anaxa:** This is a present that my sister gave to me. It's a doll that was made to look like our family pet.

> **那刻夏：**那是姐姐给我的礼物，按照家里宠物的模样做的玩偶。

**Cerces:** So, there are other members in your family, eh? Are they also living in Okhema?

> **瑟希斯：**汝还有家人哪，他们也在这奥赫玛城中？

**Anaxa:** Don't you just love to pick through my brain? Go on, continue flipping through my memories.

> **那刻夏：**你不是爱看我的脑子么？继续往后翻。

**Anaxa:** It won't take long... You'll discover the answer when you reach the memories of when I was five.

> **那刻夏：**用不了多久…到五岁那年，你就知道答案了。

**Cerces:** The black tide is everywhere... I can't bear to look.

> **瑟希斯：**…遍地黑潮，不忍直视哪。

**Anaxa:** When I was young, I couldn't accept this outcome either. Georios, Aquila, Kephale... and you, of course. Although I prayed to every Titan that I could think of for help, my efforts were ultimately futile.

> **那刻夏：**年幼的我同样不能接受这个结果。吉奥里亚、艾格勒、刻法勒…当然，还有你。能求的泰坦我求了个遍，可惜，都无济于事。

**Cerces:** This... Could this be the inception of your research into alchemy?

> **瑟希斯：**这…莫非就是汝研究「炼金」的开端？

**Anaxa:** Indeed it was. Everything started from that verdant courtyard of yours. When I was pursuing my education there, I naturally also came to learn of Thalesus' theory as well.

> **那刻夏：**正是，一切始于你那座绿意盎然的庭院。我在那里求学的时候，自然也接触到了塞勒苏斯的理论。

**Cerces:** Oh, I remember that person. He was the one to come up with the concept of "souls," right?

> **瑟希斯：**喔，吾记得他。就是他首先提出了「灵魂」的概念，没错吧？

**Anaxa:** Not only that, he also believed that life, as well as the composition, movement, and transformation of matter, all stem from souls themselves. This is why I thought that...

> **那刻夏：**不仅如此，他还认为所有生命和物体的组成、运动和变化全都源自「灵魂」本身。于是，我想……

**Anaxa:** Since all living things stemmed from the same origin, why could I not sacrifice myself in order to resurrect my nearest and dearest?

> **那刻夏：**既然一切生命皆出于同一根源——为何我不能以自己为代价，让至亲复活呢？

*(Screen fades to black)*

**Empedocles:** Anaxa, you...

> **恩贝多克利斯：**那刻夏，你……

**Anaxa:** I did it, teacher.

> **那刻夏：**我成功了，老师。

**Empedocles:** B—But this is absolute blasphemy... Regardless of how much freedom the Grove provides, such an act could cause you to be burned at the pyre...!

> **恩贝多克利斯：**这、这可是渎神哪…即便自由如树庭，此举也足以将你送上火葬的尸床…！

**Empedocles:** Your beloved... Where is she now? Send her away from the Grove at once, to a place where nobody will ever be able to find her. Otherwise, the both of us will be judged by the Council of the Seven Sages...!

> **恩贝多克利斯：**你那至亲…她现在身在何处？快将她送离树庭，去到人寻不得的地方，否则你我都将接受七贤人会的审判哪…！

**Anaxa:** Calm down, teacher. My sister, she...

> **那刻夏：**放心吧，老师。我的姐姐，她……

**Anaxa:** She's still not in the mortal realm... even to this day.

> **那刻夏：**如今…依旧不在人世啊。

**Empedocles:** What do you mean...?

> **恩贝多克利斯：**这是什么意思…？

**Anaxa:** It was naive of me to think that I could snatch the deceased from Thanatos' grip by merely sacrificing one eye...

> **那刻夏：**单凭一只眼睛为代价，就想从塞纳托斯手中夺回逝者，还是太过天真了……

**Anaxa:** Equivalent exchange... All I received for that price was but an opportunity to see her for one final time. Nothing more.

> **那刻夏：**等价交换…我所能得到的，不过见她最后一面，仅此而已。

**Empedocles:** ...

> **恩贝多克利斯：**唉……

**Empedocles:** Let it go, Anaxa. As one of my best students, I cannot watch you go down the wrong path without doing anything to stop it.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**收手吧，那刻夏。你是我最好的学生，我不能眼睁睁看着你误入歧途。

**Empedocles:** Never do something taboo ever again. Don't disappoint the deceased and never harm yourself. If you can do that, this classroom will still be yours and you'll have a chance to live out your own life.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**永远别再触碰禁忌：别再辜负逝者，别再伤害自己。这样，这座教室还能属于你，你还有机会活出自己的人生。

**Anaxa:** Since I was able to see her one last time, I have no other desires...

> **那刻夏：**既然见过她最后一面，我已别无所求……

**Anaxa:** You're right, teacher. It's time for me to start a new life.

> **那刻夏：**你说得对，老师。我也该开始新的人生了。

**Empedocles:** I am sincerely glad for you, child. Now then, what topic would you like to study?

> **恩贝多克利斯：**我由衷为你感到欣慰，孩子。接下来，你想研究怎样的课题？

**Anaxa:** I wish to continue Thalesus' research.

> **那刻夏：**我想继续塞勒苏斯的研究。

**Empedocles:** Continue the research into souls? But you know that the Grove has long since understood his philosophy like the back of their hands. Even if you continue digging into this subject, your efforts will likely be futile...

> **恩贝多克利斯：**继续「灵魂」的研究？可你知道，树庭早已对他的思想明如指掌，即便继续深挖，恐怕也是徒劳……

**Anaxa:** No, teacher. After my fleeting glimpse of the deathly fog, I finally understood a single truth: Since ancient days, our understanding of souls is but the tip of the iceberg.

> **那刻夏：**不，老师。我于死雾中匆匆一瞥后，终于晓得一个道理：从古至今，我们对「灵魂」的理解不过冰山一角。

**Anaxa:** I may have lost an eye, but my view of the world has become clearer than ever...

> **那刻夏：**我失去了一只眼睛，视野却比任何时候都更加明朗……

*(Screen fades to black)*

**Anaxa:** I have grasped the truth with my bare hands... Watch as I turn this farcical world upside down.

> **那刻夏：**真理已尽在我手…看我如何令这个有悖常理的世界天翻地覆吧。

**Cerces:** Hmm... Who would have thought such a secret lay concealed beneath that hefty eye patch of yours?

> **瑟希斯：**喔…汝那厚重的眼罩下，竟掩藏着如此秘密。

**Cerces:** However, this story doesn't seem to be connected to your plan... In fact, I'd say that they aren't related at all?

> **瑟希斯：**不过，这个故事与汝所盘算的不能说瓜葛相连…只能说毫无干系哪？

**Anaxa:** The answer lies within my tale. If even you cannot understand it, perhaps you should relinquish the divinity of Reason to me? After all, it'd be a waste for you to hold onto it.

> **那刻夏：**答案就在其中。如果你连这都想不明白，不妨把「理性」的权柄让渡给我吧，留着也是浪费。

**Anaxa:** My story ends here. Now, it's time for you to tell me your secret. After all, this is what equivalent exchange means, correct?

> **那刻夏：**我的故事就到这里，该说说你的秘密了。正所谓「等价交换」，对吧？

**Cerces:** As expected, you laid a trap for me to step into...

> **瑟希斯：**果不其然，为吾留了陷阱哪……

**Cerces:** Unfortunately, even if I was willing to talk about my past, I may not know more about it than you do.

> **瑟希斯：**可惜…即便吾有意述说自己的过去种种，可吾所知者，未必比汝等更多呢。

**Anaxa:** Oh? Are you saying that the renowned Reason Titan is now admitting a lack of wisdom? Your followers are going to be sorely disappointed by this.

> **那刻夏：**哦？大名鼎鼎的理性之泰坦承认自己不够智慧，你这帮信徒可要失望了。

**Cerces:** Child of humanity, remember this: Being cognizant of one's ignorance is the greatest wisdom of all.

> **瑟希斯：**人子啊，记住了：知晓自身的无知便是最大的睿智。

**Anaxa:** Now I understand. No wonder you asked me to answer your random, nonsensical questions.

> **那刻夏：**难怪你要拜托我来解答你那没头没脑的问题。

**Cerces:** Not only that, you're the only one who can shed light on my query. As the saying goes, onlookers see most clearly.

> **瑟希斯：**不仅如此，是惟有汝能解答吾之所惑——正所谓「旁观者清」嘛。

**Anaxa:** Hmph, is that so...

> **那刻夏：**哼，「旁观者清」么……

**Military Runner:** Lord Anaxa...!

> **传令士兵：**那刻夏阁下——！

**Anaxa:** Hmm...?

> **那刻夏：**嗯…？

**Military Runner:** I saw you muttering to yourself and was wondering whether you were caught in a delirium of reflective thought? I'm sorry to interrupt you, but time is of the essence...

> **传令士兵：**看您刚刚在自言自语，是正在进行让自我陷入迷狂的思考吧？抱歉打断您，但时间紧迫……

**Military Runner:** I have a message from Lord Lygus: Lady Caenis has met up with the Council of Elders and is awaiting your arrival, so please depart forthwith.

> **传令士兵：**在下奉来古士大人之命前来通报：凯妮斯大人已莅临元老院，敬候阁下光临，还请即刻移驾。

**Anaxa:** Hmph... It's finally time.

> **那刻夏：**哼…可算是来了。

**Cerces:** Kephale, the one who created the world, and the one who bears the world...

> **瑟希斯：**刻法勒，创世亦是负世之神啊……

**Cerces:** If you truly have not met your end... how will you answer the challenges of those who defy you?

> **瑟希斯：**倘若汝果真未曾迎来终结…又将如何回应悖逆之人的诘问呢？

### Follow the Messenger and meet with Elder Caenis

> *The Council of Elders dispatched a messenger, as you anticipated, to escort you to Dawncloud before Aglaea could make her move. Here, you will be under the Elders' protection. They await your arrival. Follow the Messenger to meet them.*

**Military Runner:** I didn't expect you to agree to Lady Caenis' invitation... What a wonderful turn of events.

> **传令士兵：**没想到您还是应了凯妮斯大人的邀请…真是太好了。

**Military Runner:** To tell you the truth, there are many people who hold grudges against that Lady Goldweaver...

> **传令士兵：**说实话，对那位「金织」大人心怀怨言的人可不少……

### Cross through Janus' Hidden Passage

*(Approach the Hidden Passage)*

**Mocking Scholar:** Isn't that the "dromas draped in finery"?

> **揶揄的学者：**那不是「穿着华服的大地兽」吗？

**Pious Scholar:** ...Avoid his gaze. I don't acquaint myself with blasphemers.

> **虔诚的学者：**…别对上眼了，我可不认识渎神者。

**Mocking Scholar:** Don't worry, he's too full of pride to pay us any attention.

> **揶揄的学者：**你放心，人家心高气傲，瞧不上咱们。

**Cerces:** Your nickname is "dromas" as well? You must really like those creatures.

> **瑟希斯：**连外号也是大地兽？汝是真的喜欢大地兽。

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

### Follow the Messenger and meet with Elder Caenis

*(After traveling through the Hidden Passage)*

**Stern Councilor:** A student of Empedocles? I hear that his views run counter to the Venerationists.

> **严厉的议员：**恩贝多克利斯的学生？我听说他的观点和「敬拜学派」截然相反。

**Amiable Councilor:** This is what being a scholar is about: A collision of ideas only occurs when there are differences in beliefs, and it is such collisions that lead to progress.

> **和蔼的议员：**学者是这样的，有不同才会有碰撞，有碰撞才会有进步。

**Stern Councilor:** Yet in my view, there are only right and wrong perspectives, neither of which can coexist with the other.

> **严厉的议员：**但在我看来，只有正确的观点和错误的观点，二者不可共存。

**Cerces:** What radical thinking... I guess this is why the children of humanity always squabble with each other.

> **瑟希斯：**非黑即白，看来这就是人子们争来争去的原因。

**Anaxa:** How boring.

> **那刻夏：**没意思。

**Cerces:** You seem to be avoiding that "Goldweaver" person. Why don't you address her face to face?

> **瑟希斯：**汝与那位「金织」互不对付，何不当面谈谈呢？

**Anaxa:** I have nothing to talk about with her... Since you're in my brain, you can look for the reason yourself.

> **那刻夏：**我跟她没什么好谈的…你在我脑子里，大可自己检索。

**Cerces:** Oh... The leader of the Flame-Chase Journey and a demigod of Mnestia...

> **瑟希斯：**哦，逐火之旅的领袖，墨涅塔的半神……

**Anaxa:** You're really searching through my brain? What are you treating it as, some sort of toy?

> **那刻夏：**你还真检索啊，把我的脑子当成什么了？

**Cerces:** Regardless, you think quite highly of her, no?

> **瑟希斯：**尽管如此，汝对她的评价并不低，对吧？

**Anaxa:** That's because I'm a teacher who grades everyone fairly.

> **那刻夏：**毕竟我是个打分公允的老师。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Military Runner:** Lord Lygus and Lady Caenis are just ahead.

> **传令士兵：**来古士阁下，凯妮斯阁下，就在前面。

**Military Runner:** I'll take my leave for now.

> **传令士兵：**我先行告退了。

*(Approach Lygus)*

**Lygus:** Welcome to the Demigod Council, esteemed guests.

> **来古士：**诸位，欢迎来到「半神议院」。

*(Approach Caenis)*

**Caenis:** Lord Anaxagoras! I've heard so much about you...

> **凯妮斯：**阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下！久仰大名——

**Caenis:** Head of the Nousporists of the Grove of Epiphany, notorious blasphemer, a Chrysos Heir who bears Cerces' Coreflame, a survivor of the Grove calamity...

> **凯妮斯：**神悟树庭「智种学派」掌门人，恶名昭彰的「渎神者」，身怀瑟希斯火种的黄金裔，树庭惨案的幸存者……

**Caenis:** Did I miss anything? If so, please be so kind as to enlighten me.

> **凯妮斯：**不知是否有所遗漏？若有，还望阁下不吝赐教，补阙拾遗。

**Anaxa:** Since Lady Caenis is already well acquainted with me, I won't waste any effort introducing myself again.

> **那刻夏：**看来凯妮斯阁下已对我相当了解，倒也免了自我介绍的工夫。

**Anaxa:** But then again, perhaps the customs are different here? Back in my hometown, there is a form of etiquette known as "honoring guests": The host will welcome their guests with warm hospitality by treating them to fine wine and a cozy bed...

> **那刻夏：**不过，或许是风俗不同？在我的家乡，有一种名为「主宾」的礼仪：主人待客应热情盈怀，赐以美酒清水、温床安睡……

**Anaxa:** And yet, ever since I arrived, I have not been welcomed with warm courtesy or ceremonial gifts. Instead, I am met with intense scrutiny. Even more absurd, the host has sharpened their blade and instructed their servants to lie in wait in the shadows...

> **那刻夏：**可我此番前来，不见主人殷勤献礼，反倒遭了质问。更荒唐的是，这位主人还磨好了屠刀，教侍从在暗处候着——

**Anaxa:** Perhaps you intend to cut open my heart, retrieve the Coreflame, and then hold a grand feast for all of the guests?

> **那刻夏：**阁下是要剖开我的心腹，取出火种，再为来宾大摆筵席不成？

**Caenis:** Everyone, stand down.

> **凯妮斯：**各位，退下吧。

**Anaxa:** Thank you for understanding. I am but a feeble scholar who is not used to the stench of avarice and blood.

> **那刻夏：**感谢理解。我不过一介孱弱书生，闻不惯铜臭和鲜血的味道。

**Caenis:** No need to be humble, my lord. From my perspective, your courage and gumption are worthy of the reputation of the Seven Sages. Your bravery is more than adequate to accomplish our perilous endeavor.

> **凯妮斯：**不必自谦，阁下。在我看来，您的胆识和魄力不愧于七贤人之名，足以胜任我等蹈锋饮血的事业。

**Anaxa:** So, you wish to start a war?

> **那刻夏：**你想要一场战争？

**Caenis:** Well, since the Chrysos Heirs are calling for war, we will respond in kind. Conflict is inevitable. Since Aglaea endeavors to suppress the virtuous commoners with blood of gold, we will not hesitate to display the rights and mettle that the citizens of Okhema are so entitled to.

> **凯妮斯：**黄金裔们呼唤战争，我们就回应以战争。冲突必将发生：既然阿格莱雅妄图用金血的力量压制善良的民众，我们就不惮彰显奥赫玛公民应有的权利和气概。

**Caenis:** As to your question of whether peace is still a viable option? Well, to that, I can only say this: Everything hinges on the next citizens' assembly and whether they have a sudden epiphany amid the clamor of the masses...

> **凯妮斯：**你问我和平的希望是否还有一丝尚存？我只能说：一切取决于下一场公民大会，他们能否在人民的呼声中幡然醒悟——

**Caenis:** ...And since you, as a Chrysos Heir, accepted our invitation, you have indeed given us a glimmer of hope.

> **凯妮斯：**——而身为黄金裔的您应邀前来，实为我们增添了希望的曙光。

**Anaxa:** Hah, what brazen arrogance. What you're saying right now is worlds apart from what you were saying before you retired your "cleaners."

> **那刻夏：**呵，大言不惭。方才退下的、你忠心耿耿的「清洗者」们听到的说辞，恐怕和这番话截然不同吧。

**Caenis:** I don't deny it. After all, they're responsible for cleaning up any threats and just a moment ago, you were on the side of the Chrysos Heirs...

> **凯妮斯：**实不相瞒，正是。毕竟他们的工作是清理害物。而就在刚才，您还站在黄金裔那边……

**Caenis:** ...But now that you're an ally of ours, we can continue talking somewhere else. As you know, these walls have ears.

> **凯妮斯：**…当然，现在您是这边的一员了。请，为免隔墙有耳，我们换个地方继续。

### Keep following Caenis

> *The Council of Elders dispatched a messenger, as you anticipated, to escort you to Dawncloud before Aglaea could make her move. Here, you will be under the Elders' protection. Caenis is like a cunning snake. Gaining her trust will not be easy.*

**Caenis:** I was fortunate enough to have the opportunity to discuss a treatise about the Venerationists with your teacher, Empedocles.

> **凯妮斯：**我曾有幸和你的老师恩贝多克利斯一同探讨「敬拜学派」的文章。

**Caenis:** Yet, it seems you subscribe to the Nousporists instead, Lord Anaxa? I've heard that this school of thought is one with many detractors.

> **凯妮斯：**但那刻夏阁下属于「智种学派」？我听闻那是反对者众多的一派。

**Anaxa:** It's normal for ideas to have supporters and dissenters, just like how there are two types of people in this world — geniuses and dimwits.

> **那刻夏：**有支持和反对很正常，就像世间也分贤才和笨货。

### Speak with Caenis

**Caenis:** Welcome back to the hub of the Demigod Council, Lord Anaxagoras.

> **凯妮斯：**欢迎回到「半神议院」中心，阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下。

**Caenis:** Surely you remember that countless eloquent orators have stood in this spot before and emerged victorious by stoking public sentiment with their words. And yet, there have also been innumerable blasphemous philosophers who set themselves up for failure because they refused to defend their actions.

> **凯妮斯：**相信您一定记得：这里站立过无数口若悬河的雄辩家，以煽惑人心的语言，奏凯而归；也站立过无数渎神的哲人，因拒绝为自己申辩，饮鸩赴死。

**Caenis:** This tiny stage is the most profound embodiment of Okhema in centuries. In fifteen days, this site shall also become the most important, as well as the final turning point in Okhema's history...

> **凯妮斯：**这片小小的舞台，正是奥赫玛千百年来最深刻的缩影。而在十五个日夜后，此地也将成为奥赫玛历史最重要、也是最后的转折点……

**Caenis:** May the both of us carry out our duties with honor and lead the masses back toward the Era Chrysea, a golden age with no Death, Strife, or Trickery.

> **凯妮斯：**但愿你我皆能不辱使命，带领世人重返那没有「死亡」、「纷争」和「诡计」的黄金治世。

**Cerces:** Oh my, fifteen days and nights... that aligns perfectly with the date of your demise. Could such a coincidence truly exist?

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀，十五个日夜…那正是汝的死期呢，天下竟有这样巧的事么？

**Anaxa:** Using death to prove myself fits my intentions perfectly.

> **那刻夏：**以死证道，正合我意。

**Caenis:** What strong conviction you have! As expected of the hero who dared to stand up to the Goldweaver.

> **凯妮斯：**多么掷地有声！不愧是敢与那位「金织」分庭抗礼的英雄。

**Cerces:** Hehe... As expected, she misunderstands you.

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵…她果然误会了。

**Anaxa:** That's enough, Lady Caenis. Dispense with the niceties. I only accepted your invitation so that I can serve the commoners alongside the members of the Council of Elders. Don't test my patience with such superfluous formalities.

> **那刻夏：**行了，凯妮斯阁下，免去客套话吧。我是为与元老院诸君共济世业才应邀前来，别用这些繁文缛节测试我的耐心。

**Caenis:** You say that now, but surely you also require my aid as well, so that you can stay alive, even when being pursued by the golden thread?

> **凯妮斯：**话是这么说，阁下也需得我出手助力，才能在金线追缉下保全性命吧？

**Anaxa:** You worry too much. She won't show her hand too freely, as she still needs to garner support from the masses at the citizens' assembly.

> **那刻夏：**你多虑了。她还需要在公民大会上争取人民支持，定然不会随意出手。

**Caenis:** Even someone like me can tell that only a sliver of her humanity remains. Surely you're not asserting that she will definitely act in a rational manner?

> **凯妮斯：**连我都看得出她的人性所剩无几。难道阁下就能断言，她必定会按常理出牌？

**Anaxa:** As you're no doubt aware, Aglaea covets the Coreflame that I possess, which makes it the bargaining chip that allows me and the rest of you to negotiate with her. However, in return, I'll need the Council of Elders to support me as much as possible. Naturally, this includes the guards as well.

> **那刻夏：**诚如你所见，阿格莱雅觊觎我怀中的火种。这是我、也是各位同她周旋的资本，但相应地，我也需要元老院提供包括护卫在内的一切支持。

**Anaxa:** You're well aware how valuable Cerces' Coreflame will be in the Era Chrysea that you are desperately pursuing...

> **那刻夏：**瑟希斯的火种，它在你苦苦追求的黄金世中有多少分量，你比我更清楚……

**Anaxa:** Talk is cheap, so you'd better act prudently as well.

> **那刻夏：**多说无用，好自为之。

**Caenis:** I understand. After all, this battle of wits is just a means for each party to acquire what they need. As long as your request is reasonable, the Council of Elders never shortchanges its allies. Since we're both intent on mutual cooperation, let's not mince words and just speak plainly to each other...

**Caenis:** Let us decide how much we can secure for ourselves and how much we're willing to sacrifice, like true philosophers — with our words and wisdom.

> **凯妮斯：**当然，我明白。所谓博弈不过各取所需，只要诉求合理，元老院向来不会亏待他的盟友。既然你我都有合作的意愿，不妨打开天窗说亮话——

- ▸ Let's begin, then.

(Continues to next step)

- ▸ I need some more time.

**Caenis:** If you need to organize your thoughts, please go ahead. But please don't go too far away, lest... complications arise.

> **凯妮斯：**让我们像真正的哲学家那样，通过话语和智慧决定你我究竟能为自己争取多少，又能为之牺牲多少吧。

(Exits dialogue)

*(Talk to Caenis again after exiting dialogue)*

**Caenis:** Let us decide how much we can secure for ourselves and how much we're willing to sacrifice, like true philosophers: with our words and wisdom. Are you ready, my lord?

> **凯妮斯：**若要厘清思绪，请自便。不过，切勿离开太远，以免…节外生枝。

- ▸ Let's begin, then.

(Continues to next step)

- ▸ I need some more time.

**Caenis:** If you need to organize your thoughts, please go ahead. But please don't go too far away, lest... complications arise.

> **凯妮斯：**让我们像真正的哲学家那样，通过话语和智慧决定你我究竟能为自己争取多少，又能为之牺牲多少吧——准备好了吗，阁下？

(Exits dialogue)

### Speak with Caenis and form an alliance

**Caenis:** Continuing from the earlier topic, let us discuss our common enemy: Aglaea.

> **凯妮斯：**就延续方才的话题，让我们聊聊那位共同的敌人，阿格莱雅吧。

**Caenis:** She is the second-most experienced Chrysos Heir across the whole of Okhema, who has gradually lost her humanity to the ravages of time and the ablation of the Coreflame... A formidable opponent indeed.

> **凯妮斯：**她是奥赫玛众黄金裔中资历次长者，在岁月磋磨与火种烧蚀中逐渐丧失人性…是一位可敬可畏的对手。

**Caenis:** Admittedly, there is ample reason for her to take your life. Yet, your previous reasoning is quite sound. As a seasoned political animal, it's unclear whether she will actually cross that irreversible boundary. Moreover, she still needs you alive so that the Titan's trial can be completed...

> **凯妮斯：**诚然，她有充分的理由对你痛下杀手，可阁下所说也不无道理：身为老练的政治动物，她是否真会跨越那道底线还犹未可知，况且她还需要您来完成泰坦的试炼……

**Caenis:** Is there anyone present who can swear by the sacred vow of Kephale and guarantee that you weren't in league with that woman before coming here? Are you just trying to pull the wool over our eyes and aid her in her savagery?

> **凯妮斯：**在场众人里又有谁能向刻法勒誓约封证，您此次赴约前没有和那女人沆瀣一气，只为掩人耳目，助长她的暴行？

- ▸ She is prepared to execute me.

**Anaxa:** I believe you're aware of Castorice the executioner. Long before I returned to Okhema, Aglaea had already dispatched her to monitor me. If I weren't able to reach this place in time, I would have been forced to meet Thanatos first.

> **那刻夏：**执刑者遐蝶，相信你对这个名字并不陌生。早在我返回奥赫玛时，阿格莱雅便派她来监视我了。如果我没能及时赴约，塞纳托斯必将先一步找上我。

**Anaxa:** As compared to a comprehensive plan with uncertain results, if you decide to cut open my chest and remove the Coreflame, wouldn't that settle her nerves even more? Everything is in place — she just needs the right timing.

> **那刻夏：**比起结果未知的「周全之法」，直接剖开我的胸膛取出火种，岂不省得她夜长梦多？万事早已俱备，她只需要一个合适的时机。

- ▸ She just needs vassals who will do her bidding without question.

**Anaxa:** If Aglaea required a suitably intelligent Chrysos Heir, then I'm the best fit for her needs. However, it's quite evident that she has no need for a Chrysos Heir who could break free of her control at any time.

> **那刻夏：**若阿格莱雅需要一位足够智慧的黄金裔，那非我莫属。但显而易见，她不需要一位随时都有可能脱离控制的黄金裔。

**Anaxa:** Since I believe in neither Titans nor the prophecy, there's no way that I would willingly walk on the path that she extols. Given how rigorously she enforces her principles, how would she ever accept a heretic such as me?

> **那刻夏：**我不信泰坦，不信神谕，更不会心甘情愿走在她宣扬的道路上。她的眼中向来揉不得沙子，又岂会容下我这个异端？

**Caenis:** Hah... I understand what you mean.

> **凯妮斯：**呵…阁下的意见，我明白了。

**Caenis:** At the very least, the both of us agree that this tyrant who wallows in extravagance and greed is the greatest threat to achieving peace in Okhema. The Council of Elders is willing to defend your right to live, just as we defend every oppressed citizen.

> **凯妮斯：**至少，你我都同意这位穷奢极欲的暴君正是奥赫玛和平最大的敌人。元老院愿意捍卫您生存的权利，正如我们捍卫每一位遭到压迫的公民。

**Caenis:** However, I regret to say that sentiments in favor of the Chrysos Heirs and Aglaea are only growing in magnitude. The peace that we are aspiring toward, as well as everything that we hold dear, will be destroyed completely. The city will be burned to ashes and the citizens' blood will be shed...

> **凯妮斯：**然而，遗憾的是，支持黄金裔、支持阿格莱雅的思潮依旧水涨船高。我们希冀的和平，以及所热爱的一切，都将化作城市的灰烬、公民的鲜血……

**Caenis:** Tell me, what are you willing to do for our esteemed citizens so that they can vanquish the enemies of our fine city?

> **凯妮斯：**告诉我，阁下：你能为我们高贵的公民奉献多少力量，以便他们击溃城邦的仇敌？

- ▸ Garnering my support is equivalent to garnering the support of the entire Grove.

**Anaxa:** Although the circumstances are rather unfortunate, from an objective point of view, it is an undeniable fact that ever since the Grove was attacked, I have become the de facto leader of the Grove.

> **那刻夏：**虽然令人感到遗憾，但客观来说，树庭遭袭之后，学院的实际权力者变成了我。

**Anaxa:** The Council of Elders needs more backing if it wishes to topple Aglaea and it's evident that the academy wields considerable influence.

> **那刻夏：**要想扳倒阿格莱雅，元老院需要支持，而整个学院的分量显然足够强而有力。

**Anaxa:** At the very least, I have served as the Grove's special envoy to Okhema for a significant amount of time. Of course, this also means that I will hold a crucial vote at the citizens' assembly.

> **那刻夏：**退一步说，我早就是树庭在奥赫玛的特派公使，在公民大会上自然也保有关键的一票。

- ▸ I will be the one to shatter Aglaea's self-righteousness.

**Anaxa:** Aglaea's authoritarianism, conceit, and arrogance have granted her unfettered control for the most part. Yet, these traits are also her greatest weakness. If I can render her helpless by forcing her to experience the agonizing pain of betrayal, I am certain she will never recover from such a setback.

> **那刻夏：**阿格莱雅专制、自负、傲慢，这让她在绝大多数时间里主宰一切，却也是她的软肋。只要令其经历惨痛的背叛而无能为力，就能使她一蹶不振。

**Caenis:** Heh, surely you don't believe that such a tiny betrayal will be enough to push her to her wits' end?

> **凯妮斯：**呵，阁下是觉得，这场小小的背叛足够让她无计可施么？

**Anaxa:** What's more, there are many who remain silent, merely watching to see how much chaos I can unleash... Not to mention, you know full well that the Chrysos Heirs have never been a united front.

> **那刻夏：**不仅如此，还有太多默不作声的人，正在观望我能掀起多大的风浪…而黄金裔们也从来不是铁板一块，你心里清楚。

**Caenis:** In the name of Kephale, Okhema thanks you for your sincere and valuable thoughts.

> **凯妮斯：**以刻法勒的名义，奥赫玛感谢您的诚意和宝贵意见。

**Caenis:** At this stage, it's clear that you and I understand the benefits to be won from this discussion. Let me put it plainly: The Council of Elders has always been renowned for its fairness, and that fairness is built on a foundation of sincerity...

> **凯妮斯：**话已至此，你我想必已对能在这场辩论中攫取何种利益心中有数了。就让我这么说吧：元老院向来以公正闻名，而公正建立在真诚的基础上……

**Caenis:** The Council of Elders has vowed to all citizens of Okhema that it will always remain loyal to them. As for you, my lord, how will you guarantee that you will be able to live up to such earnestness and will not betray Okhema's indomitable citizens when the opportunity presents itself, just as you now betray Aglaea when it suits you?

> **凯妮斯：**元老院宣誓对奥赫玛全体公民永远忠诚——至于你，阁下，你要如何保证自己配得上这份诚意，而不会像你现在背叛阿格莱雅一样，择机背叛奥赫玛战无不胜的人民？

- ▸ How absurd. My loyalty is unquestionable.

**Anaxa:** Hmph, surely you jest. My loyalty does not need to be proven. The fact that the Council of Elders is asking for proof of my loyalty is itself akin to a form of betrayal.

> **那刻夏：**哼，可笑。我的忠诚无需证明，但元老院要求证明忠诚本身已经无异于背叛。

**Caenis:** Huh...

> **凯妮斯：**呵……

**Lygus:** I am incredibly honored to have witnessed the both of you bury the hatchet in a civilized manner. However, I'm afraid that I must interrupt for a brief moment...

> **来古士：**能够看到两位以文明的方式握手言和，是我莫大的荣幸。然而，请容我稍事打断——

**Lygus:** As the Theoros, I have observed the following: Lady Aglaea, the Goldweaver, is currently on her way to Dawncloud.

> **来古士：**以「神礼观众」之名，我见到：「金织」阿格莱雅阁下正动身前来黎明云崖。

**Caenis:** Oh? Well, I do believe that we might have a slight overabundance of guests today.

> **凯妮斯：**哦？我们今天的客人似乎有些过多了。

**Anaxa:** Feeling dissatisfied, Lady Caenis? Despite the Council of Elders' lofty residence on Dawncloud, it must still tolerate the whims of the Chrysos Heirs.

> **那刻夏：**不甘心吧，凯妮斯阁下？元老院高居云崖，却仍要仰头看黄金裔的脸色。

**Caenis:** Heh, so you were holding back just now? Seems like you are better at playing this game than I. In any case, I am glad that she's arriving here...

> **凯妮斯：**呵，没想到阁下还是留了一手，是我棋差一着。不过，我个人倒是十分乐见她的出现……

**Caenis:** So, tell me, Anaxagoras: Now that you're an ally of mine, if Aglaea were to demand that I hand you over, what should I do?

> **凯妮斯：**告诉我吧，阿那克萨戈拉斯：如今你已身为盟友，倘若阿格莱雅来问我要人，我该怎么做呢？

**Anaxa:** You can do as she says. Tell her that the Council of Elders merely wished to chat with me and had no intention of offending her.

> **那刻夏：**你可以把我交出去，告诉她元老院只想找我聊聊天，并无任何冒犯之意。

**Anaxa:** However, by doing so, the authority of the Council of Elders will be as thin as papyrus that has been soaked in blood. Not only can it be destroyed at the slightest touch, but the Council will also lose possession of a valuable Coreflame...

> **那刻夏：**不过，如此一来，元老院的威严成了一张浸血的莎草纸，一戳就破，还痛失了一枚宝贵的火种……

**Anaxa:** No, more like two Coreflames. After all...

> **那刻夏：**不，应该是两枚。毕竟……

**Anaxa:** At the next citizens' assembly, that young lady who is synonymous with death will also be standing on our side.

> **那刻夏：**在下一次公民大会上，那位与死亡如影随形的少女也会站在我们这边。

**Caenis:** Oh? How can you be sure of that?

> **凯妮斯：**哦？你怎能如此断言？

**Anaxa:** Is that of importance to you? All you need to know is that I have a plan. In my opinion, you should be more worried about the impending threat.

> **那刻夏：**这很重要吗？我有的是办法。依我看，你还是多关心关心迫在眉睫的威胁吧。

**Anaxa:** If Aglaea can seize me in full view of the masses today, she could also dismiss the entire Council of Elders in one fell swoop tomorrow. If that happens, the discussion that we've just had will turn into an utter joke instead...

> **那刻夏：**阿格莱雅，今天她能在众目睽睽之下把我带走，明天她就能大手一挥，彻底废黜元老院，而我们方才议论的种种也都成了笑话——

**Anaxa:** Is this the outcome that you wish to see, Lady Caenis?

> **那刻夏：**这是你希望看到的吗，凯妮斯阁下？

**Caenis:** Fine, I will handle this matter appropriately.

> **凯妮斯：**呵呵…明白了。我会妥善处理此事。

**Caenis:** But you'd do best to remember this: Fool the people and karma will be on your tail. I'll overlook it this time, but don't count on there being a next time...

> **凯妮斯：**不过，阁下，记住了：愚弄人民者必得报应。这次我就睁一只眼闭一只眼，但绝不会有下次……

**Caenis:** ...Unless, of course, you crave cruel punishment and consider suffering the perfect state to ponder the true nature of souls.

> **凯妮斯：**…除非你爱好酷刑，善于在痛苦中思索灵魂的真谛。

**Anaxa:** Well, what do you know! That happens to be one of my rare talents.

> **那刻夏：**你知道吗？这恰恰是我最宝贵的天赋之一。

**Caenis:** We shall see, then.

> **凯妮斯：**那就让我们拭目以待吧。

**Cerces:** Ah, I've got to say that you've managed to really impress me again. But... shouldn't you be going after more?

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀，汝着实再次令吾刮目相看…不过，汝所求者，莫不是太少了些？

**Anaxa:** Dying to know what I'm after, huh?

> **那刻夏：**你就那么好奇我想干什么？

**Cerces:** I mean... I'm the Reason Titan, after all. It is in me to ask and seek answers.

> **瑟希斯：**毕竟吾乃理性之泰坦，生而为求索哪。

**Anaxa:** Easy there, you'll learn the answer soon enough.

> **那刻夏：**别急，马上你就知道了。

**Anaxa:** I didn't bring it up with her purely because she doesn't have the means to give me what I truly want. It's as they say, onlookers see most clearly.

> **那刻夏：**我刚才没在她面前提，只因为她给不了我真正想要的。毕竟「旁观者清」——

**Anaxa:** The true master of Dawncloud has always been the Theoros, who watches over everything.

> **那刻夏：**黎明云崖真正的主人，始终是那位纵览全局的「神礼观众」啊。

### Speak with Lygus

> *You reached out for protection and successfully gained Caenis's trust, forming an alliance with the Council of Elders. "Seeking protection" — what a perfect excuse, enough to convince you, the Council, and the Chrysos Heir of its legitimacy. Unfortunately... the real reason you came here was something else entirely— This requires the help of "Theoros" Lygus.*

*(Approach Lygus)*

**Lygus:** ...An expected visitor. Welcome, once again.

> **来古士：**…如我所料。再次欢迎您的到来，阁下。

**Lygus:** You know, you could actually skip all the pointless debate and come straight to me to seek an audience with Kephale.

> **来古士：**您知道吗？其实，您可以跳过那些无足轻重的辩论，直接向我要求觐见刻法勒的资格。

**Lygus:** I have no reason to refuse you, do I?

> **来古士：**我没有任何理由拒绝您，不是么？

**Anaxa:** You may not, but Caenis has. Whoever gets hold of Kephale's Coreflame will get to rule over Okhema...

> **那刻夏：**你当然没有理由，但凯妮斯有。谁能掌握刻法勒的火种，谁就能掌握奥赫玛……

**Anaxa:** She's wary of me, so naturally, I had to gain her trust. I would say that debate was not at all pointless.

> **那刻夏：**她忌惮我，我得博取她的信任。方才那场辩论非常必要。

**Lygus:** Very true.

> **来古士：**确凿的真理。

**Cerces:** In my view, this individual has gone to great lengths to get close to Kephale. He might very well be plotting something nefarious against the Titan.

> **瑟希斯：**依我看，此子费尽心思接近刻法勒，备不住是妄图对泰坦行不轨之事呢。

**Cerces:** Are you still willing to let him pass knowing this?

> **瑟希斯：**即便如此，汝也甘愿为此子放行么？

**Lygus:** Yes, very much so. All living creations are always free to visit Kephale's transcendent vessel. This comes with being the Worldbearer.

> **来古士：**当然，即便如此。刻法勒的奥体始终欢迎一切生命，此乃负世者命运使然。

**Anaxa:** Hmph, how convenient it is to be able to hear what they say...

> **那刻夏：**呵，能听到这家伙说话还真是方便……

**Lygus:** It is truly my honor to speak to a venerated Titan in person.

> **来古士：**能与尊贵的泰坦直接对话，是我至高无上的荣幸。

**Lygus:** Please, go ahead. Let me join you both on this journey.

> **来古士：**走吧，阁下，让我与你们同行一程。

### Keep following Lygus

**Lygus:** A bygone myth tells of an occasion where Kephale once answered their followers' questions on the peak of the holy mountain.

> **来古士：**在久远的神话里，刻法勒曾在神山上回答信众们的发问。

**Lygus:** Their followers have since believed that scaling this Titan cliff is one way of being with the god.

> **来古士：**所以，信众将登上泰坦断崖视为一种与神同行的方式。

**Cerces:** Hear that? It's not every day that you get to climb a mountain with Cerces. You should sing me a hymn of praise.

> **瑟希斯：**听见了吗？走吧，与瑟希斯登山同行，机会难得，汝当吟唱赞歌。

**Anaxa:** ...Titan hymns are beyond me.

> **那刻夏：**…我可不会唱什么泰坦赞歌。

*(Upon reaching the destination)*

**Lygus:** Go on ahead. Take this "Janus shortcut" and the long pilgrimage stairway will be waiting for you at its end.

> **来古士：**去吧，阁下。透过这道「雅努斯密径」，你便能步上朝圣的长阶了。

**Anaxa:** I have to admit I never expected you to not ask me anything, not even my motive.

> **那刻夏：**实话说，我没想到你竟然连理由都不会过问。

**Lygus:** Because I know very well what it is you are after. As the saying goes: A gentleman helps others achieve their goals. I have a feeling that your theory will be validated at the end of this journey—

> **来古士：**因为我十分清楚阁下所求为何物。同样地，正所谓「君子成人之美」：我有预感，阁下的理论能够在此行终点付诸证明——

**Lygus:** I believe Kephale will not deprive you of your right to ask your question, especially now that you're on the brink of death... you must be closer to Kephale than anyone else, wouldn't you agree?

> **来古士：**我相信，刻法勒绝不会拒绝您施行向泰坦提问的权利。毕竟，阁下身为将死之人，如今应是与刻法勒最为相近的存在了吧？

**Anaxa:** Ha... I hope things go as you say, then.

> **那刻夏：**呵…那就借你吉言了。

**Lygus:** Putting a child of justice into a farcical world is bound to turn it upside down...

> **来古士：**正义之子面对有悖常理的世界，会让世界天翻地覆……

**Lygus:** It is my hope that you bring a much-needed change to this world which is on the brink of destruction.

> **来古士：**愿您能为这濒毁的世界，带来真正的「变革」。

### Walk with the divine and ascend the Titan Cliff

> *The Titan Cliff — the closest one can get to Kephale's mystic body in all of Amphoreus. You have been planning to seek an audience with the Worldbearing Titan. To walk with the gods, and ascend the holy mountain.*

*(Cross through Janus' Hidden Passage)*

**Anaxa:** Honestly, this is my first time going up to Dawncloud's peak...

> **那刻夏：**老实说，这是我第一次登上黎明云崖的山巅……

**Cerces:** What a splendid view... Isn't it about time you recognized the magnificence of us Titans?

> **瑟希斯：**好景致哪…这下，汝也该承认泰坦十足壮观了吧？

**Anaxa:** Hmph. Well, I don't see how this place is different from those temples in the city. All they have are magnificent facades...

> **那刻夏：**哼，我反而觉得它和城中的神殿没有区别，虚有其表……

**Anaxa:** And even though I've come this far, I'm still doubtful about getting useful clues from him.

> **那刻夏：**即便到了这时候，我也仍在怀疑它到底能否给我带来有价值的线索。

**Cerces:** Hmph... I must be getting ahead of myself to be expecting to hear something nice from you.

> **瑟希斯：**呵…会期待汝说些中听的话，是吾自作聪明了。

**Anaxa:** Oh? Haven't you been in my head for some time now? It's high time you get used to how I feel about things.

> **那刻夏：**你在我的脑子里也算待了有段时间。怎么，还没有习惯身体主人的态度？

**Anaxa:** Alright, we're just a short hike away from Kephale. No time to waste here...

> **那刻夏：**行了，距离刻法勒只有几步之遥，别浪费时间……

**Anaxa:** Urk...!

> **那刻夏：**呃…！

**Cerces:** So, the intellectual debate from earlier was too draining for you, huh... If Amphoreus gets a new lease on life, you should focus on strengthening your body and stop shirking social interactions.

> **瑟希斯：**看来方才的智辩实属枯脑焦心哪…若翁法罗斯有来生，记得多加强身健体，也别再逃避人情酬应了。

**Anaxa:** ...Save your concern.

> **那刻夏：**…不劳你费心。

**Cerces:** The Titan is in sight. Can you make it?

> **瑟希斯：**眼前距离泰坦仅有几步之遥，汝还撑得住么？

**Anaxa:** Pfft... Why? Are you going to carry me up if I were to fall now?

> **那刻夏：**哼…就算我撑不住，你还能把我抬上去不成？

**Anaxa:** I know how far I can go, so just hold your tongue and come along.

> **那刻夏：**我自有把握，闭嘴跟着就是。

**Cerces:** Fine, then. I guess I won't say any more. I'll just wait till your soul dissipates completely to gain control over your body.

> **瑟希斯：**好，好。那吾也不多费口舌了，就只待汝魂息彻底散去，径自执掌躯壳罢。

**Anaxa:** (Wait, who is that...? Cerces?)

> **那刻夏：**（那是谁…瑟希斯？）

**Anaxa:** Hey, look at that...

> **那刻夏：**喂，你看那个……

**Cerces:** Hm? What should I be looking at?

> **瑟希斯：**嗯？要吾看什么？

**Anaxa:** (Am I hallucinating...?)

> **那刻夏：**（幻觉么……）

**Anaxa:** (Or do we even get to see souls when we're close to dying...?)

> **那刻夏：**（还是说，人在濒死之时，连灵魂也能看见了呢……）

### Continue climbing the mountain and investigate the phantoms that appeared when the soul left the body

> *The Titan Cliff — the closest one can get to Kephale's mystic body in all of Amphoreus. You have been planning to seek an audience with the Worldbearing Titan. Your body climbs the Sacred Path of Dawncloud, but your soul lingers at the shores of River of Souls... The souls along the mountain path — what do they want to tell?*

*(Approach the mysterious "souls")*

**???:** Oh? Why didn't you bring your favorite spear with you on this trip, Gnaeus?

> **？？？：**咦？格奈乌斯阁下，此行怎不见汝那伴身的爱枪了？

**Anaxa:** (Gnaeus? I know I've heard this name somewhere...)

> **那刻夏：**（格奈乌斯？熟悉的名字……）

**Gnaeus:** Ah, it's unfortunate...

> **格奈乌斯：**呵，说来遗憾……

**Gnaeus:** The black tide destroyed every last inch of my Kalyx.

> **格奈乌斯：**我的「喀利斯」，不久前已被黑潮彻底毁蚀了。

**???:** How could that be...?

> **？？？：**怎会如此……

**???:** Even a creation of Georios, from their prized ores and molten fire, couldn't escape the fate of destruction...?

> **？？？：**就连吉奥里亚宝矿和熔火的造物…都难逃毁灭的命运么？

**Anaxa:** Hey, Cerces... Do you know a certain Gnaeus?

> **那刻夏：**喂，瑟希斯…我问你，你可认识名为格奈乌斯的人？

**Cerces:** Gnaeus? No, not that I remember. How did you even think up such a strange name?

> **瑟希斯：**格奈乌斯？从未听说过。怎地突然想起这样一个古怪的名字？

**Anaxa:** I just saw him. Gnaeus... a warrior-like figure who was just chatting with you here...

> **那刻夏：**我刚才看到了：格奈乌斯，似乎是一名战士，与你就在此处交谈……

**Cerces:** No, I have no such memory. Could you be hallucinating?

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀…吾可不记得有这样的回忆，只怕是错觉吧？

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** (This is a... departed spirit...?)

> **那刻夏：**（这是…亡灵……）

*(Offer greetings to Kephale, optional)*

This is a pilgrimage platform dedicated to devout believers. But you, Anaxagoras, never kneel to false gods.

*(Approach the mysterious "souls" again)*

**Anaxa:** Hah, you two again...

> **那刻夏：**哈，又是你们……

**Gnaeus:** Enough about me. Did Khaos ever reply to you?

> **格奈乌斯：**不说我的事了。卡厄斯之前回应过你么？

**???:** Ahh, well, regrettably...

> **？？？：**啊呀，很可惜……

**Gnaeus:** ...Seems like the price of becoming a demigod is a lot heftier than we thought.

> **格奈乌斯：**…成为半神的代价，远比我们想象得要沉重啊。

**Anaxa:** (Khaos? Demigod?)

> **那刻夏：**（卡厄斯？半神？）

**Anaxa:** (I've never heard of this person... Which Titan's authority did he inherit?)

> **那刻夏：**（我从未听过这号人物…他接过的是哪个泰坦的权柄？）

**???:** Look on the bright side, Gnaeus. Since we've come all this way, I doubt he'd snub his old friends, would he?

> **？？？：**乐观些，格奈乌斯。吾等既已找上门来，他总该不会让旧友颜面扫地吧？

**Gnaeus:** Hmph... Let's just hope we mean as much as the world to him.

> **格奈乌斯：**呵…但愿我们在他心里的分量，能与整个世界相当。

**Cerces:** Hey? Are you seeing that same hallucination again?

> **瑟希斯：**人子啊，汝又看到方才那幻觉了？

**Anaxa:** Yes... It's still you and Gnaeus, but you're now talking about a certain Khaos. I'm guessing he's one of your companions?

> **那刻夏：**是啊…还是你和格奈乌斯，在讨论另一个叫卡厄斯的人，大概是你们的同伴吧。

**Cerces:** Khaos? This name sounds just as strange to me...

> **瑟希斯：**卡厄斯？这名字也十足陌生……

**Anaxa:** Never mind, I think I know what this is about. If things are as I thought, then they aren't hallucinations, but...

> **那刻夏：**算了，我大概有点头绪。如果不出我所料，他们应该不是幻觉，而是……

**Anaxa:** The souls of people.

> **那刻夏：**某人灵魂的样貌。

**Cerces:** Wha... But if those are the souls of the dead, then what am I?

> **瑟希斯：**啊呀…倘若那当真是死魂灵，那吾又是什么东西？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, beats me... For all I know, you're just an illusion I conjured up in my dying moments.

> **那刻夏：**哼，不知道…没准你才是我临死前臆想出来的幻象呢。

**Anaxa:** Let's just keep going. As long as I take in more sights and hear more stories...

> **那刻夏：**先走吧，只要能看到更多，听到更多故事……

**Cerces:** Know your limits. Wander too far among the dead and even I might not be able to save you.

> **瑟希斯：**量力而行罢——要是在死者当中走得太远，可连吾都将束手无策喔？

**Anaxa:** I'm fine...

> **那刻夏：**呵呵，无妨……

**Anaxa:** If this is indeed as I thought... then this will be very amusing.

> **那刻夏：**如果真如我所想…那可就太有意思了。

*(Approach the mysterious "souls" again)*

**Anaxa:** So, we meet again... I'm sure you wouldn't mind a snooping ear?

> **那刻夏：**又见面了…你们应该不介意多个听众吧？

**???:** Hngh... Ugh...

> **？？？：**呵…呵……

**Gnaeus:** Can you keep going?

> **格奈乌斯：**还能坚持住么？

**???:** O—Of course... I'm just...

> **？？？：**当…当然…吾只是……

**???:** Tsk... If I knew we had to climb a mountain, I wouldn't have come along with you...

> **？？？：**哎呀，真是的…早知道要爬这山，吾便拒绝汝等邀请了……

**Gnaeus:** I guess this also counts as one aspect of the Worldbearing burden. If Amphoreus does have a next life, you may want to get your body in shape.

...

Everything that I see and hear this time has left me shaken to the core.

What may seem like a mundane conversation is, in truth, a once-in-a-lifetime exchange that has revealed the ultimate answer to Cerces' question.

The conjecture has taken shape and must now be solidified into a theorem. Next, I only need to seek verification from Kephale. By doing so, I can answer Cerces' question and reclaim my body...

*(Screen fades to black)*

...But no, I will not do that. That isn't important.

Because another one of my conjectures — one that I have always dreamed of proving — has been proven right this very instant.

Fusing a mortal's soul with that of a Titan to replace said Titan... is not a ludicrous idea after all.
