> **丹恒：**阿那克…萨戈拉斯，是那位与「理性」火种合为一体的学者。

**Hyacine:** This is new. I didn't know Professor Anaxa was researching the Sky. He's never mentioned anything about it.

> **风堇：**连我也不知道，原来那刻夏老师进行过「天空」的研究。他从未提起此事。

**Dan Heng:** Phainon said Anaxa might be opposed to the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **丹恒：**听白厄说，他可能会站在逐火之旅的对立面。

**Hyacine:** "Might" is an understatement. Anaxa has never been one to venerate the gods, nor has he ever found it within himself to put any faith in the prophecy. People have come to think of him as a heretic, even in a place as liberal as the Grove.

> **风堇：**说「可能」有些委婉了。老师一向不敬神明，对神谕也持否定态度，即便在自由的树庭，他也被视作异端。

**Hyacine:** Yet, it is also this very "blasphemer" that Cerces favors. Fate sure works in mysterious ways.

> **风堇：**但偏偏是这样一位「渎神者」，却成了瑟希斯的神选。命运真是捉摸不透啊。

**Dan Heng:** I picked up a scroll on my way here, too. It looks like it may be related to the one you're holding.

> **丹恒：**我在路上也找到一份卷轴，似乎和你手中的有些关联。

**Hyacine:** Oh? Let me have a look.

> **风堇：**是吗？让我看看。

Hyacine combines both records together...

**Hyacine:** By the looks of them, these scrolls are records of two conversations that took place in the same setting, one after the other.

> **风堇：**看来这两份记录，是同一场景下的前后两段对话。

**Dan Heng:** There's still much I don't know about the Grove. Do you mind if I ask you a few questions?

> **丹恒：**我对树庭还不够了解，方便问几个问题么？

**Hyacine:** Sure, go ahead.

> **风堇：**请随意。

- ▸ Who are the Venerationists?

**Hyacine:** The Grove of Epiphany is home to seven schools of thought. While all seven schools are founded on the ideologies of Thalesus the First Scholar, each is unique in its own way. Among them, the Venerationists revere and worship the Titans the most.

> **风堇：**神悟树庭有七大学派，虽然都是围绕「最初的学者」塞勒苏斯的理念建立的，但彼此间各有不同，其中「敬拜学派」是最为敬奉泰坦的一支。

**Hyacine:** As the sage of the Venerationists, Euthyphro has always been at loggerheads with Anaxa.

> **风堇：**尤绪弗罗老师是「敬拜学派」的贤人，总是和那刻夏老师针锋相对。

**Hyacine:** If we were to place the Seven Sages on the scales of Talanton, Anaxa and Euthyphro would be placed on opposite sides to achieve balance.

> **风堇：**如果把七贤人都放到塔兰顿的天平上，一端是那刻夏，另一端一定得放上尤绪弗罗才能保持平衡吧。

**Dan Heng:** A very illustrative explanation. Thank you.

> **丹恒：**很形象的描述，我完全理解了。

- ▸ Was it a mistake to build the skyships?

**Hyacine:** Nearly everyone in Amphoreus has heard the story of the skyships by now. Aquila's stature is loftier than the heavens, and their divine retribution is merciless.

> **风堇：**如今，几乎每一个翁法罗斯人都知道「天舟」的故事。艾格勒的身躯比天空更伟岸，它的神罚是如此无情。

**Hyacine:** That's why it's no use building giant sky-faring ships... Oh, but I do wonder if a supercharged mini Tribbie cannon could slip past their watch?

> **风堇：**所以，建造大型航天器是行不通的。嗯…不知道威力乘以一百倍的缇宝小火筒能不能骗过它的眼睛。

**Dan Heng:** That was a joke... right?

> **丹恒：**这只是开玩笑，对吧。

- ▸ What is Anaxa's field of study?

**Hyacine:** Nousporists primarily study the application of alchemy in soul repair.

> **风堇：**「智种学派」，核心课题是藉由炼金术达成对「灵魂」的修补。

**Hyacine:** Everyone considers Anaxa a blasphemer, but that has never stopped him from sharing his ideas, no matter how taboo. If you must say that he's hiding something... then it's likely what lies beneath his eye patch.

> **风堇：**虽然被视作渎神者，但那刻夏本人从不避讳宣扬理念。要说有什么秘密…也只有他眼罩下的样子了。

- ▸ I don't have any more questions.

**Hyacine:** Anaxa often says that... "Every failure brings us one step closer to the truth."

> **风堇：**所有「错误」的尝试，都是更接近「正确」的一步…老师常把这句话挂在嘴边。

**Hyacine:** Perhaps, to him, the crash of the skyship carries a different interpretation. The firmament is not impenetrable. On the contrary, it was precisely because the skyship represented the first step toward breaking free from the shackles of the earth that it provoked the wrath of the Sky Titan and brought divine punishment upon itself.

> **风堇：**也许在他看来，天舟的坠毁具有另一重意义：天幕并非不可突破，正因它迈出了关键的一步，才会招致神罚。

**Hyacine:** It's a shame that the majority of the Grove's research on Aquila was headed by the Venerationists, with sacrificial rituals making up a predominant part of their findings. Discourse about more esoteric subject matters in relation to Aquila dwindled even further after the skyship incident, so much so that even this scroll is completely new to me.

> **风堇：**可惜，树庭对艾格勒的研究仍以「敬拜学派」为主，大多是些祭祀的仪式。对更外侧的探讨，在天舟一事后越发式微，连这份卷轴我也是第一次看见。

**Dan Heng:** Don't worry about it. We'll just collect everything we can find on the Titans for now.

> **丹恒：**没关系，眼下先回收针对泰坦的研究就好。

**Dan Heng:** There's something else that's caught my attention, though... This says that Anaxa is conducting secret experiments of some sort?

> **丹恒：**我更好奇另一件事：这里提到，那刻夏在进行某种秘密实验？

**Hyacine:** That's what I'd like to know. What are these experiments he's involved in? Even I, the assistant lecturer, know next to nothing about them...

> **风堇：**我也很在意…有什么研究，是连我这个讲师助理都不知情的？

### Head to the Library of Philia

> *Head to the Library of Philia with Hyacine to collect documents for the data bank and expand our understanding of Amphoreus. Anaxagoras, who survived the tragedy in the Grove, remains a mystery.*

**Hyacine:** I have a bad feeling about this. Could there be more to what happened at the Grove?

> **风堇：**总有种不妙的预感，难道树庭之灾背后还有别的秘密？

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hyacine:** This is the Library of Philia, a place where the Grove stores its collection of tomes.

> **风堇：**这里是树庭的藏书处，友爱之馆。

**Hyacine:** Other than books written by the seven schools, you can also find philosophical texts, ballads, and religious writings from the outlands here. Cities that desired to learn from the Grove were apparently expected to contribute knowledge of equal value to what they sought.

> **风堇：**七大学派的著述此处均有收藏，还囊括了外邦的哲学、诗歌、信仰书籍…据说向树庭求取智慧的城邦，都要献上等价的知识。

**Hyacine:** We've had more than our fair share of hilarious episodes over the years. For instance, it's said that the Erythrokeramists once borrowed a manuscript from a certain author but ended up returning a copy instead of the original! What's more, the original manuscript was supposedly then hidden here.

> **风堇：**历史上还发生过不少令人哭笑不得的事。据说「赤陶学派」曾向某位作家借取原稿，最后归还的却是副本，而真迹则被悄悄藏在这里。

**Dan Heng:** That... doesn't seem like a nice thing to do.

> **丹恒：**这…似乎有些无礼。

**Hyacine:** Nice? No need to hold yourself back, Dannie. You can just tell it like it is: Their behavior was no different from bandits!

**Dan Heng:** Well, if you don't mind: Did the Grove ever rob Castrum Kremnos?

> **丹恒：**那，树庭「打劫」过悬锋城么？

**Hyacine:** You've got me there. Some said you couldn't even find a dictionary in Castrum Kremnos, so I guess they were spared.

> **风堇：**这我就不知道了。有人说悬锋城连字典都没有，也许有幸逃过一劫吧。

**Hyacine:** There are too many books in the Library of Philia. Flipping through them all will take too long. Why don't we use the biblioslate to search for the texts we need? We can bring them back to Okhema for reading after that.

> **风堇：**友爱之馆的书目过于庞杂，一卷卷翻阅不现实。我们用藏书石版检索出需要的资料，统一带回奥赫玛研究吧。

**Dan Heng:** Agreed, this place isn't safe. We should leave as soon as we're done.

> **丹恒：**此地危险，也不适宜久留。

**Dan Heng:** There are still several more scrolls strewn all over the place. I'll gather them while you pull up those texts, this way, nothing will be overlooked.

> **丹恒：**地上还有些散落的卷轴。在你操作时，我四下检查一遍。这样应该不会有遗漏了。

### Search for the materials left by Anaxa

> *Head to the Library of Philia with Hyacine to collect materials for the data bank and expand our understanding of Amphoreus. Look for documents related to Anaxagoras.*

*(Investigate the scroll to the left of the stone statue, optional)*

- ▸ Investigate

A book covered in annotations from many scholars. Its title is The Art of Sophistry: 40 Ways to Improve Your Debating Skills.

You skim through it, and the highlighted methods include virtual causation, false premises, contradiction conversion, conflating prevarication and reality, misinterpreted punctuation, misattribution, and generalizing specificity, among others.

Amidst it are scholars' exclamations: "A true eye-opener, the art of language knows no bounds," "If I can't master sophistry, I can at least learn to defend myself against them as an honest man," and so on.

**Dan Heng:** The last resort is... "When all else fails, employ brute force."

> **丹恒：**最后一条方法是…「实在不行，付诸武力」。

**Dan Heng:** ...This isn't going to be of any help, that's for sure.

> **丹恒：**…不是什么有价值的信息。

*(Investigate the scroll to the right of the stone statue, optional)*

- ▸ Investigate

A record of the many research topics a Caprist scholar had applied for, and the replies from their school's Sage about these topics...

Topic 1: A discovery that many mythical beasts renowned for their longevity actually reside in areas with poorly managed birth and death records for such creatures. Permission to research. Reply: Lacks research significance, not approved.

Topic 2: An observation of real plants within the Grove imitating the growth patterns of nearby White Dryads. Permission to research. Reply: Falls under the domain of the Lotophagist school, not approved.

Topic 3: Investigating the feasibility of placing flying birds into rocket tubes to guide the direction of the rocket's flight. Permission to research. Reply: Too fanciful, not approved.

...The subsequent topics grew increasingly eclectic, yet the outcome of the replies remained unchanged.

**Dan Heng:** Impressive tenacity... Hopefully it helped him survive the black tide.

> **丹恒：**这种锲而不舍的精神…但愿能帮助他从黑潮中幸存。

*(Investigate the scroll in front of the stone statue)*

- ▸ Investigate

These are complaint letters from the scholars about Anaxagoras "The Blasphemer." There's a thick stack of letters.

Based on the dust accumulated and the perfectly intact seals, the complainee did not even bother opening them and just chucked them aside into a corner.

**Dan Heng:** This is... about Anaxa? I should read this. Hyacine, you should come have a look, too.

> **丹恒：**似乎是那刻夏的资料？值得注意，风堇也应该来看看。

**Hyacine:** Ack, it's so dusty in here... Whoa, are these all complaints about him?

> **风堇：**咳咳，好多灰尘…这些都是他收到的投诉信吗？

**Hyacine:** I can already see what they must have written: "Anaxa has no respect for the gods! His very existence undermines order in our society!" ...They're probably all things like that.

> **风堇：**不用打开也知道，里头一定是「那刻夏不敬神明，其人的存在就是对社会秩序的威胁」…诸如此类。

**Dan Heng:** Does Anaxa hold that much influence?

> **丹恒：**他的影响力这么大？

**Hyacine:** Why else would they call him "the Great Performer"? When Anaxa established his school of thought, the first thing he apparently did was read his own work to everyone.

> **风堇：**不然怎么会被称作「大表演家」呢？据说老师创立学派后的第一件事，就是当着所有人的面宣读自己的著作——

**Hyacine:** "Are the Titans truly an accurate reflection of what humans think gods should be? Worlds created by gods are indubitably perfect, but what of Amphoreus? Only foolishness and insensitivity everywhere you turn!"

> **风堇：**「至于泰坦，它们真的符合凡人对神的认知？神造的世界必定是完美的，可翁法罗斯却遍布愚钝和丑陋！」

**Dan Heng:** ...An uncanny impersonation. What happened after?

> **丹恒：**…很有他的风格，然后呢？

**Hyacine:** They marched him straight to the seat of judgment before he could finish.

> **风堇：**他没能讲完，就被押上了审判台。

**Hyacine:** Though what he took on in the end was more a debate than an actual trial. He had to address every one of the scholars' allegations, or face expulsion from the Grove.

> **风堇：**说是审判，其实是辩论。他需要一一回应学者们的控诉，否则就要被逐出树庭。

**Hyacine:** The first allegation was... ahem. "Do you agree that since the Titans created the heavens and earth, so the mountains, rivers, and seas must likewise be their creations?"

> **风堇：**第一项控诉是：咳咳——「你是否同意泰坦们塑造了天地，山川海洋便是它们的杰作？」

**Dan Heng:** ...Am I supposed to put myself in Anaxa's shoes and answer that?

> **丹恒：**…是要我站在那刻夏的立场作出回答吗？

- ▸ Yes.

**Dan Heng:** As told in the myths, the seas, the skies, and the earth are all, beyond the shadow of a doubt, creations of the Titans.

> **丹恒：**依照神话所述，海洋、天空、大地都是泰坦的造物，确凿无疑。

**Hyacine:** Anaxa would've probably been spared a lot of trouble if he had answered this way... Instead, he quoted past instances of the Flame-Chase Journey and posited that humans have the potential to take over the divinity of Titans.

> **风堇：**如果老师也能这样回答，恐怕就不用受难了。但他却以逐火的历史为例，宣扬人类也有接过神权的可能性。

- ▸ No.

**Dan Heng:** Based on the history of the Flame-Chase Journey, humans have the potential to take over the divinity of Titans.

> **丹恒：**从逐火的历史来看，人类也有接过神权的可能性。

**Hyacine:** Anaxa said the same thing then.

> **风堇：**这也是老师当时的观点。

**Hyacine:** Needless to say, his critics didn't take kindly to his answer, so they followed up with a second allegation.

> **风堇：**反对者自然对这个回答很不满意，于是便有了第二项控诉。

**Hyacine:** "You claim to be studying souls, yet you are, in fact, defiling them and committing acts condemned by the gods. Do you admit it?"

> **风堇：**「你以研究灵魂之名玷污灵魂，进行神所憎恶的交易，你是否承认？」

**Dan Heng:** That's a serious allegation. Did he really do that?

> **丹恒：**相当严肃的指控，他还做过这种事？

**Hyacine:** Anaxa always wears an eye patch, never removing it, even when alone. Some say he used one of his eyes as an alchemical ingredient.

> **风堇：**老师永远戴着眼罩，连独处时都不摘下，有人说他是把一只眼睛用作了炼金材料。

**Hyacine:** Every being in the world owes their safekeeping to Kephale, and this is even more so for the Chrysos Heirs who have divine blood coursing through them. If he has really done something of that nature, then it'd indeed be sacrilegious.

> **风堇：**世间的人都由刻法勒庇护，尤其是沐浴神血的黄金裔。倘若真有此事，无疑是亵渎神明。

**Hyacine:** Thankfully, they had no way to prove it, so it amounted to nothing more than an allegation in the end.

> **风堇：**幸好，因为没有证据，这项控诉最终不了了之。

**Hyacine:** One by one, Anaxa engaged in wars of words against those who would challenge him. Toward the end, a scholar asked him, "Anaxagoras, have you any respect for the gods?"

> **风堇：**老师就这么舌战群儒，与挑战者一一辩论。直到最后，一位学者问他：「阿那克萨戈拉斯，你是否不敬神灵？」

**Hyacine:** It was only after a brief silence that he answered, "I have never denied that Titans are meant to be noble constructs."

> **风堇：**他沉默了几秒，回答道：「我从不否认泰坦应是伟大之物。」

**Dan Heng:** What he didn't say — that the Titans are not noble — rings louder in this case.

> **丹恒：**显然话里有话。他的潜台词是，泰坦并不伟大。

**Hyacine:** Yes... I'm not sure how that controversy was eventually resolved, but what's for certain at least is that Anaxa was allowed to stay in the Grove where he continued his research, despite being regarded as a heretic.

> **风堇：**嗯…我不知道那场骚动是如何平息的，但老师最终还是留在了树庭。即便被视作异端，他也在继续自己的研究。

**Hyacine:** By the way, this stack of complaint letters has reminded me of a place in the Grove... We may find clues there that aren't on the teleslate.

> **风堇：**对了，这沓投诉信提醒了我，树庭里还有个地方，或许存在石版中都查不到的线索……

**Hyacine:** I'm talking about Anaxa's alchemy lab, hidden between the cornerstones.

> **风堇：**根石之间，那刻夏老师的炼金实验室。

**Dan Heng:** Given the Grove's meticulous approach to archiving, could there really be such an oversight right under their noses?

> **丹恒：**以树庭的藏书风格，竟会有这种眼皮底下的遗漏？

**Hyacine:** It's a well-kept secret. Among the Seven Sages, excluding the nominal Chief Sage — each has a private sanctuary where they can conduct research undisturbed in their free time.

> **风堇：**这是个秘密：七贤人除去首席虚设，其余列位各自享有一处禁地，供他们在遐思之余，进行不会被干扰的研究。

**Hyacine:** Even the administrators of the Library of Philia are restricted from accessing those places and documenting the sages' research. But...

> **风堇：**就连友爱之馆的管理员也不能越过禁令，收录贤人的课题。不过现在……

**Hyacine:** With the Grove in this state, I'm sure Anaxa won't be mad even if we sneak into the lab for a short while... Right?

> **风堇：**树庭都成这样了，小小打破一下禁令也没事吧…对不住啦那刻夏老师。

### Search for Anaxa's lab between the cornerstones

**Dan Heng:** These trees... They're like a maze.

> **丹恒：**真是一片曲折的树海啊。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hyacine:** This is the place, but... where is everything?

> **风堇：**我们到了，不过…这里居然什么都没有了？

**Hyacine:** Materials for alchemical experiments, matrix diagrams, and even the seal to the lab... They're all gone. Did the black tide destroy it all?

> **风堇：**炼金材料，法阵图样，甚至禁令的封印也不在了…难道都被黑潮摧毁了？

**Dan Heng:** No wonder we didn't see any defense mechanisms along the way.

> **丹恒：**难怪，这一路连点像样的防备都没有。

**Dan Heng:** Have you ever been here before?

> **丹恒：**你之前进入过这里么？

**Hyacine:** No, not even close. Not only are alchemy experiments high-risk activities, but Professor Anaxa also has a penchant for using bizarre materials. Students often saw him walk in looking right as rain but come out covered in soot with his hair singed. Naturally, no one dared to come near this place.

> **风堇：**没有，甚至从未接近过。炼金是高危活动，那刻夏老师又钟情古怪的材料。学生们经常见他好好地进去，却灰头土脸甚至满脸炸毛地出来，自然不敢靠近这里。

**Hyacine:** Huh? That bookshelf over there seems to be the only thing still intact.

> **风堇：**…咦？唯独那边的书柜，保存得很完整呢。

### Search for the materials left by Anaxa

*(Investigate the scroll on top of the broken shelf)*

- ▸ Investigate

These are the materials requested by Anaxagoras. Documents from various fields have been compiled in one place, including "Titan Creations," "The Universality of the Soul," "Alchemy Doctrine," and so on.

Most of the materials look to be within the research domain of the Nousporist school of thought. However, a document titled A Study on the Chrysos Heirs' Background is mixed within.

*(Quick read)*

A Hastily Written Journal (I)

*These appear to be quick notes, examining the background of Chrysos Heirs...*

The Holy Maiden of Janusopolis — Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon, descendants of the Holy Maiden from the Temple of the Three Fates... Goldweaver Aglaea of Okhema... from the Mnestia priestly family... The Prince of Castrum Kremnos... Hyacine of the Twilight Courtyard... All of their ancestry have varying degrees of connection to the divine Titans or their divine authorities.

...

Does the golden blood... come from the heritage of priest families... or royal bloodlines...?

...

Anaxagoras — I myself show no such connection. Kephale the Creator... what criteria did they use to create the golden blood... to shape the Chrysos Heirs?

...

Yet Castorice, the Servant of Death from Aidonia... her lineage is neither Titan priest nor demigod... but she has walked the earth for at least a millennium with the power to grant "Death" to humans... Where did this power come from?

...

Another text speaks of the dragon legends of Styxia... The alchemical extraction of "soul" ripped from dragon corpses is intriguing... Dragons appear to be the Titans' creations... Perhaps there's a way to reverse the process — using alchemy to re-fuse human "souls" with Titan creations, increasing the purity and divinity of golden blood toward the absolute?

...

This theory must be tested. Experiments to be arranged.

**Hyacine:** What's going on...? Anaxa is conducting research on our friends in Okhema and... me?

> **风堇：**这是怎么回事…奥赫玛的大家，还有我？连我也在老师的研究范围内？

**Dan Heng:** This journal contains several intriguing points...

> **丹恒：**这份手记，有不少值得细究的地方——

- ▸ Creations of Titans...

**Dan Heng:** From the Abyss of Fate to Castrum Kremnos, I've seen many a Titan creation during my time in Amphoreus. Lifeforms created by Cerces can also be found in the Grove.

> **丹恒：**从命运重渊到悬锋城，我已见过许多泰坦造物，树庭里也有不少瑟希斯创造的生灵。

**Dan Heng:** But fusing them with human souls through alchemical means? I can't even begin to imagine...

> **丹恒：**可是通过炼金术将它们与人的灵魂融合？我很难想象……

- ▸ The Chrysos Heirs...

**Dan Heng:** Anaxa seems to hold special interest in the divinity coursing through the Chrysos Heirs' veins, and has even conducted a thorough investigation into the heroes' backgrounds.

> **丹恒：**那刻夏格外关注黄金裔流淌的「神性」，还对英雄们的背景做了细致考察。

A Hastily Written Journal (II)

*These documents appear incomplete. Judging from what's left behind, the important alchemical formulae have been taken away — only the author's reasoning remains...*

The previous approach... has flaws. Fusing human "souls" with Titan creations does not purify golden blood. Even pure Titan creations are contaminants to the Chrysos Heirs' golden blood. Soul fusion between humans and creations merely repairs impurities of the soul or maintains a connection to dying souls.

...

While creation myths tell us that Chrysos Heirs and other creations were all made by Titans, their nature differs fundamentally. The Titans' creations are no intermediate products of an alchemical process, and the Chrysos Heirs are closer to the Titans than the creations. In other words, I should not have considered Titan creations equal to Chrysos Heirs. The hypothesis I should focus on is one pointed towards the Titans themselves.

...

Such rare chances to study Titans demand a meticulous selection of research subjects. Living Titans prove impossible to restrain. And dead Titans offer no insights worth pursuing. But dying Titans — they are the perfect research subjects.

...

Only by understanding the fundamentals of creation can we ensure perfection at the moment of everything's birth. Through my own hands, I will melt a Titan and reach the true essence of the soul...

**Hyacine:** My biggest fear... It's come true... After going through his notes, I think I can piece it all together now:

### Have an audience with Kephale's divine vessel, and prepare for transmutation

> *You had a feeling that getting close to Kephale would reveal unexpected truths. Indeed, as one of the living dead, you can perceive "precious memories" that others cannot. Now, prepare for transmutation.*

**Anaxa:** Sir, this sky above us... It's not real, is it?

> **那刻夏：**老师，我们看到的这片天空是假的，对吧？

**Empedocles:** Of course not. This is just an image from a projection device used for educational purposes. A simple trick.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**当然，是假的。这只是教学装置，不起眼的魔术。

**Empedocles:** The day-night cycle has been lost to this world. So that students like you may still learn about the night sky, astronomers have harnessed the Titans' miraculous power to manipulate the light, projecting the "nights of the past" here for you.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**如今昼夜失序，为了方便你们理解星空，天文学者借助泰坦神迹，变换光线，将「过去的夜晚」投影在这里……

**Empedocles:** Much like how the sky above the holy city is also illuminated by Kephale's Dawn Device.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**正如圣城的天空，也是由刻法勒的黎明机器点亮的。

**Anaxa:** So, the skies of Okhema are fake, too.

> **那刻夏：**所以，奥赫玛的天空也是虚假的。

**Empedocles:** You mustn't conclude that, Anaxa. You should know that only a child would expose a trick and be proud of it.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**不能这么定性，那刻夏。你要知道：只有孩子才会揭开魔术的幕布，并以此为傲。

**Empedocles:** Often, people know more than they let on. They simply choose to feign ignorance because they have no other choice.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**很多时候，人们并非真正无知，而是必须装作无知。

**Anaxa:** Then they're just deluding themselves. People believe in Kephale's tale of genesis, accepting wholesale whatever the priests preach, but we're different. We've already walked out of the fire-lit cave of ignorance.

> **那刻夏：**这是自欺欺人。民众信奉刻法勒的创世神话，祭司们说什么，他们就信什么。可是，你和我，我们已从蒙昧的洞穴里醒转。

**Anaxa:** We have already realized that we live in a world where Titans make the rules. Even when we look at the sky, we're only seeing what they allow us to see. Rather than gazing at this mockery of a night sky fabricated by them for the rest of my life, why shouldn't I be the one to take control of the projection device instead?

> **那刻夏：**我们已经意识到，自己身处在一个由泰坦编织规则的世界，满眼尽是它们投下的「星空」。与其守望这片虚假的永夜，我为何不能成为操作投影仪器的那个人？

**Empedocles:** So much time has passed since you sowed the seeds of doubt in my heart, child. Many a night, I find myself penning down all my theories and wild ideas, only to burn them to ashes when I open my eyes in the day.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**孩子啊，自你为我播下怀疑的种子，又已过去了许久。我在无数个日夜记录下自己的推论和狂想，却又在醒来时把它们尽数焚毁。

**Anaxa:** Why? Because we'll face death by burning if word gets out?

> **那刻夏：**为什么？仅仅是因为它会将你我送上火刑的尸床？

**Empedocles:** As a sage of the Venerationists, my soul has already become inextricably linked to the Great Tree. Every word I say and every move I make is certain to influence every leaf, every branch... even the Tree itself.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**我身为「敬拜学派」的贤人，根系早已与巨树相连，一举一动必将牵掣它的每一根枝、每一片叶，甚至撼动大树本身。

**Empedocles:** But above all else, my days are numbered. Instinctively, I have my doubts about the golden blood and the human soul, yet I have no means of verifying them. The golden blood in my veins is woefully thin, and I am loath to waste what little time I have left obsessing over a hypothesis that cannot even be proven in the first place.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**然而，最重要的是：我身为将死之人，对金血和灵魂的思考只能通过直觉怀疑，而无法付诸实践。黄金的血脉在我体内如此稀薄，我不愿再去思考本就无力证明的猜想。

**Anaxa:** But I can, sir! I can do it for us! The time is right. Give me all that I need, and I shall accomplish this grand feat on your behalf.

> **那刻夏：**但是我可以，老师！时机已经成熟，给我需要的一切，我将代您完成这至高至伟的大功业。

> **恩贝多克利斯：**去吧，准备好创立你自己的学派——

**Cerces:** Child of humanity...

> **瑟希斯：**所以，人子啊……

**Cerces:** You didn't go to such lengths for an audience with Kephale just to ask him a few questions, did you?

> **瑟希斯：**汝大费周章，只为博得刻法勒一面光照…恐怕不只是为了质问神明吧？

**Anaxa:** Hmph. So you figured it out.

> **那刻夏：**哼，不错。

**Anaxa:** If I were as foolish as a dromas, perhaps I'd throw myself down and prostrate before their greatness, begging for the gods' prophetic wisdom. Well, sorry to disappoint, but I'm no dromas. Alas, I am a human, standing tall on two feet, possessing both intellect and pride.

> **那刻夏：**倘若我是一头愚昧无知的大地兽，面对此等伟力，也许会将四肢匍匐在大地上，盼望诸神为我降下神谕。只可惜，我是靠双足直立行走，拥有智慧和尊严的人类。

**Cerces:** Heh... Before such power, does it make any difference whether you're a human or a dromas?

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵…不过，恕吾直言：在如此悬殊的力量面前，是人，或是大地兽，有何殊异呢？

**Anaxa:** I'm sure you're familiar with the story of the Styxians. When the raging waves threatened to swallow them, they didn't just give up and become sitting ducks. No, they constructed a dam that kept the roaring tide at bay, chaining down the mad Phagousa.

> **那刻夏：**你应当听说过斯缇科西亚人的故事：他们面对汹涌进犯的大海，并没有坐以待毙，而是修建了匹敌怒涛的堤坝，为癫狂的法吉娜套上了枷锁。

**Anaxa:** They call me a blasphemer, Cerces, but that doesn't mean I deny the existence of gods. It's just...

> **那刻夏：**瑟希斯，人们都说我是渎神者。但这不代表我否认神性的存在，只是在我看来——

**Anaxa:** ...As I see it, Titans are simply a force that mankind has yet to find a way to tame.

> **那刻夏：**——泰坦，不过是人类尚未征服的力量罢了。

**Cerces:** Oh? If power that rivals the Titans' is what you crave, why not conform to the prophecy and brave the trial?

> **瑟希斯：**哦？既然汝意图拥有匹敌泰坦的力量，那末依神谕所示，挑战试炼便是。

**Anaxa:** Hah, why stop at harnessing the power of the Titans when I can do so much more?

> **那刻夏：**呵，仅仅掌握泰坦的神力，未免太过肤浅。

**Anaxa:** The source of life is what I wish to grasp... To understand what makes up the soul, and to learn what we really are.

> **那刻夏：**我要掌握的是生命根源之法，「灵魂」的本质——「『我们』究竟是为何物」。

**Anaxa:** Thanks to you, I've had the chance to personally verify — in more ways than one — that the soul indeed ceases to exist in "Death." But where there is death, there is life, and where there is an end, there must also be a beginning.

> **那刻夏：**拜你所赐，「死亡」是灵魂的终结这一事实，我已通过种种迹象亲自验讫。可有死必有生，有终结必有开端——

**Anaxa:** How, then, is a soul born? That was the only unknown variable left in my calculations.

> **那刻夏：**灵魂如何诞生？——在我的算式中，只剩下这一个未知数。

**Cerces:** And by the sound of it, you have already formed a conjecture.

> **瑟希斯：**听汝这口气，想必是有所猜度了罢？

**Anaxa:** No, I have already drawn a conclusion. In my journey unto death just now, I finally saw the answer with my own eyes.

> **那刻夏：**…不，是已有结论了。就在刚才的死亡之旅中，我亲眼看见了答案。

**Anaxa:** Of course, I'll bring this to a conclusive end and give you the answer you so desire.

> **那刻夏：**当然，我会一锤定音，给你想要的解答。

**Anaxa:** However, in accordance with the principle of "equivalent exchange," I must also demand from you what I need.

> **那刻夏：**但依「等价交换」的原则，我也必须向你索取我需要之物。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** ...As you wish.

> **遐蝶：**…我同意交换。

**Castorice:** It's a matter of uncovering the truth about the Death Titan — and (Trailblazer)'s survival...

> **遐蝶：**事关死亡泰坦的真相，还有开拓者的性命……

**Castorice:** This may be the only time I can ever save someone's life. There is no need for me to think twice.

> **遐蝶：**这或许是我此生唯一一次能挽救一条生命的机会，我没有犹豫的理由。

**Anaxa:** I see. In that case... let the show begin.

> **那刻夏：**好啊，那…就让表演开始吧。

**Cerces:** Indulge me: Am I correct to say that you intend to prove Thanatos' whereabouts and prove the nature of our existence now?

> **瑟希斯：**且慢。容吾打断一下，汝是准备同时证明塞纳托斯之所在，以及「我们」究竟为何物？

**Anaxa:** Before I begin, I must confess I had been stuck in a cognitive trap all this time.

> **那刻夏：**第一，我必须承认，我一直以来都落入了认知陷阱。

**Anaxa:** As unrelated as these two propositions may seem at first glance, they are, in fact, logical equivalents. They are merely two different ways of telling us about what the soul is at its core.

> **那刻夏：**表面上看，这两个命题毫不相干；然而，它们恰恰逻辑等价，不过是对「灵魂本质」的两种叙述。

**Anaxa:** The reason I say this is as I've mentioned earlier: The answer was hidden in my journey unto death.

> **那刻夏：**第二，为什么我会这么说？刚才已经解释过，答案就藏在那场死亡之旅中。

**Castorice:** Anaxa... you were in the nether realm?

> **遐蝶：**老师，你…去到了冥界？

**Anaxa:** Yes — I am but a living dead man now — but that's not important. What truly matters is — I overheard a conversation between heroes.

> **那刻夏：**不错，因为我现在是个活死人。但这不重要，重要的是——我听到了一众英雄人物的谈话。

**Anaxa:** That's not all... Remember how I said you were a part of that conversation too, Cerces?

> **那刻夏：**而且，不仅如此…记得吗，瑟希斯？我还见到你也参与了那场对谈。

**Cerces:** And I've told you that I have no recollection of such an event. That was likely just an illusion conjured up by the mind of a dying man.

> **瑟希斯：**可吾也说了，吾从未有过如此记忆。那怕是汝死前眼中臆造的幻象。

**Anaxa:** Of course you wouldn't remember.

> **那刻夏：**不记得就对了——

**Anaxa:** Because that wasn't you. That was Calypso, one of the Seven Sages of the Grove.

> **那刻夏：**因为那根本不是你，而是「卡吕普索」，来自树庭的七贤人之一。

**Castorice:** ...Calypso?

> **遐蝶：**…卡吕普索？

**Cerces:** That's the name I made up on a whim when speaking to this girl. How do you know of it?

> **瑟希斯：**那不是吾在这姑娘面前随口杜撰的假名么，汝怎会知道？

**Cerces:** And surely you jest. Don't you think I, of all people, would know if that were the name of one of the Seven Sages under the shade of my tree? Don't you claim to know the Grove's past like the back of your hand?

> **瑟希斯：**七贤人就更是笑谈了，若真是在吾之树荫下蒙受庇护的贤人，吾必能发觉。汝不也对树庭的过去如指诸掌么？

**Anaxa:** It seems you've lost even the memories seared into your soul.

> **那刻夏：**看来你连烙印在灵魂中的记忆都忘却了啊。

**Anaxa:** Use your head, Cerces. Since all of this took place in the realm of the dead, it stands to reason their likenesses are of souls from the past.

> **那刻夏：**动动脑子吧，想一想：既然这一切全部发生在死者的领域，那么，他们一定都是过去某人灵魂的样貌。

**Anaxa:** The living cannot step foot into that world, so who could they be? Think about Calypso. Why does she share your likeness, yet you have no idea who she is?

> **那刻夏：**生者绝无可能步入这片天地，那他们究竟是谁？尤其是方才提到的卡吕普索，缘何长着一副和你瑟希斯相同的面孔，可后者却对所有人毫无印象？

**Anaxa:** The answer is evident, is it not?

> **那刻夏：**我想，答案已经显而易见了——

**Anaxa:** Those fallen heroes are the mortal incarnations of the Titans before their ascensions to godhood. The gods aren't master creators born out of thin air, and they are no different from humans — because they all evolved from one.

> **那刻夏：**那诸位已陨的英雄，正是泰坦们成神前的模样。众神并非凭空诞生的造物巨匠，而是与人类无异，并由之演化而来的存在。

**Cerces:** Hmm...

> **瑟希斯：**唔……

**Anaxa:** Does this answer disappoint you?

> **那刻夏：**怎么，这个答案让你失望了吗？

**Cerces:** Yes, because you've answered nothing. If I truly was once human as you claim, then where do the children of humanity come from?

> **瑟希斯：**当然，汝这不是说了等于没说么？如果说吾果真本为人子，那汝等人子又是从何而来呢？

**Castorice:** Even on the off chance that you speak the truth, this brings us no closer to grasping Thanatos' whereabouts. How are we to find them?

> **遐蝶：**而且，就算事实果真如此…那也与塞纳托斯的所在相距甚远，我们要如何找到它？

**Anaxa:** Heh, I knew you would say that.

> **那刻夏：**呵，我早知道各位会这么说……

**Anaxa:** But it matters not. I know what I'm doing. Actually, why don't we have this supreme being standing before us tell us everything?

> **那刻夏：**我自有把握。不妨就请眼前这位至高之神，为我们一一道来吧。

**Cerces:** You can revive a Titan?

> **瑟希斯：**汝…有把握复活一位泰坦？

**Anaxa:** Humans cannot return from the dead. Neither can gods.

> **那刻夏：**人本不能死而复生，更何况是神明。

**Anaxa:** But all we need to do is look at this from a different perspective, and the problem will resolve itself. The solution is simple: Just like what you did to me...

> **那刻夏：**但只要把思路逆转过来，一切便能迎刃而解。答案很简单，就和你对我做的事一样……

**Anaxa:** If I'm reborn as Kephale, I can rebuild the world and all within it as they once did. Simple, is it not?

> **那刻夏：**我来作为刻法勒重新诞生，并按它的方法再造世间万物，不就是了吗？

## 117　Debate, Discourse Without Spears　争辩啊，代长矛分庭抗礼

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Demigod Council" Dawncloud　|　角色：Trailblazer; Phainon; Aglaea; Krateros; Mydei; Lygus; Caenis; Anaxa; Castorice; Gnaeus; Cerces　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Papyrus, Read the Blasphemer's Will'' completed

### Ask about Axana's whereabouts with Aglaea and the others

In the meantime...

**Aglaea:** I have news from Hyacine and Dan Heng. Anaxa might be trying to do something with Kephale's divine body.

> **阿格莱雅：**风堇和丹恒传来消息：那刻夏可能会对刻法勒的神躯有所动作。

**Phainon:** Kephale? But what could Anaxa want...? Is the Council of Elders going to sit around and let such a sacrilegious act unfold?

> **白厄：**刻法勒？这是要做什么…元老院会放任如此大不敬之举吗？

**Aglaea:** That is not possible.

> **阿格莱雅：**绝无可能。

**Aglaea:** He is indeed "the Great Performer," capable of fooling anyone and everyone with his tricks.

> **阿格莱雅：**真是名副其实的「大表演家」，任何人都可能被他的演技欺骗。

**Phainon:** I... I'm sorry.

> **白厄：**我…很抱歉。

**Aglaea:** No need for guilt, for this is good news to us. The Council of Elders has found themselves a traitorous ally. Anaxa's ambitious desires will be something we can exploit.

> **阿格莱雅：**不必感到愧疚，这反而是个好消息。元老院的盟友并不忠诚，那刻夏的野心定能为我们所用。

**Aglaea:** What we should be doing now is to keep our eyes peeled for whichever trickery Caenis is up to and react accordingly to thwart her plans.

> **阿格莱雅：**至于现在，端看凯妮斯将设下何种圈套，见招拆招便是。

**Aglaea:** Being Okhema's political center, the Demigod Council holds just as much significance as the Vortex of Genesis. Someday, after my duty is over, this site will take the place of the battlefield where you hunt gods, become the next challenge you ought to overcome...

> **阿格莱雅：**半神议院是奥赫玛的政治腹地，重要性不亚于创世涡心。在我的使命结束后，总有一天，这里会取代猎神的疆场，成为你们的下一道难关——

**Aglaea:** Till then, it's best to get used to this place as soon as possible.

> **阿格莱雅：**趁时间还多，尽早适应这片舞台吧。

- ▸ We will be waiting here for your good news.
- ▸ Come find us anytime you need something.

**Strolling Councilor:** Preposterous! The Chrysos Heirs only have their standing because of the Council of Elders. How dare they trample over it!

> **闲逛的议员：**荒唐啊！黄金裔的地位是元老院给的，他们竟然不珍惜。

**(Trailblazer):** (Wow, these people are shooting daggers with their eyes...)

> **开拓者：**（这些人的目光，跟刀子一样啊……）

**Krateros:** It's been a while.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**久违了，两位。

- ▸ Hello, Oleg of Amphoreus.

**Krateros:** ...What is that supposed to mean? Is this really the sort of person that the young lord praised as a ferocious warrior?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**…你在说什么胡话，少主赞不绝口的猛士就这副德行？

- ▸ But we've never met.

**Krateros:** That's fine. I recognize you. The young lord has nothing but good words about your courage.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**无妨，我认得你。少主对你的勇猛赞不绝口。

**Phainon:** It has indeed been some time, Lord Krateros. What brings you here in such haste...?

> **白厄：**好久不见，克拉特鲁斯阁下。怎么来得如此匆忙……

**Krateros:** Shh...! Keep it down.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**嘘，安静点…！

**Krateros:** Be more vigilant. See those duplicitous dogs over there? They do the dirty work for Caenis.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**招子放亮些，看到那群假笑的鬣狗了么？凯妮斯手下干脏活的。

**Krateros:** I've already ordered my people to hold them back, but we should still lay low... Unless you're itching for a bloody fight to get a workout in.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**我吩咐族人绊住他们了，但还是别太张扬。除非你们想动动筋骨，来一场厮杀。

**Phainon:** My bad. So, did something happen?

> **白厄：**抱歉。所以，发生了什么？

**Krateros:** Did you hear thunderclaps last night?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**昨晚，你们听见雷声了么？

- ▸ It would be hard not to notice thunderclaps in broad daylight.
- ▸ No, I'm a heavy sleeper.
- ▸ Hurray, it's Acheron! Someone's coming to save us!

**Krateros:** Acher... what?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**黄…什么？

**Phainon:** He's/She's been through a lot lately. Please pardon him./her. Can you tell us more about these thunderclaps?

> **白厄：**他/她最近太劳累了，体谅一下。展开说说雷声吧？

**Krateros:** Thunderclaps are beacon signals of Strife. They are a message from the young lord. He threw a spear bearing several words at Okhema.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**雷声是「纷争」的烽火，是少主捎来消息了。他向奥赫玛掷来了传信的投枪，上面只有几个字。

**Mydei:** "Watch out. The Flame Reaver is back."

> **万敌：**「盗火者，死灰复燃，警惕。」

**Phainon:** ...I guessed as much. We didn't actually kill them back at the Kremnos arena.

> **白厄：**…果然。先前在悬锋竞技场，我们没有真正杀死它。

**Krateros:** I figured this is something you would want to know. We Kremnoans never back down from a fight, so if you plan to take that thing down in a battle, reach out to me anytime.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你们应当知晓这一信息。悬锋人绝不退缩。倘若需要应战，随时找我。

**Phainon:** You have my gratitude. I will let Aglaea know about this right away.

> **白厄：**感激不尽，我会立刻转告阿格莱雅。

**Krateros:** Remind her to be on her toes while you're at it. That vicious Caenis has been unusually active lately.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**还有，叫那女人小心：那如毒蛇般的凯妮斯，近期异动频频。

**Krateros:** Those lackeys she keeps undercover have been going in and out of the city a lot more frequently these days. Some even seem to be getting Kremnoans to defect to the Council...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**她手底下见不得光的走狗，在城中出没得愈发频繁了，甚至还有策反悬锋人的倾向……

**Crowd:** Get off the stage! Scram!

> **众人：**下台！下台！

**Phainon:** That's...

> **白厄：**这是……

**Krateros:** Hmph. Looks like you can see it for yourself.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**哼，看来不用我多说了。

**Phainon:** We should get back. Thank you for the intel, Lord Krateros.

> **白厄：**我们回去看看，克拉特鲁斯阁下，多谢你的情报。

### Return to the Demigod Council and inform Aglaea about things

> *After discovering Anaxagoras had fled to the Council of Elders, Aglaea rushed to Dawncloud to confront the Council. Deliver Mydei's warning to Aglaea.*

*(Approach the two councilors to the left of the bridge, idle text)*

**Nosy Councilor:** The Council of Elders has long fallen into disgrace.

**Steadfast Councilor:** I want to support the Council of Elders.

*(Listen to the two councilors to the left of the bridge, optional)*

**Nosy Councilor:** Haha, someone trampled right over the master's flower bed, yet the master only fumes in silence. Oopsie, there goes the reputation of the Council of Elders.

**Steadfast Councilor:** Whose side am I on? Why, I'm all for the Council of Elders to reclaim their authority, of course!

*(Approach the three councilors on the bridge, idle text)*

**Careful Councilor:** Hush, the Chrysos Heir is here.

**Pious Councilor:** On behalf of the Chrysos Heir, I plead for forgiveness.

*(Listen to the three councilors on the bridge, optional)*

**Careful Councilor:** They're here. The Chrysos Heirs are here.

**Elderly Councilor:** I should've gone back home to fetch a sacrificial vessel, some floral crowns, and frankincense to burn and offer...

**Pious Councilor:** O Titans, please forgive these arrogant and self-conceited heroes.

*(While crossing the bridge)*

**Lygus:** By Kephale and Talanton above, as the Theoros, I have observed...

> **来古士：**刻法勒与塔兰顿在上，以神礼观众之名，我见到——

**Lygus:** Virtuous citizens of Okhema, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae and (Trailblazer) the outlander are heading toward us!

> **来古士：**奥赫玛正直的公民，哀丽秘榭的白厄，与异乡人开拓者，正在向我们走来！

*(Approach the stage)*

**Phainon:** Aglaea! What happened here?

> **白厄：**阿格莱雅！这是怎么了？

**Aglaea:** I know about the poisonous fangs of that kingsnake through the golden threads. In the past, I would be confident about keeping her in check, but... I admit I am now unable to do so.

> **阿格莱雅：**这条王蛇的毒牙，透过金丝，我已心中有数。过去的我有信心压制住她，但现在…是有些力不从心了。

**Phainon:** What do you mean?

> **白厄：**什么意思？

**Aglaea:** It is no longer about eloquence or arguments. I have been a demigod for far too long that I hardly have any humanity left. Before I realized it, I have become no different from these hypocrites, no longer capable of relating to the people.

> **阿格莱雅：**问题不在辩术。我已成为半神太久，人性将尽，不知不觉间竟也和这些伪君子踏上了相似的道路，离民众越来越远。

**Aglaea:** This has come a lot sooner than I expected, but perhaps it is time. I need you to step up, Phainon, in order for me to retreat behind the scenes.

> **阿格莱雅：**这比我预想中来得更早，或许是时候了：白厄，我需要你站出来，而我退居其后——

**Phainon:** Yes, this is my duty, but...

> **白厄：**我责无旁贷，但……

**Aglaea:** It is far easier for the Council of Elders to fool many than just fool one person. Their so-called "more support" is no more than a ruse to distribute power using words instead of lances.

> **阿格莱雅：**对于元老院，愚弄多数人远比愚弄一个人容易得多。所谓的「更多支持」也只是代替长矛，成了权力分配的手段。

**Aglaea:** There is no glory in winning this debate, but that makes having respect for "humans" so much more priceless. Phainon, you are a Chrysos Heir without flaw, capable of relating deeply with humans...

> **阿格莱雅：**这场辩论本就没有荣誉可言。但也正因此，对「人」的尊重才显得更为珍贵。白厄，你是没有缺陷的黄金裔，对人性体悟至深——

**Phainon:** I appreciate what you've taught me, Aglaea. I hope we'll live up to your expectations and win this debate.

> **白厄：**感谢你的教诲，阿格莱雅。愿我们能不辱使命。

**Caenis:** Pfft. Hahaha...

> **凯妮斯：**呵呵……

**Caenis:** You've done well, Lord Callictis. Let me take over and personally close this debate for today.

> **凯妮斯：**辛苦了，卡勒克提斯阁下。接下来，让我亲自给今日的辩论收尾吧——

**Caenis:** Dearest councilors, let it be known that I sorely miss the blissful times portrayed in lyrical poems. Back then, Amphoreus was home to nearly a thousand cities — each with their own local faith — while gods walked the earth and shared close bonds with humans.

> **凯妮斯：**尊敬的各位议员，我无比怀念那些诗歌里的美好时代。彼时，翁法罗斯存在过近千座不同信仰的城邦，神明行于世间，与凡人相亲。

**Caenis:** Yet in the present, people from all over are forced to leave their homeland, to seek refuge in the holy city, where they hope to rest under Kephale's protection. All of us have long been in the same boat, with no way out of this plight.

> **凯妮斯：**但时至如今，天南地北的避难者离开故乡，奔赴圣城，希望能在刻法勒的庇护下寻得栖息之地。我们所有人早已同舟渡水，退无可退。

**Caenis:** We didn't have a choice in the past, but now... the Council of Elders brings to the table a new possibility. All I have left is one question for you:

> **凯妮斯：**曾经，我们别无选择，但现在，元老院带来了另一种可能。我只剩下最后一个问题——

**Caenis:** Would you rather strive toward the unheard-of "Era Nova," or return to the prosperous "Era Chrysea" of our past? Make your choice!

> **凯妮斯：**诸位，选择吧！是选择谁也不知道的「再创世」，还是选择回到繁荣的「黄金世」？

**Caenis:** Now, which of the two would lead mortals to an ideal future?

> **凯妮斯：**究竟哪一条路，才能为凡人带来理想的明天？

- ▸ No hesitation! I stand by "Era Nova"!

**(Trailblazer):** "Era Nova" is a path of salvation the gods promised. We should not have doubts in the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **开拓者：**「再创世」是众神约定的救赎之道，应当坚定选择逐火之旅。

- ▸ If it's possible to bring "Era Chrysea" back...

**(Trailblazer):** But how are we supposed to send Amphoreus back to "Era Chrysea"?

> **开拓者：**可是，要怎么才能让翁法罗斯回到「黄金世」？

**Phainon:** We all know that "Era Chrysea" is a thing of the past, Lady Caenis. Please enlighten me — how does the Council of Elders plan to turn back time?

> **白厄：**凯妮斯阁下，所有人都知道「黄金世」早已结束。试问，元老院要如何让时光倒流？

**Caenis:** Let the Three Titans of Calamity be born and Era Chrysea is nevermore, but bring demise to those Titans and Era Chrysea will descend upon us again.

> **凯妮斯：**灾厄三泰坦诞生，则黄金世不复。灾厄三泰坦消殒，则黄金世再临。

**Caenis:** Know that the Council of Elders is never one to make empty promises. Look at who we have on our side...

> **凯妮斯：**元老院不会给出空口承诺。诸位请看，站在我身边的人——

**Caenis:** Anaxagoras, one of the Grove's Seven Sages, and Castorice, Aidonia's "Goddess of Death"!

> **凯妮斯：**树庭七贤人之一，阿那克萨戈拉斯，以及哀地里亚的「死亡女神」遐蝶——

**Caenis:** With the help of these two heroes, I can promise all of you that "Era Chrysea" will exist once more!

> **凯妮斯：**有这二位英雄在，我便可向诸位许诺「黄金世」的存在！

**A Member of the Council of Elders:** Aren't they Chrysos Heirs? They're supporting the Council of Elders instead?

> **某位元老院议员：**那不是黄金裔吗？他们也支持元老院？

**Another Member of the Council of Elders:** Hmph, it's clear from the start that these heroes do not get along well in their group. All the more proof that Aglaea isn't fit to speak on behalf of every Chrysos Heir. I am in favor of "Era Chrysea"!

> **另一位元老院议员：**哼，早就知道这群人内部不和，阿格莱雅更没法代表所有黄金裔。我支持「黄金世」！

**A Member of the Council of Elders:** Era Chrysea, Era Chrysea!

> **某位元老院议员：**黄金世！黄金世！

**Members of the Council of Elders:** Era Chrysea, Era Chrysea!

> **元老院议员们：**黄金世！黄金世！

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Phainon:** Castorice? Why are you standing over there...

> **白厄：**遐蝶？为什么你会站在那里……

**Aglaea:** You've finally arrived, Anaxa. So have you... Cas.

> **阿格莱雅：**终于出现了啊，那刻夏。还有…蝶。

**Anaxa:** Don't worry, Aglaea. While I know that you have many queries, could you continue to converse like how "the citizens normally do"?

> **那刻夏：**别心急，阿格莱雅。我知道你有很多疑问，但继续用「公民的方式」交流，可以么？

**Anaxa:** Tell me, dear citizens...

> **那刻夏：**诸位，告诉我——

**Anaxa:** If we successfully retrieve and return Thanatos' Coreflame without anyone passing the Death Titan's trial, what would happen next?

> **那刻夏：**如果我们成功取回并归还塞纳托斯的火种，而无人通过那死亡之泰坦的试炼，会发生什么？

**Caenis:** The answer is simple: We will have a chance to rid the world of "death" and bring Amphoreus back to an era when all beings live forever... We'll usher in a new future that will be entirely different from "the Flame-Chase Journey"!

> **凯妮斯：**很简单：我们将有机会消除世间「死亡」，令翁法罗斯回到万物永生的时代…一个与「逐火之旅」完全不同的崭新未来！

The members of the Council of Elders are discussing fervently.

**Phainon:** Your proposal is much too idealistic. As long as the black tide still exists, how can we go back to the old days?

> **白厄：**这未免太过于理想。只要黑潮还存在一天，我们怎么可能回到过去？

**Anaxa:** Remember what I previously said? Kephale's Coreflame is guarded by the secret power of the Council of Elders. Plus, Phagousa's water curtain will be able to block out any external perceptions.

> **那刻夏：**不，还记得我说过什么吗？刻法勒的火种有元老院秘法管控，法吉娜的水幕也足以隔断外界的感应——

**Anaxa:** Tell me, dear citizens: Have any of you ever seen even a glimpse of the threat that this person is talking about anywhere within the walls of this majestic holy city?

> **那刻夏：**诸位，告诉我——此人口中的威胁，你们可曾在庄严的圣城中见过哪怕一面？

- ▸ But this place will become the Flame Reaver's target sooner or later!

**Caenis:** Oh? Could it be that you heard that from the Flame Reaver themself?

> **凯妮斯：**哦？莫非，盗火行者亲口告诉你了？

- ▸ Isn't this just survivorship bias?

**Caenis:** If we eliminate death, then there's no need for us to fear that Flame Reaver, right?

> **凯妮斯：**若我们消除了死亡，便不必畏惧那盗火者，不是么？

**Caenis:** Citizens, let's loudly sing the blessed hymn: "O beautiful old world, where are you now? O legendary Era Chrysea, return to the mortal realm!"

> **凯妮斯：**人民啊，高唱赞歌吧：「美丽的旧世界，如今在何处？传说的黄金世，快回到人间！」

**Anaxa:** After the destruction of the Three Titans of Calamity, the Flame-Chase Journey will have to end!

> **那刻夏：**灾厄三泰坦消殒后，逐火之旅必须到此结束！

The members of the Council of Elders are cheering in excitement.

**Phainon:** This is bad. The masses are now totally riled up...

> **白厄：**不好，人群彻底被煽动了……

**Aglaea:** Anaxa, pray tell: Everything that you've mentioned hinges on the premise that we can find Thanatos's existence, right?

> **阿格莱雅：**那刻夏，告诉我：你所说的一切前提，是我们*能够*找到塞纳托斯的存在，没错吧？

**Anaxa:** Thank you, Lady Aglaea, for still caring about this city. However, dear citizens, we are truly fortunate and blessed...

> **那刻夏：**感谢阿格莱雅阁下还有余力为这座城市担忧。但是，公民们啊，请听万幸中的万幸——

**Anaxa:** ...For Castorice, Aidonia's "Goddess of Death," will surely find Thanatos under my guidance!

> **那刻夏：**——哀地里亚的「死亡女神」遐蝶，必将在我的指引下找到塞纳托斯！

**Phainon:** What...!?

> **白厄：**什么…？！

**Caenis:** With that, I solemnly promise that the Coreflame Lady Castorice brings back will be personally managed by the Council of Elders.

> **凯妮斯：**由此，我承诺，遐蝶阁下带回的火种，将由元老院亲自管理。

**Caenis:** Thereafter, if we are able to garner the support of all of you at the citizens' assembly, we shall spare no effort in ensuring that the Era Chrysea will return to our world once again!

> **凯妮斯：**而后，若能在公民大会上得到诸位的支持，我们会不竭余力确保黄金世回到人间！

The members of the Council of Elders are cheering in excitement.

**Lygus:** With the balance shattered, the result is clear. By Kephale and Talanton above, as the Theoros, I have observed...

> **来古士：**平衡已被打破，结果已经明朗。刻法勒与塔兰顿在上，以神礼观众之名，我见到——

**Lygus:** Okhema has chosen her destiny. The issue up for discussion at this citizens' assembly shall be modified to the "Suspension of the Flame-Chase Journey." This resolution shall be put up on the Wall of Heroes such that all citizens are aware of the city's way forward.

> **来古士：**奥赫玛选择了她的命运。本次公民大会议题将变更为「暂停逐火之旅」，该决议并将张贴于纪名英雄墙前，全体公民应知晓城邦的前路。

The members of the Council of Elders are cheering in excitement.

**Anaxa:** Aglaea, dear Aglaea... What has caused your heart to soften? You also don't seem to be as quick-witted as before?

> **那刻夏：**阿格莱雅啊，阿格莱雅…是什么让你心肠变软，口舌不似当年锋快了？

**Polyxia:** Have you heard of this tale before?

**Polyxia:** A tale about the Ocean God's treasured pearl — our hometown...

**Polyxia:** It was a seaside town which enjoyed the ocean breeze and glorious, peaceful times thanks to the wisdom of its ruling queen.

**Polyxia:** Until one day, an evil dragon descended upon the town. Its wings were large enough to blot out the sun, while its claws were sharp enough to rip the waves apart...

**Polyxia:** It barged into the tall tower and devoured the queen's beloved daughter. It even settled itself in her bedroom, like a tyrant king.

**Polyxia:** The queen assembled three hundred warriors, who managed to subdue the evil dragon by chaining it up. However, when the queen slit open the dragon's belly, she realized that the princess had long since been digested and all that was left of her was a pile of bones. Devastated, the queen wept for several days...

**Polyxia:** It was then that a devious, smooth-talking scholar arrived at the palace and suggested that the princess could be revived through the use of dragon bone and dragon blood. That liar called this process the secret art of "alchemy."

**Polyxia:** The princess was reborn. The queen was overjoyed, blissfully unaware that the evil dragon's soul still lurked within the princess's body. As revenge, the evil dragon swallowed the poor queen, the glib liar, the courageous warriors, and all of the town's innocent civilians in a single gulp.

**Polyxia:** Worse still, the souls of the deceased were refused entry to the nether realm because they were filled with regret. Forced to roam the mortal realm, these resentful souls ultimately converged into a River of Souls, that would not retreat for a long time.

> **玻吕茜亚：**我…不知道……

**???:** I don't know either. But, regardless of what the answer is, regardless of how we're shaped by others, or even by this world...

> **？？？：**我也无法断言。但…无论答案是什么，无论我们如何被他人、被世界塑造——

**???:** Ever since we were born, the fact that we existed was predestined, and nothing could erase it. This is why I will always, always be by your side...

> **？？？：**自诞生起，我们存在的事实就已注定，任何事物都无法将其抹消。也正因此，我会一直、一直与你同在……

**???:** We're twins after all.

> **？？？：**毕竟，我们是天生的双子嘛。

**Polyxia:** ...

> **玻吕茜亚：**……

**???:** Alright, my dear Polyxia...

> **？？？：**好了，我亲爱的玻吕茜亚……

**???:** It's time to extract my heart and light the flame of Death...

> **？？？：**取出我的心脏，点燃「死亡」的火苗——

**???:** After that, use it to plant the first bud in the prophesied new world.

> **？？？：**然后，用它在预言许诺的新世界里，播下第一枚花蕾吧。

**Calypso:** If I imparted my knowledge of the art of alchemy to you...

> **卡吕普索：**如果，吾将「炼金」之法传习与汝……

**Calypso:** Would you use it to resurrect someone you loved?

> **卡吕普索：**汝…会为珍重之人加以运用，令之起死回生么？

**Polyxia:** ...

> **玻吕茜亚：**……

**Polyxia:** Death is inevitable. In the days to come, I am certain that someone whom I cherish deeply will leave me for good...

> **玻吕茜亚：**凡人终有一死。日后，一定会有我无比珍视的人离我而去……

**Polyxia:** If such a mighty power really existed, I think... I would use it without hesitation.

> **玻吕茜亚：**如果真有如此伟大的力量，我应该…会毫不犹豫地使用它吧。

**Polyxia:** Even now, my answer remains the same.

> **玻吕茜亚：**时至今日，我的回答也未曾改变。

**Polyxia:** This is why, sister, I promise you...

> **玻吕茜亚：**所以，姐姐，我答应你——

**Polyxia:** In the prophesized new world, I shall plant the first bud on your behalf...

> **玻吕茜亚：**在预言许诺的新世界里，我将为你播下第一枚花蕾——

**Polyxia:** Go now, return to the Ocean God's treasured pearl — our hometown.

> **玻吕茜亚：**回去吧，回到海洋之神的掌上明珠，我们的故乡。

**Polyxia:** I shall open wide the entrance to the nether realm and grant you a second life. The living beings on this earth shall respond to my call and lead you to the sacred daylight.

> **玻吕茜亚：**我将为你洞开冥界的大门，赐你以第二次生命。地上的生灵必将响应我的呼声，将你接入神圣的白昼。

**Polyxia:** Remember, do not look back on the land of the dead and do not stop moving. In the new world promised by the prophecy, march toward your new life...

> **玻吕茜亚：**记住，不要向死者的疆域回首，不要停下你的脚步。在预言允诺的新世界里，行向属于你的人生……

*(Screen fades to black)*

**Polyxia:** As for the price of our "equivalent exchange," let me be the one to pay it.

> **玻吕茜亚：**至于「等价交换」的代价…就由我来偿还吧。

**Anaxa:** Everybody, we've come to the end. Unfortunately, I am not compatible with the supreme being, so I was unable to completely fuse their soul into my own body. However, being able to glean all this from them is exciting enough...

> **那刻夏：**诸君，到此为止了。可惜，我与至高之神适性不佳，没能彻底把这家伙的灵魂熔于己身。不过，能从中窥探到这些，也足够令人振奋——

**Anaxa:** I'm certain of this now: Before the Titans that we are well acquainted with were born, a group of heroes roamed the land. These heroes followed a prophecy and took possession of divine authorities one by one, until they eventually became the pantheon of the new world...

> **那刻夏：**不会错：在世人熟知的泰坦诞生前，也有一群英雄行走大地。他们听凭预言，逐一接过神权，最终成为了新世界的众神……

**Anaxa:** ...Just like the Flame-Chase Journey of today. Amphoreus' history is cyclical in nature.

> **那刻夏：**正如今日的逐火之旅——翁法罗斯的历史是一种「轮回」。

**Cerces:** Heh, what a startling revelation... This means that the demigods who bear the Coreflames will also become the new Titans at the World's End, thereby shaping a new world?

> **瑟希斯：**呵，真是十足令人震撼哪…所以背负「火种」的半神，亦将在世界尽头成为新的泰坦，再造天地？

**Anaxa:** I believe that this is the truth behind the "Era Nova" as mentioned in the prophecy.

> **那刻夏：**我相信，这就是神谕中「再创世」的真相。

**Castorice:** This is... utterly unbelievable.

> **遐蝶：**实在是…令人难以置信。

**Castorice:** Besides, those twins in the past... Even if they really are Thanatos, their whereabouts are still unknown even today...

> **遐蝶：**而且，过去那对双子…即便她们真是塞纳托斯本尊，如今也依旧不知去向……

**Anaxa:** Are their whereabouts truly unknown? Didn't Polyxia mention that seaside town — the birthplace of alchemy and the tale of the dragon...

> **那刻夏：**当真不知去向么？那位玻吕茜亚说过了吧，诞生了炼金与巨龙童话的海滨小城……

**Anaxa:** In our world, there exists a city which perfectly matches that description: Styxia.

> **那刻夏：**斯缇科西亚——在我们的世界里，也有一座城邦完美符合这描述。

**Castorice:** But the people there worship Phagousa and bear no relation to Thanatos. I have also never been to that place, as in the time of Aidonia... it had long turned into underwater ruins.

> **遐蝶：**可那里信仰法吉娜，和塞纳托斯并无关联。我也从未去过那里，在哀地里亚的时光…它早已是一片被淹没的废墟了。

**Anaxa:** ...Take this.

> **那刻夏：**…拿去。

**Castorice:** This is...

> **遐蝶：**这是……

**Anaxa:** The Nousporists' masterpiece: the Philosopher's Stone. It was made by transmuting all of the Sages' wisdom into a single entity, with their flesh and blood as its foundation.

> **那刻夏：**贤者之石，「智种学派」的杰作，以贤人的血肉为根基，将其全数智慧炼就而成的实体。

**Anaxa:** Remember? I've mentioned before that the art of alchemy is all about conversion, tempering, and ascension. This process also applies to the pursuit of truth. Regardless of how unbelievable this sounds, the truths of the world have been refined from the impurities.

> **那刻夏：**记得吗？我说过炼金是转化、淬炼和扬升的艺术，这也同样适用于追求真理。无论多么难以置信，世界的真相已从杂质中精炼而成。

**Anaxa:** Think about the story of the dragon. This stone will lead you to your real hometown, allowing you to reform the door to the nether realm and discover Thanatos's fate.

> **那刻夏：**想想巨龙童话——这枚石子定将指引你回到真正的故乡，重铸冥界大门，找到塞纳托斯的下落。

**Cerces:** You still look as confident in your victory as ever, but what if your conjecture is wrong?

> **瑟希斯：**汝还是一副胜券在握的样子哪。可要是汝猜错了呢？

**Anaxa:** Simple. I shall merely admit to the entire world that my experiment was a failure.

> **那刻夏：**那也很简单，向全世界承认实验失败了就是。

**Anaxa:** However, my line of thinking will not fail because I have already planted the seeds of doubt into the hearts of the masses.

> **那刻夏：**但我的思想绝不会失败，因为我已将「怀疑」的种子播在每个人心中。

**Anaxa:** Actions speak louder than words, Castorice. With the assembly about to begin, don't forget the promise you've made.

> **那刻夏：**用行动证明吧，遐蝶。决议马上要开始了，别忘了你的承诺。

**Castorice:** I've already searched for so long, I'm willing to try one more time. While we converse, (Trailblazer)'s life is also slowly slipping away...

> **遐蝶：**我已走过漫长的寻索，不介意再做一次尝试。在我们交谈的当下，开拓者的生命也在一分一秒地流逝……

**Castorice:** But before I leave, I have another question: What exactly did we witness just now...?

> **遐蝶：**但临行前，我还有一个问题：我们方才所见的一切究竟是…？

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** That was the world imprinted in Kephale's soul.

> **那刻夏：**那是烙印在刻法勒灵魂中的世界。

**Anaxa:** If we are to prove its existence, you must take the final step. Go, do not disappoint me.

> **那刻夏：**若想证明它，也必须由你写下最后一步。去吧，别让我失望。

**Castorice:** ...I understand.

> **遐蝶：**…我明白了。

**Cerces:** Hey, child of humanity...

> **瑟希斯：**喂，人子啊……

**Anaxa:** Don't get antsy, I still remember the question you posed to me.

> **那刻夏：**别心急，我一直都记得你的问题。

**Anaxa:** As a scholar, I do not wish to offer up ambiguous conclusions. Let's discuss this topic again when she manages to find the evidence.

> **那刻夏：**我是个学者，不想也不能给出模棱两可的结论。等她找到证据，我们再聊这事。

**Cerces:** I merely wanted to ask: Was that stone transmuted from your heart?

> **瑟希斯：**吾只是想说：那石子乃是汝用心脏炼成而来的罢？

**Cerces:** If so, rebirth... is no longer an option for you.

> **瑟希斯：**汝…已绝无法回生了哪。

**Anaxa:** Why not? When I was a child, I lost someone dear to me. When I was a teenager, I lost my faith. A while back, although you saved my life, I also lost my soul in the process...

> **那刻夏：**有何不可？我年幼时失去了至亲，年少时放弃信仰，不久前虽被你捡回条命，却还是丢了灵魂……

**Anaxa:** Now, I'm merely forsaking my body. What's the big deal?

> **那刻夏：**现在不过是又抛弃了肉身而已，何必大惊小怪？

**Cerces:** Why are you always finding ways to die? Isn't it good to be alive?

> **瑟希斯：**为何偏要急着赴死，活着不好么？

**Anaxa:** This is but a necessary sacrifice on my path to transform into the purest of them all.

> **那刻夏：**不过是向至纯粹者转化时，必要的牺牲罢了。

**Cerces:** Heh, can someone who's dead even claim to be the purest of them all? Furthermore, if your hypothesis is proven correct, does it not just confirm that the myths are true and thereby invalidate all your theories?

> **瑟希斯：**呵，死者能称作至纯粹者么？而且，倘若汝那猜想得以证明，岂不反坐实了神话的真实性，令汝的理论前功尽弃？

**Anaxa:** Heh...

> **那刻夏：**呵……

**Anaxa:** No, it's just the opposite. If my hypothesis is correct...

> **那刻夏：**不，恰恰相反。如果我的猜想真能应验……

**Anaxa:** It means that I can actually ascend to godhood and reshape this absurd world that you all have created.

> **那刻夏：**那也就代表——我完全可以登上神座，重塑你们创造的荒谬世界了。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

*(Quick Read)*

Letter from the Phantom Thief Cipher

Dear Princess Homebody: Walking through the busy garden, I discovered the remarkable gifts crafted by Princess Homebody. They are truly splendid, and I am most impressed. I'm also taken with your charming presents prepared for fellow Chrysos Heirs. In your right parlor, I observed the beautiful felt dolls, the beige-and-lavender striped tapestry, the classic Aidonian tripod table, and in the left parlor, the jewelry and exquisite art pieces meant as gifts. This time, I've claimed only this modest gift. I fear it would attract tasteless little thieves, so I shall safeguard it for now. Naturally, I've left appropriate payment — a single coin. If the price displeases you, feel free to negotiate its return. Though should that happen, I won't be satisfied with merely one coin. Should you wish to find me, visit the Marmoreal Market. There lies a Golden Short Spear I've left — you know what to do. It shall guide you to me! And before you ask — I won't accept any questions. Please forgive this small intrusion and accept my highest regards. P.S.: Who displays biological specimens at home? They're quite unsettling. You might want to store them away when entertaining guests. P.P.S.: Despite your considerable investment in the Aidonian Ossuary hanging in your parlor, I regret to inform you it's a fake. The original was craftily switched by those cunning scholars of the Grove of Epiphany. The full story can be found in the yet-unpublished Memoirs of Cifera. Sincerely, Kitty Phantom Thief

To retrieve the gift and teach Kitty Phantom Thief a lesson, you head to the market and find the short spear she left behind.

*(Obtain Letter from the Phantom Thief Cipher, Cipher's Coin)*

Letter from the Phantom Thief Cipher (Continued)

Dear Princess Homebody and Gray Mystery: You probably guessed already — I had no intention of showing up at the pack beast workshop. This means, of course, I had no plans to return the gift there either... This was just a warm-up for our little game of cat and mouse. But surely you've realized that the Kitty Phantom Thief never cared about *stealing a mere trinket*, right? — No, what I'm really after is your hearts. Just kidding. I have no interest in your hearts. If Princess Homebody can track my path, then by all means try. I'll be waiting at the end. And rest assured, we'll meet face-to-face this time. P.S. Gray Mystery, where did you get that Dew of Divine Blood? Don't tell me it's a gift from the seamstress? I'll taste it for you. Sincerely, Kitty Phantom Thief

*(Obtain Letter from the Phantom Thief Cipher, Cipher's Coin)*

Letter from the Phantom Thief Cipher (Continued Again)

Gray Mystery: These formal addresses at the start of letters are such a hassle, and you've already read three useless phantom thief letters. I've left Princess Homebody's gift here — take good care of it. Remember, skip the baths and come straight to Okhema's city gate to find me. You know I have no patience. Come late and I'll leave without you, clear? P.S. Yes, I'm the one who tucked you in — you're welcome. Sincerely, Kitty Phantom Thief

**Cipher:** That's the ferry fare for entering the nether realm. However, I honestly won't ask you to pay this time. I'll pay. And then we'll just have to go through the motions...

> **遐蝶：**哎……

**Cipher:** You can't catch the wind by waiting. You only live once, so make the most of every moment you have.

## 120　Death, Dripping Like Morning Dew　死亡啊，如朝露滑落指尖

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Aidonia's Cloister　|　角色："Elder" Amunet; Castorice

### Speak with "Elder" Amunet

> *You remember "Elder" Amunet as well as Aidonia, this city-state that worshiped Thanatos. This is where your memory begins. Of course, you also remember how you became an executioner and a Maiden of War.*

**Castorice:** I... I can't do it.

> **遐蝶：**我…做不到。

**Castorice:** Their voices... They pierce my ears... The pleading, the thanks...

> **遐蝶：**他们的声音…哀求也好，感谢也罢，全都那样刺耳……

**"Elder" Amunet:** Ahh. I know that feeling, child. I was once as lost as you are now.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**呵，孩子，我明白。我也曾有过如你这般的迷茫。

**"Elder" Amunet:** But you must respect those walking towards death, just like how you should respect yourself as the Holy Maiden granting humans glory and liberation.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**但你应当敬重赴死之人。同样地，身为予人以光荣和解脱的圣女，你应当尊敬自己。

**Castorice:** What you're asking me to do is no better than the task of an executioner, and yet you want me to respect myself?

> **遐蝶：**您要我做的事，根本和处刑人无异，我要如何尊敬自己？

**Castorice:** No matter the titles, I am still taking away human lives...

> **遐蝶：**无论拥有怎样的名目，这都是在夺走他人的性命……

**"Elder" Amunet:** This won't be the first time that Aidonia's fanatical netherwarriors have faced death. On the contrary, recruits new to the battlefield often run away from their first fight.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**狂热如哀地里亚的冥军战士，接纳死亡也不在一朝一夕。第一次踏上战场时，新兵往往都会变成逃兵。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Death is the mightiest and gravest power in this world. The fact that you can wield it means you are deserving of respect.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**因为死亡是天地间至高至难的力量。而你能将其掌握，你便配得上这敬意。

**Castorice:** That's such a... twisted reason...

> **遐蝶：**这…太扭曲了……

**"Elder" Amunet:** ...Then, let me tell you a story.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**…听我讲个故事吧。

**"Elder" Amunet:** In Aidonia, the greatswords commonly used by executioners are called "Drakonian" in memory of Drakon, an elderly executioner.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**为了纪念德拉孔，一位老行刑人，哀地里亚行刑官常用的重剑被称作「德拉孔尼安」。

**"Elder" Amunet:** The sword has three air holes along its body. If the sword is swung down swiftly and accurately, the holes would make no sound, and the executed would also depart this world quickly and painlessly...

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**它的剑身上有三道气孔。如果这柄剑能不偏不倚，迅速落下，三处气孔便不会发出任何声音，受刑者也能在一瞬之间离开人世……

**"Elder" Amunet:** But should the executioner have any hesitation, the holes will produce a piercing whistle. On that cue, the screams of the executed are sure to follow.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**但如果有所犹豫，它们便会发出刺耳的噪声。随后，行刑官也必定会听见受刑者的哀嚎。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Every executioner after Drakon is to hone their sword skills every day in preparation of future executions. Every swing of their blade must be more resolute than the last, for failing to do so will bring pain.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**自德拉孔起，行刑者必须日夜磨砺处决的剑技。他们每一次挥剑都要比上一次更为坚决，以免痛苦滋生。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Pain for the dying... as well as pain for themselves.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**既是为了赴死者，也是为了自己。

**Castorice:** ...No. I can never get used to this.

> **遐蝶：**…恕我无法对这种行径感到麻木。

**"Elder" Amunet:** You misunderstand, child. I am not telling you to get used to it.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**不，孩子。我不想你对此习以为常。

**"Elder" Amunet:** I'm asking you to consider the source of the dead's suffering, and use that knowledge to fortify your future touches with more and more determination.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**我只要你思考死者为何而痛苦。然后让你的每一次触碰，都比上一次更加坚决。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Go walk with the dead, and love them as you love life... Then you'll no longer hear those noises.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**与死者同行，敬爱他们如同你敬爱生命…你将再也听不见那些杂音。

### Execute the unknown death-row prisoner

**Unknown Death-Row Prisoner:** Please... Don't kill me... I don't want to die....

> **不知名的死囚：**别杀我…求求你，我不想死……

- ▸ No. I can't do it.

**"Elder" Amunet:** This is your duty. Remember, consider the source of the dead's suffering, then give them the respect they deserve.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**这是你的义务。记住，思考死者为何而痛苦，然后向他们献上敬意。

**Unknown Death-Row Prisoner:** Th—Thank you... I beg you... Don't listen to her...

> **不知名的死囚：**谢、谢谢你…别听她的，求求你……

- ▸ A gentle touch.
- ▸ I—I can't...

**"Elder" Amunet:** If you cannot learn to accept death, then Aidonia has no place for you.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**若你无法学会接纳死亡，便不能为哀地里亚所接纳。

**"Elder" Amunet:** This is your last chance. Do it.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**这是最后一次机会：动手。

**Unknown Death-Row Prisoner:** No, please... I don't want to die... Please, I'm begging you...

> **不知名的死囚：**不…我不想死…求求你了，求求你……

- ▸ A gentle touch.

**Unknown Death-Row Prisoner:** No... No...

> **不知名的死囚：**不、不……

*(Screen fades to black)*

You gently touch the prisoner's forehead with your finger. His face is pale, his eyes flicker with fear, and his body trembles uncontrollably.

Is it because of fear, anger, and hatred, or is your so-called merciful Touch of Death simply a lie?

The dark purple mottles spread like petals across the death row inmate's face, then seemed to be submerged by springwater, flooding his body. His trembling ceased, and the cries that had once begged for mercy slowly turned into a sigh-like whimper, as he murmurs to himself.

Quickly, everything falls into a dead silence, turning into pitch-black dew that scatters with the wind. You hear your own heartbeat drown out the sound of heavy breathing.

**"Elder" Amunet:** May you gain some insights through his sacrifice.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**希望斯人牺牲能令你有所感悟。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** Is... this all?

> **遐蝶：**这样…就可以了吗？

**"Elder" Amunet:** No. Come with me.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**不，跟我来。

**"Elder" Amunet:** This is just the beginning of your life as the Maiden of War.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**作为「督战圣女」，你的人生才刚刚开始。

### Speak with "Elder" Amunet and learn about the duties of an executioner

> *You remember "Elder" Amunet as well as Aidonia, the city-state that worshiped Thanatos. This is where your memory begins. The "Elder" does not wish that you become a merciless executioner, but that you would fully understand the weight of life and death, then carry out with utmost respect the duty with which you were born.*

for granting me release

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**我老了，必须响应塞纳托斯的召唤，去往它的国度了……

**"Elder" Amunet:** ...The final resting place of every Aidonian.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**这…也是每一个哀地里亚人的归宿。

**Castorice:** You're not that old yet... You still have many, many years ahead of you...

> **遐蝶：**您还远没有衰老到那种地步，还有许多岁月可活……

**"Elder" Amunet:** No no... I'm already "dead."

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**不…我已经「死」了。

**"Elder" Amunet:** For I have come to fear... my declining health and impending death.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**我对加诸此身的衰老，还有临近的死亡…开始感到恐惧了。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Castorice, will you give me one last chance to charge towards fear and prove that I am no coward?

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**所以，遐蝶，你能给我最后一次机会，让我向那恐惧发起冲锋，证明自己并非懦夫。

**Castorice:** I...

> **遐蝶：**我……

**Castorice:** I... I can't take this anymore...

> **遐蝶：**我…再也受不了了……

**"Elder" Amunet:** Child, it was I who brought you here from the frosty plains and set your farcical fate in motion. It was also I, chief executioner of Aidonia, who forced the heavy duty of executing others onto you.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**孩子，是我从冰原中带回了你，令你荒唐的命运开始转动。身为哀地里亚的行刑官之首，也是我将「处刑」这道重任不由分说，强压在你肩头。

**"Elder" Amunet:** I've always known... that you have doubts and even anger about my faith... and about me...

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**我深知，你始终都对我…对我们信仰的一切感到困惑，甚至愤怒……

**Castorice:** I... never had a choice...

> **遐蝶：**我…别无选择……

**"Elder" Amunet:** Indeed. We essentially abducted you.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**是啊，我们的所作所为与绑架无异。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Yet you can end any sinful life with just a touch of your fingers... Shouldn't it be easy for you escape from here?

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**所以，只要你轻轻一触，一条罪恶的生命便将就此凋零。逃离对你而言，不是易如反掌？

**Castorice:** ...I'll never do that.

> **遐蝶：**我…不可能这么做。

**Castorice:** It doesn't matter how lightly the Aidonians take death, or how readily they accept it... I simply can't do it.

> **遐蝶：**就算哀地里亚人再怎么不加思考，视死亡如儿戏，但…我做不到。

**"Elder" Amunet:** Haha... I knew I was right about you.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**呵呵，所以…我果然没看错你。

**Castorice:** Wh—What do you mean?

> **遐蝶：**什么…？

**"Elder" Amunet:** I've taught you and cared for you as my own daughter all this time... because of this blessing that's been with you since your birth. I know no one else in the world would understand the weight of life and death more than you do.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**我一直把你当成女儿，悉心培养，因为你生来受赐如此祝福，这世上没有人比你更明白生命和死亡的重量。

**"Elder" Amunet:** In the eyes of an Aidonian, "Death" is the end of all life, a destination we will all eventually reach. Humans should not resist it, but learn how to face it.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**若用哀地里亚人的眼光看待「死亡」：它是一切生命的终点，是我们每个人的必由之路。人不应抗拒，而要学会如何迎接它。

**"Elder" Amunet:** You can only live your life to the fullest after understanding this principle.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**只有在彻悟这个道理后，你才能好好活下去。

**"Elder" Amunet:** And when you do, even if you eventually leave Aidonia's protection... even if you must carry the curse forever... you can live with your head held high, like everyone else.

> **「长老」阿蒙内特：**哪怕你终将离开哀地里亚的庇护——哪怕永远肩负着诅咒——你也可以像普通人一样，昂首挺胸地活下去。

**Castorice:** No, how can I—

> **遐蝶：**不，我怎么能……

**"Elder" Amunet:** Come now. It's time. I have already taught you all that I know...

**"Elder" Amunet:** Show me that you are a fitting Holy Maiden... or at least, that you possess the resolve to become one.

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**我最后一次服从她的指示，轻轻触碰了眼前的老人。

**Castorice:** The executioner who had seen too much death died the same way as everyone else... A gasp for air, a wistful look in her eyes, and a barely perceptible tremor in her fingertips.

> **遐蝶：**这位见惯了死亡的处刑人，她的消逝与常人并无不同：喉头的呜咽，眼中的遗憾，还有…微不可见的颤抖从指尖传来。

**Castorice:** Elder Amunet, the person who tried to teach me about "Death," ultimately vanished like dew in the morning mist. She spent her life embodying the beliefs of the Aidonians, and when death finally came, she embraced it exactly as their doctrine had taught...

> **遐蝶：**阿蒙内特，试图向我传授「死亡」的人，也最终如朝露消失在晨雾中。她一生都在贯彻哀地里亚人的信仰，而当死亡真正来临，她也如那信仰所述……

**Castorice:** I have nothing but respect for her.

> **遐蝶：**除去敬畏，再无其他。

> **遐蝶：**我在哀地里亚城外的沼泽间，为她树立了一座衣冠冢，将她留在我亲手送葬的一众死者间。然后，为了觐见塞纳托斯，我再次踏上了流浪。

**Castorice:** It was a long, long journey... I heard so many discussions about death along the way. "Life finds meaning in death." "The world improves because there is an end"...

> **遐蝶：**那是一段很长、很长的路，我听过太多对死亡的议论。「生命从死亡中寻获意义」、「万物因终结而前进」……

**Castorice:** It seems the most terrible darkness in this world. Everyone talks about its heavy weight. Some resist it, some worship it, and some even revere it...

> **遐蝶：**仿佛世间最可怕的幽暗，所有人都在讲述它的沉重，或是抵抗、或是崇拜、或是敬畏……

**Castorice:** How did a disappeared Titan become the deepest fear of humankind...

> **遐蝶：**一尊去向不明的泰坦，何以成为人们心中最深的恐惧……

**Castorice:** ...And why do they haunt my dreams?

> **遐蝶：**…为何，会在我的梦中萦绕不去？

**Castorice:** I continued my travels to seek an answer. Initially, I was running away. Then, I started searching for it. And now, at the end...

> **遐蝶：**为了这个问题的答案，我继续旅途。起初是逃离，而后变成了寻索，最后……

> **遐蝶：**我想知道，是否，我的触碰，我的拥抱…并非只能剥夺……
