> **老去的女孩：**是…遐蝶姐姐吗？

**Old Woman:** All of them... have gone.

> **老去的女孩：**大家…都已经走了

**Old Woman:** heeding Death's summon.

> **老去的女孩：**响应死神召唤

**Old Woman:** crossing into their domain.

> **老去的女孩：**去往它的国度了

**Old Woman:** I...

> **老去的女孩：**我也…

**Old Woman:** must go too.

> **老去的女孩：**是时候了

**Old Woman:** I don't fear it,

> **老去的女孩：**我不害怕

**Old Woman:** just... I have some regrets.

> **老去的女孩：**只是…有些遗憾

**Old Woman:** Watching people leave this world again and again...

> **老去的女孩：**一直在看护人们的死亡

**Old Woman:** it must be

> **老去的女孩：**你一定

**Old Woman:** lonely for you.

> **老去的女孩：**很寂寞吧

**Old Woman:** You must want to desperately embrace them

> **老去的女孩：**也会想在离别的时候

**Old Woman:** before they go.

> **老去的女孩：**尽情拥抱别人吧

**Castorice:** I can't...

> **遐蝶：**不行…

**Castorice:** these hands...

> **遐蝶：**这双手…

**Castorice:** they're too cold...

> **遐蝶：**很冰冷…

**Old Woman:** But big sister Castorice...

> **老去的女孩：**遐蝶姐姐的手…

**Old Woman:** your hands are so warm.

> **老去的女孩：**明明很温暖啊

**Old Woman:** Just like anyone else's.

> **老去的女孩：**就像普通人一样

**Old Woman:** So go on, don't hold back,

> **老去的女孩：**尽情拥抱吧

**Old Woman:** give me a warm hug.

**Castorice:** Can hands like these...

**Castorice:** really be warm?

> **遐蝶：**我…我也可以。

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Castorice:** On the day that Oronyx's Trial ended, you must have had many questions, (Trailblazer).

**Castorice:** I felt just as confused. Why, as someone from beyond the sky, were you so cruelly dragged into Amphoreus's fate?

**Castorice:** ...I wished I could take action, but no matter how hard I try, I cannot simply interact with the living as ordinary people do.

> **遐蝶：**我度过了比大部分人更漫长的时光，就算不像阿格莱雅大人那样善于营生，也还算有些积蓄。

**Castorice:** Even when we brushed against each other unintentionally in the Grove... and it was obvious that nothing had happened...

**Castorice:** When an almost unattainable hope finally welled up in my heart...

> **遐蝶：**那…那就百分之九十…不，八十吧……

**Castorice:** When I thought that perhaps my touch would no longer bring sorrow...

**Castorice:** Fate declared to me: It wasn't that the curse of "Death" had finally been lifted...

> **遐蝶：**更换接头地的原因是……

**Castorice:** ...but that your soul no longer belonged to this world... and there was nothing left for my hands to take. Nothing.

**Castorice:** And did I really think this time would be any different? It's not that I don't understand this reality — just that I'm unwilling to accept it.

**Castorice:** Having walked such a long road, I am still unable to find the answer to life and death. I am resigned to eternal despair...

**Castorice:** Anything I wish to hold on to, "Death" will inevitably wrest from my grasp...

**Castorice:** And from that moment on, I lacked the courage to confirm it again... Until Oronyx laid bare the cruel truth before us.

## 121　Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls　摇船啊，请载我横渡冥流

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Dragonbone City" Styxia　|　角色：Trailblazer; Castorice; Mem; Cipher; Centurion; Aglaea; Court Scholar; Vault Officer; "Elder" Amunet; Eulogistic Butterfly; Alchemist; Queen of Styxia; Mydei; Trianne; Pollux　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Pathstrider, Set Sail Upon the River of Souls'' completed

### Speak to Castorice

> *Styxia, the City-State of Dragons and Waves... At present, the River of Souls has encircled this pitiful city. The Philosopher's Stone provided by Anaxagoras and the art of alchemy can summon the Titan lurking with the River of Souls, and the alchemical materials should be found in the city as well.*

**Castorice:** Are you feeling alright?

> **遐蝶：**阁下…还好吧？

- ▸ Ugh... I swear she was speeding...
- ▸ Where am I? Are we still in Amphoreus?

**Castorice:** She's the demigod of Trickery. When she flips the Coin of Whimsy into the air, she gains incredible speed that lets her travel anywhere in an instant.

> **遐蝶：**她是「诡计」的半神，当她将「翻飞之币」高高抛起，便会获得无可比拟的神速，来去只在一瞬间。

**Castorice:** ...Just like how she clearly had no intention of lingering here for even a second longer. We can only depend on ourselves for the rest of the way.

> **遐蝶：**就像现在，她似乎没有多待一秒的打算…接下来的路，看来只能靠我们自己了。

**Mem:** Don't worry. Mem's here to help!

> **迷迷：**…还可以靠人家哦！

- ▸ So you came!
- ▸ Can you teleport too?

**Mem:** Being cute isn't my only forte. I'm strong, too!

> **迷迷：**毕竟人家不止外表可爱，还是实力派呢。

- ▸ Mem, did you pay your fare?

**Mem:** Um... I don't think I even meet the height requirement for the ticket, so I won't need one, right?

> **迷迷：**啊，这个…人家小小一只，身高不够，可以免票吧？

**Castorice:** This is Styxia, the city of dragons and waves... Anaxa hypothesizes that the missing Thanatos is somewhere in these ruins.

> **遐蝶：**这里就是「斯缇科西亚」，巨龙与海浪的城邦…依照老师的猜想，不知去向的塞纳托斯就隐藏在这片废墟中。

**Castorice:** This is the land of their birth... Or perhaps of their death.

> **遐蝶：**这…就是它的诞生之地，或长眠之地。

- ▸ Are there any other clues?
- ▸ How would we find them?
- ▸ Aha, I know this one! They returned whence they came!

**Castorice:** Anaxa had used a secret alchemical art to temporarily fuse his soul with Cerces and Kephale. And when he was on the verge of death, he was pulled by Thanatos and caught a glimpse of the flowing River of Souls.

> **遐蝶：**老师曾用炼金秘法，成功将自己的灵魂与瑟希斯、刻法勒短暂熔合，又在弥留之际受到塞纳托斯招引，窥见了流淌的冥河。

**Castorice:** This alchemy, considered a sacrilegious act, might actually make the souls of mortals and Titans attract each other...

> **遐蝶：**被称作「渎神之举」的炼成，也许能使凡人的灵魂与泰坦相互吸引……

**Castorice:** Even though Thanatos has been missing for a thousand years and even their followers haven't been able to find them... perhaps that special attraction could summon them here.

> **遐蝶：**尽管塞纳托斯在过去的千年几乎杳无音讯，也从未被信徒寻获。但那引力…或许能将它召唤至此。

**Mem:** Oh! So are we going to use that Philosopher's Stone to... conjure the Titan?

> **迷迷：**噢！所以，我们要用那块「贤者之石」，在这里把泰坦给…变出来？

**Castorice:** I guess that's the only way. The only leads we have now are the name of this city and the fairy tale about the dragon...

> **遐蝶：**想必是这样了。只不过，眼下的线索仅有这座城邦的名字，还有巨龙童话……

**Mem:** Thanatos is even more mysterious than I am...

> **迷迷：**塞纳托斯，真是比人家还神秘呢……

**Castorice:** Indeed. Even one as erudite as Anaxa could only point us to this place, and no further. We will be entering the unknown as soon as we take our first steps... But that won't be the first time in my long journeys.

> **遐蝶：**是啊，老师的智慧也只能照亮我们脚下这方寸土地。只要踏出第一步，前路就是未知的浓雾…但在我漫长的旅行中，这也不是第一次了。

**Castorice:** I believe that...

> **遐蝶：**我相信……

**Castorice:** Rather than standing still, taking action is by far the better option. Perhaps fate might take pity on us today and show some compassion.

> **遐蝶：**比起犹豫，还是启程更好。也许就在今天，命运会恻隐一时，抛下些许仁慈。

*(Unlock Achievement Cruel Night Oppresses Unfortunate Mortals)*

### Search Dragonbone City and find the entrance to the nether realm

*(Approach the bridge)*

**???:** Go back whence you came... Go back...

> **？？？：**回头吧…回头吧……

**???:** Ahead lies the realm of the dead...

> **？？？：**前方是那死者的疆域哪……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Mem:** What a beautiful butterfly. Is it talking to us?

> **迷迷：**好漂亮的蝴蝶，是在和我们说话吗？

**Castorice:** This is a Eulogistic Butterfly. They are found in places where many have met their demise.

> **遐蝶：**这是「亡语蝶」，死难者众多的地方，总有它们翩飞的身影。

**Castorice:** In Aidonia, some think of them as manifestations of Thanatos' benevolence. By turning the lingering obsessions of the deceased into these creatures, it is as if the Titan has made life into a specimen.

> **遐蝶：**在哀地里亚，有些人认为它们是塞纳托斯的仁慈。它将死者的最后一道执念，化作这些生灵，就像是为生命制作了标本。

**Castorice:** Others think of them as Thanatos' messengers that guide the departed to the nether realm.

> **遐蝶：**也有另一些人认为，它们本就是塞纳托斯的信使，引渡亡魂去往冥界。

**Mem:** The second theory sounds more believable. After all, the things they're saying are rather... um, direct?

> **迷迷：**感觉后一种说法更可信呢，毕竟它的发言还是有点太…嗯，直抒胸臆？

**Castorice:** Perhaps. But the fact that we're able to hear their whispers proves that we're fast approaching the realm of the dead.

> **遐蝶：**或许吧。但能听见它们窃窃私语，至少说明我们确实在向死者的世界靠近。

### Speak with the Styxian Spirit of the Dead

*(Go through the door)*

**Castorice:** These shadowy figures... are they illusions reflected by the River of Souls?

> **遐蝶：**这些人影…是冥河倒映出的幻象吗？

*(Listen to the two children, optional)*

**Young Girl:** Mommy told us to go ahead first, but she's still not here yet.

> **年幼的女孩：**妈妈说让我们先走，可她一直没有过来。

**Young Boy:** One, two, three... I don't even know how many days have passed since then.

> **年幼的男孩：**一，二，三…已经数不过来天数了。

**Young Girl:** Perhaps Mommy will be back once you finish counting the days on all ten fingers.

> **年幼的女孩：**也许数完十根手指，妈妈就回来了。

**Young Boy:** One, two, three... It's been much longer than ten days since we left Mommy.

> **年幼的男孩：**一，二，三…早就超过十根手指的天数了。

**Castorice:** Strange... For reflections in the River of Souls, they seem oddly... animated.

> **遐蝶：**奇怪，要说是冥河的倒影，似乎又太过…灵动。

*(Listen to the two blacksmiths, optional)*

**Apprentice:** But master... Did that lady really lie to us?

> **学徒：**可是，师父…那位姐姐，难道骗了我们？

**Apprentice:** The centurion personally instructed us to inlay the inscription into his shield.

> **学徒：**百夫长亲自嘱咐过，要把那个铭刻镶嵌在他的盾牌上。

**Apprentice:** But now she's taken it away...

> **学徒：**可现在，却被她拿走了……

**Blacksmith:** My child... that shield can never be cast, and there's no need for it anymore...

> **铁匠：**孩子啊…盾牌永远不可能铸成，也不需要了……

**Blacksmith:** ...

> **铁匠：**……

**Blacksmith:** Come, child, what is the name of that centurion again? Actually... Never mind, forget I said that.

> **铁匠：**来，孩子，百夫长叫什么名字？…不，忘了我刚才的话吧。

**Blacksmith:** I'll wait with you for her to come back. When that happens, you'll be able to keep making shields.

> **铁匠：**我陪你一起等她回来。那时候，你就可以继续打造盾牌了。

**Mem:** Don't be afraid, (Trailblazer). Let's open our eyes wide... together!

> **迷迷：**开拓者，别害怕，和我一起…睁大眼睛！

*(Approach the merchant)*

**Marketplace Merchant:** Do you... want to buy any fish?

> **集市商人：**你们…要买鱼吗？

**Mem:** Huh? He can see us?

> **迷迷：**咦？他能看见我们？

**Marketplace Merchant:** Wait... More living people?

> **集市商人：**怎么…又是活着的人？

**Marketplace Merchant:** I'm not selling my fish to... living people. You lot are too... shameless. Buying fish without paying... If I ever see her again, I will... I will...

> **集市商人：**活着的人…不卖，你们…太无耻了。买了鱼却不付账…等我再见到她，我非得…我非得……

**Castorice:** Could it be that... You're all dead souls that never achieved rest?

> **遐蝶：**难道说，你们…是未能安息的灵魂？

**Marketplace Merchant:** The nether realm... Does not exist...

> **集市商人：**冥界…子虚乌有……

**Marketplace Merchant:** Titan... Rejects... You... Me...

> **集市商人：**泰坦…拒绝…你…我……

**Marketplace Merchant:** So... do you want to buy any fish?

> **集市商人：**所以…要买鱼吗？

**Mem:** But there aren't any "fish" nearby...

> **迷迷：**可是，附近也没有「鱼」呀……

**Marketplace Merchant:** Are the fish... gone? The people... are gone, too...

> **集市商人：**鱼…不见了？人…也不见了……

**Marketplace Merchant:** I can only... keep selling fish. So... do you... want to buy any fish?

> **集市商人：**只能…卖鱼。你…要买鱼吗？

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** Apologies, I don't have much in the way of savings, but perhaps there's another way for me to release you from your suffering.

> **遐蝶：**对不起，我的积蓄所剩无多。但或许有别的办法，能解脱你的痛苦。

**Castorice:** Please relax. May you fall asleep peacefully... amid a gentle field of flowers.

> **遐蝶：**请放松。愿你在温柔的花乡中…平静睡去。

**Marketplace Merchant:** This... this...

> **集市商人：**这…这是……

**Castorice:** I knew it. These people aren't illusions. They're the souls of the deceased.

> **遐蝶：**果然，这些人不是幻影，而是死难者的魂灵。

**Castorice:** They're supposed to reach the end of the west wind by floating down the River of Souls... Yet they have floundered upon the shoals of the stream, unable to move forward.

> **遐蝶：**他们本该顺着冥河，去往西风的尽头才对…如今却搁浅在了冥流的浅滩上，徘徊不前。

**Castorice:** Did Thanatos reject them? Or perhaps they didn't really die at all?

> **遐蝶：**是塞纳托斯拒绝了他们？还是他们根本没有真正死去？

- ▸ Are they in the same situation as me?
- ▸ Perhaps all of us can still use some CPR.

**Mem:** Why are you identifying as a dead person now? Don't give up on yourself!

> **迷迷：**你怎么现在就以死者自居啦，别放弃呀！

**Castorice:** I have no idea. This circumstance... is completely unprecedented.

> **遐蝶：**我不知道，这情景…闻所未闻。

**Castorice:** Hang on...

> **遐蝶：**等等……

**Castorice:** Didn't he say... "more living people"? So that means he's seen other members of the living apart from us?

> **遐蝶：**他刚才是不是说了…活着的人？他见过的生者，不仅仅只有我们？

**Castorice:** Could it be that these souls are stuck here due to someone's deceit?

> **遐蝶：**莫非，亡魂滞留于此，是受到了某个人的诓骗？

**???:** If something is done out of goodwill, are you really gonna call it "deceit"?

> **？？？：**若是出于好心，还算得上「骗」嘛？

**Castorice:** Goodwill? I can't see anything good out of—

> **遐蝶：**好心？这无论怎么看都……

**Castorice:** Wait a minute... Lady Cipher?

> **遐蝶：**等等…赛飞儿阁下？

**Cipher:** That's me.

> **赛飞儿：**没错，就是我。

- ▸ What are you doing here?
- ▸ Are you dead too?

**Cipher:** Umm... I still belong to the land of the living as of now, thank you very much!

> **赛飞儿：**呃…暂时还和你「阴阳两隔」着呢！

**Castorice:** Could it be that... You are the living being that those souls met...?

> **遐蝶：**亡魂们之前遇见的生者，莫非就是……

**Cipher:** I couldn't take their behavior anymore, so I stole some things here and made up some lies there... Just trying to give them something to look forward to, you know?

> **赛飞儿：**实在看不过眼，所以东边偷了点，西边骗了点，希望给他们的生活多添一点盼头。

**Mem:** ...It's because of vile living creatures like you that we're being misunderstood by all of them!

> **迷迷：**…就是因为有像你这样恶劣的生者，我们才会被大家误会！

**Cipher:** What else am I supposed do? You can see the mess this place is in — These poor souls are denied by the nether realm, so now they're stuck in a few brief moments of their life, unable to move forward or back.

> **赛飞儿：**不然呢，这地头什么光景你们也看见了——这些可怜人被冥界拒之门外，困在自己生前的几个弹指里面，进退不得。

**Cipher:** Did you see those two blacksmiths on the way? They've been here for as long as the sun's been around, wailing over and over about casting a shield... Styxia is so freaky.

> **赛飞儿：**路上见过两个铁匠没？不知道他们在那儿呆了多久，一遍又一遍地想锻出面盾牌来…噫，斯缇科西亚，太可怕啦。

**Castorice:** Then why have you come back, Lady Cipher?

> **遐蝶：**所以，赛飞儿阁下为何又回来了？

**Cipher:** Hey, I'm not here to aid your adventure. I hate water more than anything else in this world, especially that stinky River of Souls...

> **赛飞儿：**别误会，我可不是来帮你们探险的…毕竟，我这辈子最讨厌的就是水了，何况还是这臭烘烘的冥河……

**Cipher:** But... I just discovered some stuff before I left, and I reckon you should know about it.

> **赛飞儿：**…咳，我只是临走前突然有些发现，觉得高低该给你们递个话。

**Cipher:** It's about that boy who defied death... I found traces of him here.

> **赛飞儿：**那个拒绝死亡的孩子——我在这里发现了他的足迹哪？

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei...?

> **遐蝶：**万敌…阁下？

**Cipher:** Oh right! That's his name! Well, now you know. As for whether that info is actually useful, that's for you lot to figure out.

> **赛飞儿：**想起来了，是叫这个名！不管怎么说，话我已经带到了…至于有没有用，你们自己定夺吧。

**Cipher:** ...So long, Princess Homebody! So long, Gray Mystery!

> **赛飞儿：**——回见，蜗居公主、灰子！

**Mem:** Huh? She disappeared just like that?

> **迷迷：**咦…她就这么消失啦？

**Castorice:** Lady Cipher has always been a... free spirit. In any case, she has brought us some valuable information.

> **遐蝶：**赛飞儿阁下一向…随心所欲。但无论如何，她给我们带来了有价值的信息。

**Castorice:** If Lord Mydei is also somewhere along the river shoals right now, then he will certainly become our signpost. After all, he's different from those wandering souls... He will find his way even in the realm of the dead.

> **遐蝶：**如果万敌阁下此时也在浅滩上，那他一定能成为我们的道标。因为，他和那些离群的亡魂不同…即便在死者的世界，他也一定能找到方向。

- ▸ Speaking of "finding the way"...
- ▸ ...How are we supposed to get over there?

**Mem:** Don't worry. It's time for me to shine...

> **迷迷：**放心，这时就该人家登场了——

**Mem:** Oronyx's Rune of Recollection is nearby. Use prayers to return the river to before it got flooded!

> **迷迷：**附近有欧洛尼斯的「追忆符文」，使用祷言，让河流回到还没泛滥的时候吧！

### Use Oronyx's Miracle to cross the River of Souls

> *Styxia, the City-State of Dragons and Waves... At present, the River of Souls has encircled this pitiful city. Use Oronyx's Miracle to cross the treacherous waters of the River of Souls.*

**Mem:** Hey, over here!

> **迷迷：**来，这边这边！

### Continue exploring Dragonbone City and speak with the spirit of the centurion

> *Styxia, the City-State of Dragons and Waves... At present, the River of Souls has encircled this pitiful city. The spirits of Anaxagoras repeatedly recount the past. They seem to be those who fell during Era Chrysea.*

**Castorice:** Oronyx's miracle has appeared...

> **遐蝶：**欧洛尼斯的奇迹，显现了……

**Mem:** Amazing! It's like we're inside a giant bubble. Even the river got parted.

> **迷迷：**好神奇！就像待在一个巨大的泡泡里，河水都被分开了。

*(Approach the wooden bridge, optional)*

**Mem:** Huh... So this path actually didn't exist back then?

> **迷迷：**啊…以前这条路反而是走不通的吗？

*(Approach the group of warriors)*

**Castorice:** There are wandering souls of the deceased here as well...

> **遐蝶：**这里也有游荡的亡魂……

**Castorice:** We must first return to "the present" before we can communicate with them.

> **遐蝶：**我们得先回到「现在」，才能和他们交流。

*(Idle chats)*

**Soldier A:** Someone's coming, ready...

**Soldier B:** Formation!

**Soldier C:** Look! Pink puppies do exist!

*(Talk to the Centurion)*

**Centurion:** Ah, by the looks of it... you must be the foreign warriors that the queen had mentioned, right?

> **百夫长：**啊，你们的模样…莫非是女王提到的异邦勇士？

**Centurion:** Since the current campaign requires the utmost care, I wasn't able to make time to welcome all of you. Please, forgive me.

> **百夫长：**实在抱歉，此次出征容不得半分疏忽，未能抽身迎接，还请见谅。

**Castorice:** ...Foreign warriors?

**Vault Officer:** "Death"... Could it be that you are here to defeat the evil dragon Pollux?

> **管理宝库的官员：**「死亡」…难道你们是来讨伐恶龙玻吕刻斯的？

**Castorice:** Pollux... Is that the embodiment of Thana — no, "Death"?

> **遐蝶：**玻吕刻斯…它就是塞纳…不，「死亡」的化身？

**Vault Officer:** According to the warriors under the centurion's command, that evil dragon has been dead for quite a while. The curse that condemns people to death didn't originate from it...

> **管理宝库的官员：**据百夫长带领的勇士们说，那恶龙早已死去，致人于死地的恶咒并非由它而起……

**Vault Officer:** But they saw a girl within the dragon's embrace... And she is the true Reaper!

> **管理宝库的官员：**而在龙的怀中，他们见到了一位少女…她才是真正的死神！

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** ...Pray tell, where is that girl right now?

> **遐蝶：**…请问，那位女孩身在何处？

**Vault Officer:** I've heard that the queen spared no effort in bringing her back to the castle. Her Majesty intends to control all of Amphoreus with this unprecedented power...

**Vault Officer:** As for whether this rumor is true... Well, no one knows.

**Castorice:** You have my thanks for the invaluable insight that you've given us.

**Castorice:** Please treat this touch... as a token of my gratitude.

> **遐蝶：**您的见闻十分宝贵，感激不尽。

**Vault Officer:** You... Are you...

**Vault Officer:** Thank you...

### Check the souls remains

> *Styxia, the City-State of Dragons and Waves... At present, the River of Souls has encircled this pitiful city. The spirits of Anaxagoras repeatedly recount the past. They seem to be fallen who lived during Era Chrysea. The remains left by these spirits seem to have a connection with alchemy and Thanatos.*

**Castorice:** This is his...

> **遐蝶：**请将此触…视作对您的谢礼吧。

*(Listen to the souls remains)*

**"Amunet":** Then, you granted him death?

**"Amunet":** That isn't like you... You would have refused even if I commanded you to do so.

**"Castorice":** I... don't know.

> **管理宝库的官员：**你…难道就是…

**"Castorice":** I did it out of sympathy, believing that I wouldn't be racked with agony, but... I am still in a state of turmoil.

**"Amunet":** Sympathy? Sympathy for that youth who triumphed in every battle?

**"Castorice":** That young man was favored by Talanton, who taught him a move that will defeat any mortal enemy with a single blow and emerge victorious every time.

**"Castorice":** He could rely on that move to always achieve victory during Era Chrysea. However, now that death has descended upon the mortal realm, that divine swordplay also means he can send others to death at will. In other words...

**"Amunet":** You feel that he's similar... to you?

**"Castorice":** Yes. Since he can never be inferior to others in martial combat, death would be the inevitable outcome every time he strikes.

**"Castorice":** That's why he approached me and pleaded... Pleaded for me to put an end to all of this.

**"Amunet":** You are perhaps feeling lost from making your peace with "Death" in so abrupt a manner.

**"Castorice":** I... have not made peace with death.

**"Castorice":** I've seen the people of Aidonia stand tall and proud in the face of death. Even before the fated finale arrived, they were already content with the lives that they'd lived...

**"Castorice":** They had such proud expressions... Expressions that made me believe "Death" would be an insult to these mortals if the concept is truly a power that a god had carelessly cast down upon the world.

**"Castorice":** Perhaps death's authority was never meant to exist in this world... just like how I shouldn't exist.

**"Amunet":** I won't refute your beliefs for now, but would you be interested in listening to a story of mine?

**"Amunet":** It's a fairy tale from foreign lands, told by the person who brought you to me in that ice field long ago...

**"Amunet":** ...A tale told by the Queen of Styxia.

**Mem:** What? W—W—W—Wait a minute...

> **迷迷：**啊？等等等等……

**Mem:** That means... The girl born in the dragon's embrace... The personification of "Death" these souls talked about... That's you? Cas???

> **迷迷：**所以，小蝶…从巨龙怀中诞生的少女，那些幽灵口中「死亡」的化身…就是你？！

**Castorice:** I've already had an inkling for a while now... But I honestly have no memories of Styxia...

> **遐蝶：**从刚才开始…我就隐约有所预感。但斯缇科西亚的一切，我的确全然不知……

**Castorice:** My memories only begin... After Amunet brought me to Aidonia.

> **遐蝶：**我的记忆…应该是从阿蒙内特将我带到哀地里亚时开始才对。

> **炼金术士：**在那神话的年代里，或许你们还尚未出世，但在炼金术的领域，已无人能够望我项背。我将在这冥河流经的浅滩，使玻吕刻斯再度腾飞…让「死亡」复归于世！

> **炼金术士：**的确，那不知所踪的少女能致人于死。而将她送来世间的玻吕刻斯…偏偏又会让人「无法死去」啊。

> **炼金术士：**不错！所以，我才必须让它重回世间，展翅高飞。我早已在高塔布下炼金法阵，只可惜…缺失了最关键的一样材料。

> **炼金术士：**诸位，我晓得自己已无力回天。拿去这个，求你们代我实现那至高的伟业……

**Castorice:** This...

> **遐蝶：**这是……

> **「斯缇科西亚女王」：**我们没能唤来尊神，却迎来了横绝冥河的魔物。那可憎的巨龙，玻吕刻斯，将所有亡者拒于冥界之外。

**"Queen of Styxia":** Unable to rest in peace, these departed spirits engulfed Styxia like a roaring tide. As a result, my city and its people no longer exist...

> **「斯缇科西亚女王」：**求死不得的灵魂如潮水般席卷了斯缇科西亚。我的城邦、我的人民，如今已不复存在……

**"Queen of Styxia":** That child is the only person whom I managed to save. Born within the evil dragon's abdomen, she has possessed the talent of "Death" since her birth. She must have been granted to us by the gods as a chance for us to atone for our sins...

> **「斯缇科西亚女王」：**那女婴是我唯一能救下的幸存者。她诞生在恶龙腹中，生来便怀有「死亡」的天赋。她一定是诸神赐给我们赎罪的机会……

> **「斯缇科西亚女王」：**身为母亲，请你为这女孩赐下姓名…在我被冥河卷走以前，至少让我知道，在来世应当如何呼唤她吧。

**"Amunet":** ...

> **「阿蒙内特」：**……

### Speak with Mydei

> *Styxia, the City-State of Dragons and Waves... At present, the River of Souls has encircled this pitiful city. Illusions of Mydei's soul seem to have appeared on the banks of the River of Souls as he slaughters his way out of the nether realm.*

*(After the battle)*

**Mydei:** You...?

> **万敌：**…你们……

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei...?

> **遐蝶：**万敌…阁下？

- ▸ I missed you so much that it almost killed me... Uh. Pun unintended.
- ▸ You're adventuring in the River of Souls, too?

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei... You've been through some tough battles once again.

> **遐蝶：**万敌阁下…你又经历了一番死战啊。

**Mydei:** That black tide... is endless. My death... is only temporary.

> **万敌：**那黑潮…无穷无尽。我的死亡…只是临时。

**Mydei:** I will find my path of return... Go back to the world of the living... and continue my "protection."

> **万敌：**我会找到归时的路…回到生者的世界…继续我的「守护」。

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei, I have always wanted to... Why does Death reject you?

> **遐蝶：**万敌阁下，我一直都想知道…死亡为何会抗拒你？

**Mydei:** I'm the one... resisting it. I must resist... the temptation to go with the flow...

> **万敌：**是我…在抗拒它。顺流而下的诱惑…我会抵抗……

**Mydei:** I'll go against the current... and kill my way out of this cursed nether realm...

> **万敌：**逆流而行，杀出这该死的冥界……

**Mydei:** That is... my destiny.

> **万敌：**那是…我的宿命。

- ▸ He seems to be stuttering...
- ▸ Is this really the proud Mydei?

**Castorice:** It seems like he's completely focused on resisting the temptation of the deads' whispers... He's working hard every minute to deny death.

> **遐蝶：**他似乎在用全部的精神和亡语的诱惑搏斗…为了拒绝在这一刻迎接死亡。

**Castorice:** It seems... his battlefield is not limited to the world of the living.

> **遐蝶：**原来…他的战场不止在生者的世界。

**Castorice:** Lord Mydei, are you willing to travel with us?

> **遐蝶：**万敌阁下，你愿意与我们同行吗？

**Castorice:** We're headed for the matrix inside that tower... to summon Thanatos from their prolonged concealment.

> **遐蝶：**我们将前往那高塔上的法阵…去唤出那藏匿已久的塞纳托斯。

**Mydei:** Tha... natos...

> **万敌：**塞纳…托斯……

**Mydei:** Challenge... death.

> **万敌：**挑战…死亡。

**Castorice:** That's right. If we can work together and defeat them... You won't have to be troubled by the curse of immortality any longer.

> **遐蝶：**没错。如果我们能齐心战胜它…那你也再不必为不死的诅咒所扰了。

**Mydei:** Let's go together, Castorice...

> **万敌：**同行吧，遐蝶……

**Mydei:** Let the person who has brushed against death countless times... guide your way...

> **万敌：**就由已与死亡擦肩过千万次之人…为你们引路……

**Mem:** Cas... Are you sure you want to do this? The alchemist said he wanted you to be a sacrifice...

> **迷迷：**小蝶…确定要这么做吗？那个炼金术士还说，要把你当成祭品……

**Castorice:** ...I'm not afraid. There's no reason for us to turn back anymore.

> **遐蝶：**…我不害怕。我们已经走到了这一步…绝无再回头的理由。

**Castorice:** "Rather than standing still, taking action is by far the better option. Perhaps fate might take pity on us today and show some compassion."

> **遐蝶：**「比起犹豫，还是启程更好。也许就在今天，命运会恻隐一时，抛下些许仁慈。」

**Mem:** G-Get what? I'm getting even more confused...

> **迷迷：**什、什么？人家怎么越来越混乱了……

**Mydei:** Heh...

> **万敌：**呵……

**Mydei:** This is... the first time I've encountered a deceased friend while going upstream in the River of Souls.

> **万敌：**这还是…第一次在冥河逆流而上时，遇见亡故的亲友啊。

**Castorice:** It really... works like this.

> **遐蝶：**果然…是这样。

**Castorice:** Just like the other departed spirits we've encountered along the way... I'm also unable to sense the aura of the living on Lady Trianne.

> **遐蝶：**正如方才途中那些亡灵…我也没能从缇安大人身上感知到生者的气息。

**Trianne:** Yup! You're right. Trianne already knows that she'll not be able to return to everyone's side.

> **缇安：**嗯！大家，说得对。缇安早就知道，自己回不到大家身边啦。

**Trianne:** But Trianne believed that Little Cas would eventually return to the nether realm...

> **缇安：**但是，缇安相信小小蝶，总有一天会回到，冥界——

**Trianne:** So Trianne has been standing here, at this spot closest to the world of the living, and waiting for Little Cas!

> **缇安：**所以，缇安一直站在这里，离人间最近的地方，等着小小蝶！

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** It must have been... very tough for you.

> **遐蝶：**真是…太辛苦了。

**Mem:** But according to what Trianne said... We're almost at the entrance of the nether realm.

> **迷迷：**不过…听小缇安的意思，我们确实也快要到冥界的入口了。

**Trianne:** Trianne promised Tribbie and Trinnon that when Little Cas got here, Trianne would get the gift ready and be the first person to run over and welcome you.

> **缇安：**缇安答应过，缇宝，缇宁。等小小蝶来到这里，缇安要准备好礼物，第一个跑来迎接你。

**Trianne:** So, Little Cas, don't cry! Please accept this...

> **缇安：**所以，小小蝶，不要哭！请你，收下这个——

**Trianne:** ...We finally finished it!

> **缇安：**——*我们*，终于把它完成啦！

**Castorice:** This is what Lady Tribbie once mentioned...

> **遐蝶：**这是，缇宝大人说过的……

**Castorice:** The gift for me... that you didn't manage to finish...

> **遐蝶：**您先前没能完成的…为我准备的礼物……

**Mydei:** The Holy Maiden of Janus... Even when separated by life and death, their thoughts are still connected.

> **万敌：**雅努斯的圣女…即便阴阳两隔，依旧心意相通。

**Trianne:** Trianne spent a lot of time drawing this! Trianne hopes that Little Cas likes it.

> **缇安：**缇安，画了好久！希望小小蝶，喜欢。

**Castorice:** Mmm... Seriously...

> **遐蝶：**嗯，真是……

**Castorice:** It's seriously... A wonderful drawing, Lady Trianne...

> **遐蝶：**缇安大人，画得真好啊……

**Mydei:** This is... our "Flame-Chase Journey"...

> **万敌：**这便是…我们的「逐火之旅」……

**Trianne:** Little Cas, we don't know what your journey was like before. Tribbie has always said that it must have been a very harsh and frightening journey.

> **缇安：**小小蝶，虽然我们不知道，你之前的旅途是什么样。缇宝总说，那一定是很重很重，很让人害怕的旅途。

**Trianne:** But Little Cas no longer has anything to fear now, because everyone is here with you in this Flame-Chase Journey!

> **缇安：**但是，小小蝶可以不用再害怕：因为，在逐火的旅途里，我们大家，都陪在你的身边！

**Castorice:** That's right...

> **遐蝶：**是啊……

**Castorice:** Only now, when I see Lady Trianne's gift, do I realize that I've already obtained so many things I would never have dared to dream of since I embarked on the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **遐蝶：**看到缇安大人的礼物，我才发现…自从踏上逐火之旅，一路走来，我已经拥有了太多曾经不敢想象的一切。

**Castorice:** It's the first time I've gained so many companions, the first time I picked up the hobbies of crafting and photography, the first time I had dinner with someone else, the first time I went to the market to buy the chimera doll I'd liked for a very long time and hugged it to sleep...

> **遐蝶：**我第一次有了很多同伴，第一次有了手工和留影的爱好；第一次和他人共进晚餐，第一次走进市场，买下喜欢了很久的奇美拉娃娃，抱着它进入梦乡……

**Castorice:** The first time I wore a dress personally made by Lady Aglaea... the first time I went for a walk with Lady Tribbie, Lady Trianne, and Lady Trinnon... the first time I enjoyed the night breeze and looked up at the stars with Miss Hyacine... the first time I took care of dromases with (Trailblazer)...

> **遐蝶：**第一次穿上阿格莱雅大人亲手编织的礼服，第一次和缇宝、缇安、缇宁大人散步，第一次和风堇小姐吹着晚风看星星，第一次和开拓者照料大地兽……

**Castorice:** Looking back at all these experiences I never thought were possible... it feels as though the scales of my life had shrunk from centuries and decades down to minutes and seconds, and all of them are so precious...

> **遐蝶：**这许许多多我不曾奢望的第一次，回忆起来的时候…我的生命好像从数百年、数十年，变成了每一分，每一秒……

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** I took countless photos in the past, but they're all black-and-white landscapes. I don't have a single one with friends.

> **遐蝶：**明明拍过很多照片…却都是些黑白色调的风景，都没留下一张和大家的合照。

**Castorice:** If I had known... I would have taken more pictures...

> **遐蝶：**早知道…应该多拍一些的啊……

- ▸ Then shall we take one now?

**Trianne:** Trianne says yes! Trianne likes Little Cas and would love to take a picture with Little Cas!

> **缇安：**缇安，可以！缇安喜欢小小蝶，喜欢和小小蝶一起拍照。

**Mydei:** Sadly...

> **万敌：**可惜……

**Mydei:** Being departed souls, our images cannot be captured in the world of the living.

> **万敌：**你我，皆为亡魂…无法在人间留影。

**Castorice:** ...I thought so, too.

> **遐蝶：**…我想也是。

**Mem:** What? Are we seriously going to leave any room for regret?

> **迷迷：**话都说到这份上了，却还要留下遗憾…这种事情不要啊！

**Castorice:** There won't be any regrets...

> **遐蝶：**不会有遗憾的……

**Castorice:** This vivid drawing surpasses any photograph from the world of the living.

> **遐蝶：**这幅五彩缤纷的画…已胜过人世间任何一张留影了。

**Trianne:** Little Cas smiled! Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne's wish has finally come true.

> **缇安：**小小蝶笑了！缇宝、缇宁、缇安的心愿，终于完成了。

**Castorice:** I remember you once said that fate is not the flowers that bloom at the end of the road, but where people tread beyond the gate... Now, the path that belongs to me is about to reach the end.

> **遐蝶：**还记得您说过，命运不是生长在道路尽头的花朵，而是人们自己踏出的小径…现在，属于我的那一条行径，即将来到终点的门关。

**Castorice:** Thank you... for bringing me the final key.

> **遐蝶：**感谢您…为我带来了最后的钥匙。

**Trianne:** Key? Little Cas, why are you talking in riddles all of a sudden?

> **缇安：**钥匙？小小蝶…你说话怎么变得难懂起来啦？

**Castorice:** I have a feeling that soon, this gift of yours will become the key to saving Mr./Miss (Trailblazer)'s life.

> **遐蝶：**我已有预感：不久后，您这份礼物将成为挽救开拓者阁下生命的关键。

**Trianne:** Wow! That's amazing! Then keep smiling and carry on bravely!

> **缇安：**喔，那真是太好啦。既然这样，那就带着笑容，勇敢前进吧！

**Trianne:** Little Cas, Little Gray, everyone — see you tomorrow!

> **缇安：**小小蝶，小小灰，还有大家——明天见！

With a blinding smile, Trianne's golden form disappears in the thin fog of Styxia's moonlit night.

**Castorice:** Lady Trianne...

> **遐蝶：**缇安大人……

**Mydei:** The aura of this place... We have indeed reached the end of the Stream of Souls.

> **万敌：**此处的气息…也确实便是冥流的终点。

**Mydei:** If I go any further... I might lose myself. I must leave now, for the black tide still wreaks havoc in the realm of the living.

> **万敌：**继续前进…我或许会迷失于此。我必须离去了…因为黑潮还在生者的世界肆虐。

- ▸ Can you find your way out?

**Mydei:** I have broken free from the chains of death countless times... This time will be no different.

> **万敌：**千万次…我已挣脱过死亡的枷锁。此次…也不例外。

- ▸ Don't you want to know the truth behind Thanatos?

**Mydei:** I seek not to enter the door of the nether realm.

> **万敌：**步入冥界之门…非我所求。

**Mydei:** Castorice... Will you fulfill your duty for the Flame-Chase?

> **万敌：**遐蝶…你会为逐火…完成使命么？

**Castorice:** I...

> **遐蝶：**我……

**Castorice:** I have yet to make my decision, Lord Mydei.

> **遐蝶：**我还在思量，万敌阁下。

**Mydei:** Heh...

> **万敌：**呵……

**Mydei:** No matter... Make the choice your heart desires. Even if it takes a thousand years of waiting...

> **万敌：**无妨…无愧本心地选择吧。就算要再等上千年……

**Mydei:** I will continue to fight till I die... Then rise from death... til the miracle descends.

> **万敌：**我会一直战斗至死…然后死而复生…直到奇迹降临之刻。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** There is one more thing you should know, Lord Mydei.

> **遐蝶：**万敌阁下…还有一件事，或许你应该知晓。

**Mydei:** ...What is it?

> **万敌：**…何事？

**Castorice:** Professor Anaxa may have deduced the principles behind the Era Nova. You and I... may be reincarnated as "Strife" and "Death" in the next cycle, becoming calamities wreaking havoc upon the world.

> **遐蝶：**那刻夏老师或许推导出了「再创世」的原理。你我…也许将在下一世化为「纷争」与「死亡」，成为降在世间的灾厄。

**Mydei:** ...I see.

> **万敌：**…我知晓了。

**Castorice:** ...Is that all? Are you going to accept such a fate without complaints?

> **遐蝶：**…只有这样吗？你愿意坦然接受…这样的命运吗？

**Mydei:** Do you have... a strategy to shatter the shackles of our fate?

> **万敌：**你…有任何策略，能让我们击碎命运的管束？

**Castorice:** I... haven't found it yet. Perhaps I never will...

> **遐蝶：**我…还没找到方法。也许不可能找得到……

**Mydei:** Then... cease wasting your thoughts on futile matters.

> **万敌：**那就…别再思考无用之事。

**Mydei:** Control what little you can. And when fate's inescapable grasp comes...

> **万敌：**掌控…你能掌控之事。待那无可逃避的命运降临时……

**Mydei:** Resist it with the strength of your mighty will.

> **万敌：**再用你强有力的意志…反抗它的摆弄。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** Thank you, Lord Mydei. I will find the right decision.

> **遐蝶：**感谢你，万敌阁下。那个正确的选择…我会找到它的。

**Mydei:** In the name of "Strife"... may you meet a fitting end.

> **万敌：**以「纷争」之名…愿你死得其所。

**Castorice:** This must be the alchemical matrix the spirit mentioned. Let's see... We need to insert "Aquila's Sky," "Georios's Earth," "Kephale's Fire," "Phagousa's Ocean"...

> **遐蝶：**这就是亡魂所说的炼金法阵——看来，需要分别置入「艾格勒的天空」、「吉奥里亚的大地」、「刻法勒的火焰」、「法吉娜的海洋」……

**Castorice:** And "Mnestia's Love"...

> **遐蝶：**还有，「墨涅塔的爱」……

**Cipher:** It seems our sweet Princess Homebody has made up her mind...

> **赛飞儿：**嘻，看来我们可爱的蜗居公主已经下定决心了……

**Cipher:** In that case, Seamstress, I suppose I no longer need to deliver your message — my time is rather precious, after all.

> **赛飞儿：**既然这样，就不帮你专门带话了，裁缝女——我的时间可是非常宝贵的哪。

**Aglaea:** Whether you wish to break free from death or not, please follow your heart. By the promise you once made, I ask that you fulfill your duty one final time —

> **阿格莱雅：**无论你是否意欲摆脱死亡，请遵从本心。以你曾经的诺言为证，我最后一次，请求你运使自己的使命——

**Aglaea:** Castorice, please live as a human... And die as one.

> **阿格莱雅：**遐蝶，请如人那般生…如人那般死。

**Castorice:** According to that alchemist, the final piece is supposed to be me...

> **遐蝶：**依照那位炼金术士的话语，最后的材料应当就是我自己……

**Castorice:** However, he is not privy to the past and therefore cannot see the full picture.

> **遐蝶：**不过，他未曾如这般窥见往昔，未知全貌。

**Mem:** ...The past?

> **迷迷：**…往昔？

**Castorice:** I saw the twins of "Death" in Kephale's memory, and now I am certain that I was one of them.

> **遐蝶：**于刻法勒的回忆之中，我见过了「死亡」的双子。现在，我已能确信，我曾是其中之一。

**Castorice:** The trial of the "Hand of Shadow" demanded "taking the life of your family." I died during that trial, falling before the gate to the new world... And my sister, whom I never met, became the Titan Thanatos of this cycle.

> **遐蝶：**「灰黯之手」的试炼，便是「夺去至亲的生命」。我在那时已然死去，倒在了新世界的门关前…而我那素未谋面的姊妹，成为了此世的泰坦塞纳托斯。

**Castorice:** She was consumed with regret over my death, so she used the Titan's authority to reforge my soul. Yet, it also means she has never revealed herself to anyone, hidden away from the entire world...

> **遐蝶：**对于「我」的死亡，她追悔莫及。而后，她以泰坦的神权将我的灵魂重塑，却也让自己从未在人前示现，无法被人寻获……

**Castorice:** That's because the Titan she became already fell at the start of everything... They died for me and now lie in slumber in the River of Souls.

> **遐蝶：**因为…它一开始就为我而身陨，如今正静静地沉睡于冥河之中。

**Mem:** You mean... Pollux?

> **迷迷：**你是说…玻吕刻斯？

**Castorice:** That's right. The River of Souls is no material river. It cannot be blocked by ordinary means. Only a divine corpse can prevent the dead from reaching the next world.

> **遐蝶：**没错，冥河并非凡物，绝不会被轻易阻断。那拒绝死者往生者，惟有神性的残躯。

**Castorice:** And that's why we have to awaken Pollux from death before we can retrieve the Coreflame of "Death"...

> **遐蝶：**也正因此，要想取出「死亡」的火种，我们唯有使玻吕刻斯从死亡中苏醒……

**Castorice:** We have to reforge its body with the power of "alchemy."

> **遐蝶：**利用「炼金」之法的力量，令它的残躯回归一身。

**Mem:** But Cas, if that alchemist speaks the truth... does that mean you're also part of the dragon body?

> **迷迷：**那，按照刚才炼金术士的说法…小蝶你也是巨龙残躯的一部分？

**Castorice:** According to those departed spirits, I was indeed born as the dragon's twin...

> **遐蝶：**在亡魂们的口中，我确实与那巨龙相伴而生……

**Castorice:** But before we talk about that... Have you heard the sighs?

> **遐蝶：**不过，在此之前，各位曾听到过声声叹息吗？

- ▸ I did when I was collecting the souls...
- ▸ You sighed many times on the way here...

**Castorice:** No, not from me. The sounds that come from the memories when we were collecting the souls.

> **遐蝶：**不，并非是我，而是在回收灵魂时，于记忆之中传来的声音。

**Mem:** Ah, I heard them too! So, what are those?

> **迷迷：**啊，人家也听到了！所以那是？

**Castorice:** I think... it's probably the sign of Pollux's wish being fulfilled.

> **遐蝶：**我想…那或许正是巨龙玻吕刻斯的遗愿实现的征兆吧。

**Castorice:** They must have realized how heavy a price they'd need to pay when they used their divine authority to resurrect a dead woman. That's why they exerted every ounce of their strength to send me to Styxia, a city that called for "Death" — A land where none is hostile to "Death."

> **遐蝶：**在行使神权，复活死者时，它也一定意识到了那代价究竟有多沉重。因此，它用尽最后一丝余力，将我送到了无人敌视「死亡」的城邦——呼唤「死亡」的斯缇科西亚。

**Castorice:** They never saw their wish fulfilled and fell so prematurely, all because they wanted to give me a new life... Pollux must really want to know how their sister has been after giving their all to save her.

> **遐蝶：**为了让我过上新的人生，它却没能顺遂心愿，早早陨落…玻吕刻斯该有多想亲眼见证，它竭尽全力拯救的姐姐，今后生活得怎么样啊。

**Castorice:** That wish is the reason Pollux's soul still lingers in the mortal realm even after their fall, quietly watching and documenting every detail of their sister's life...

> **遐蝶：**也正是带着这样的心愿，即便身陨，玻吕刻斯的灵魂仍旧盘桓人间，静静地注视并记录着有关至亲的一切……

**Mem:** Ah! So, instead of picking up objects on the way here...

> **迷迷：**啊！所以这一路上，我们不是捡拾到了某物……

**Castorice:** ...We were instead showing things to a strand of yearning that lingers here. Now we must weave these yearnings together and restore them into Pollux's complete soul.

> **遐蝶：**…而是给飘荡在此处的一缕思念，展现了某物。而我们将要把这些思念逐一编织，复原成玻吕刻斯完整的灵魂。

**Mem:** But all of this is just a hypothesis, right? What if we go through with the ritual, and it turns out totally different?

> **迷迷：**可这些…都只是猜想吧？万一我们完成了仪式，结果却又完全不是这么一回事呢？

**Castorice:** That's fine. As Professor Anaxa often reminds us, such is the nature of alchemy. The true outcome can only be known once the process is complete. This is why alchemy is also known as the "Miracle of Creation."

> **遐蝶：**那也无妨。那刻夏老师总说「炼金」一向如此：我们能得到什么，只有在炼成的那一刻才能知晓。因此，这门学问才能被称为「造物的奇迹」。

**Castorice:** Now, the puzzle of Pollux's yearnings is nearly complete, with only one final piece remaining —

> **遐蝶：**现在，玻吕刻斯思念的拼图已甄于完整，只差最后一块碎片——

**Castorice:** I shall reveal what they have been yearning to know in the alchemical matrix — I will show them my whole life since I left Styxia...

> **遐蝶：**我将在炼金法阵中呈现，它无比想要知晓的一切：我离开斯缇科西亚后所经历的全部人生……

**Castorice:** ...Lady Trianne has already drawn a picture of my entire life, and it is now in my hands.

> **遐蝶：**…缇安大人已将它完整描绘，并交到我的手中了。

*(Obtain Trianne's Hand-Drawn Picture)*

### Borrow the Philosopher's Stone and complete the alchemical ritual

> *With Anaxagoras's Philosopher's Stone, Styxia's alchemical array, and the spiritual remains in Castorice's hands, the alchemical preparations are ready. According to data from the Grove, "All things come into being through the combination of the Four Roots and perish with their separation." Complete the alchemical array and summon the Titan.*

*(Investigate the marked location)*

**Castorice:** Then... let the ritual begin.

> **遐蝶：**就让仪式…开始吧。

**Castorice:** "...And now, the objective is complete. That is all."

> **遐蝶：**「——至是，工程已毕，言尽于此。」

**Castorice:** Such is what Professor Anaxa always says after preparing for an alchemical ritual. And now —

> **遐蝶：**那刻夏老师总喜欢在完成炼金准备后说上这样一句话。而现在——

**Castorice:** Pollux, I shall lift you from the River of Souls and reforge your soul. Perhaps you will be disturbed by this, and your body may become horrifying to behold...

> **遐蝶：**玻吕刻斯，我会将你从冥河中抽离，然后重塑。或许你将因此受到惊扰，身形残缺可怖……

**Castorice:** But if even the slightest trace of your soul remains, the soul that once ferried me to this side of the shore, then please look at me. I wish to thank you in person — I have obtained everything you sacrificed your divinity for.

> **遐蝶：**但若是当初你引渡我的意识仍有一息尚存——请看向我吧。我想当面和你诉说感谢：你以神性为我换回的一切，我已经品尝过了。

**Castorice:** Lady Amunet...

> **遐蝶：**阿蒙内特阁下……

**Mem:** Did she recognize you...? She must be the first soul we can communicate with here, right?

> **迷迷：**她…认出你了？这应该是我们第一次见到能沟通的…灵魂吧？

**Castorice:** I'm afraid she is just another spirit trapped here, like the rest of them. The only difference is that I was there when she died.

> **遐蝶：**恐怕…她也和其他亡魂一样，仅仅是受困于此。只不过，她的片刻，我原本在场。

**Castorice:** ...Please, go on. I'm listening. Are you about to tell the story of Styxia?

> **遐蝶：**…请您继续吧，我在听。您要讲的，莫非是斯缇科西亚的故事？

**Amunet:** Yes. You're just as smart as I imagined, but...

> **阿蒙内特：**正是，你果然如我想象的那样聪慧，但……

> **遐蝶：**不必担心，长老。您看周围…这里已是亡魂的归处。

**Amunet:** The world of the dead? But how...

> **阿蒙内特：**亡魂的世界？这怎会……

**Castorice:** I understand that you chose to remain silent about the story to protect Castorice, the girl who coexists with death...

**Castorice:** But you no longer need to hide that story. I can assure you that you have protected that girl's life well.

> **遐蝶：**但现在，您不必再藏于心间了。我可以告诉您，那女孩的人生，已被您尽力守护住了。

**Amunet:** ...

> **阿蒙内特：**……

**Amunet:** ...Well, in that case, let me begin.

> **阿蒙内特：**…也罢，那我就此展开吧。

**Amunet:** Styxia, the pearl of the ocean, plagued by unwarranted calamity and ravaged by death in the fairy tale — It was the first city-state to shift their faith towards "Death," even before Aidonia did.

> **阿蒙内特：**斯缇科西亚，那在童话中遭受无妄之灾，被死亡肆虐的海洋明珠——在哀地里亚之前，它是第一个转向「死亡」信仰的城邦。

**Amunet:** From where you stand now, I suppose that's a bit hard to believe, right?

> **阿蒙内特：**在现在的你看来…这或许有些不可思议吧？

**Castorice:** Indeed... I cannot imagine that those from the Era Chrysea, blessed with eternal life, could ever yearn for death.

> **遐蝶：**没错…我难以想象，身处黄金世，享受着无尽寿数的人们…如何会醉心于死亡？

**Amunet:** Heh... I have pondered the same question, child. But I'm afraid there is no clear answer to it.

> **阿蒙内特：**呵…我也一直在思考同样的问题，孩子。但恐怕这个问题没有确切的答案。

**Castorice:** "Journeys and epics are only glorious and magnificent because all things eventually fade to dust." That's how someone once explained to me why death is necessary to the world.

> **遐蝶：**「征途之所以伟大，史诗之所以壮阔，是因为万物终有逝去之时」——曾有人如此为我解释，为何死亡是世间必需之物。

**Castorice:** But I still can't take that as the final answer... Would a world without journeys and epics be worse off?

> **遐蝶：**但我无法将它视作最后的解答…难道一个没有征伐与史诗的世界便不再美好了吗？

**Amunet:** Heh... I take it the one who spoke this line was a general? Or perhaps a warrior?

> **阿蒙内特：**呵…对你说出这话的人，我想他或许是一位将军？斗士？

**Amunet:** I, however, did find some inspiration in these words. Would you like to hear my thoughts, Castorice?

> **阿蒙内特：**你我并非战士或统帅，「征途」和「史诗」也无法引起我心灵的共鸣。

**Amunet:** But what if I reframe it like this — what if "love" is not a trait bestowed upon mortals by Mnestia, but a blessing from death?

> **阿蒙内特：**但若将这语境稍加修改…如果…「爱」并非墨涅塔赐予凡人的天性…反而是死亡降给我们的赐福呢？

**Castorice:** "Love"...

> **遐蝶：**「爱」……

**Amunet:** No one ever invented the word "cherish" in the era when life was eternal, for all the good things in the world could be revisited endlessly, and every bond formed carried no risk of being severed.

> **阿蒙内特：**在生命无尽的时代，无人发明「珍惜」二字——因为世间一切美好皆能无限次地被收入眼帘；一切被建立起的联系都无需担忧破碎。

**Amunet:** The so-called Era Chrysea was an age devoid of both virtue and sin. People did not truly live — they simply existed, going through the motions without passion or purpose, then waiting for something to happen.

> **阿蒙内特：**所谓的黄金世，便是一个既无美德也无罪恶的时代。人们并非活着…而仅仅是存在于世上，倦惰地行走、等待。

**Amunet:** Perhaps the people of Styxia chose to embrace death not out of fascination with its cruel and fearsome side...

**Amunet:** But instead became fixated and obsessed with the impulses and desires tied to a finite existence.

> **阿蒙内特：**而是伴随有限的生命降临的冲动和欲念，令他们沉醉痴狂，无法自拔。

**Amunet:** If you truly intend to deprive the world of "Death," then be prepared to witness it return to a state of stagnation.

> **阿蒙内特：**你若打算剥夺世间的「死亡」…那就要做好准备，目睹这世界回归停滞和怠惰。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** I understand, Lady Amunet.

**Castorice:** "Mnestia's Love" was the Chrysalis of Gold's first blessing, but it most likely wasn't understood by mankind. We only learned to interpret this wonderful gift after Thanatos' arrival.

> **遐蝶：**「墨涅塔的爱」…黄金之茧最初的赐福，大概并不为人所理解。直到塞纳托斯降临后，人们才学会了解读这份美好的赠礼。

And Aglaea would never have invited me to join the Flame-Chase Journey. None of these stories would ever have happened.

**Thanatos:** If the Reaper raises their left hand, the soul is granted death and allowed to enter the nether realm to await reincarnation. If they raise their right hand, the soul is rejected by the nether realm and returned to the mortal realm to continue walking the earth.

> **塞纳托斯：**若死神举起左手，则灵魂受赐死亡，步入冥界，等待转生；若举起右手，则灵魂将被冥界拒绝，回归人间，继续在大地上前进。

> **遐蝶：**所以…「灰黯之手」只能施行宣判，而不能进行拥抱。

**Thanatos:** Yes. "Death" was originally a pair of hands, but they were split in twain when we reached the mortal realm, never to touch each other ever again...

> **塞纳托斯：**是啊。正如「死亡」本为一体的双掌，在我们抵达人间时一分为二，再也无法触及对方……

**Thanatos:** The half of Thanatos that represents life, the undying human who "grants death"... That was the curse I forced upon you.

> **塞纳托斯：**塞纳托斯的生之半身，「赐予死亡」的永生之人…这就是我强加于你的诅咒。

**Castorice:** Then... What about you?

> **遐蝶：**那…你呢？

**Thanatos:** As the half of Thanatos representing death, I shoulder the fate of "defying death." I died the moment I entered the world of the living.

> **塞纳托斯：**它的死之半身，我背负「拒绝死亡」的命运，在人间诞生的那一瞬间便宣告死去。

**Thanatos:** From then on, my Titan also no longer existed in this world. The cycle of life and death stopped. Souls were denied their audience with death, and were left stranded within an ever-rising river of the dead....

> **塞纳托斯：**自此，泰坦也在世上不复存在。生死断绝流转，灵魂无缘面见尊神，只能搁浅在亡者有涨无落的河流中……

**Thanatos:** What irony. My selfish desire to save your life gave you and the world a fate far worse than death instead.

> **塞纳托斯：**多可笑啊…我的私心本为挽救生命，却为你，还有这个世界写下了比死亡更沉重的命运。

**Thanatos:** Then, what was left of my reason was left behind in the nether realm...

> **塞纳托斯：**然后，我，玻吕茜亚残存的理智，徒留在冥界……

**Thanatos:** Where I've been taking care of these flowers day after day, until there is now a sea of them....

> **塞纳托斯：**日复一日地照料鲜花，看它长成花海……

**Thanatos:** ...All while mulling over the subject of life, death, partings, and enduring an endless loneliness.

> **塞纳托斯：**…咀嚼着生与死，离别，还有漫长的孤独。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Castorice:** ...Fate is absurd indeed.

> **遐蝶：**…命运果然很荒诞。

**Castorice:** Our lives were a mirrored image of one another, like a pair of hands... Even so, we arrived at the same destination.

> **遐蝶：**我们的人生如同手掌对映…可即便如此，仍旧殊途同归——

**Castorice:** We walked different paths, yet experienced the same pain.

> **遐蝶：**我们走过不同的路，却品尝着同样的滋味。

**Thanatos:** I had hoped that maybe you would at least have a good life in this cycle...

> **塞纳托斯：**我本以为，至少你能在这一世好好活下去……

**Thanatos:** ...I'm sorry.

> **塞纳托斯：**…对不起。

**Castorice:** I lived my life well. It didn't go to waste. As far as I'm concerned, you did nothing wrong.

> **遐蝶：**我的人生并没有虚度。对我而言，你不曾做错过什么。

**Thanatos:** Even though I forced the authority of "granting death to humanity" upon you, a total stranger?

> **塞纳托斯：**即便…我将「赐死」的权柄强加给与我素不相识的你？

**Castorice:** Too many responsibilities were forced upon me in the course of this long journey. I was once lost, even upset...

> **遐蝶：**在这场漫长的旅途中，我的肩头早已被强加过太多使命。我也曾迷茫，乃至愠怒过……

**Castorice:** But I realized recently that if it weren't for your blessing, I wouldn't have understood the source of these emotions, nor would I have realized my purpose...

> **遐蝶：**但直到不久前，我才意识到：若非你的赐福，我也无法理解那些感情从何而来，无法理解自己的使命——

**Castorice:** I wouldn't have made it here to reunite with my missing half, and we wouldn't be discussing our past while sharing the same view of this landscape...

> **遐蝶：**更遑论行至此地，与我宿命的半身重逢；一同分享我们的过去，眼前的风景……

**Castorice:** I wouldn't have understood the meaning of our "existence."

> **遐蝶：**还有，你我二人「存在」的意义。

**Thanatos:** The meaning of our existence...

> **塞纳托斯：**存在的意义……

**Castorice:** This sea of flowers blooms on dead land because we sowed it with the seeds of our separation and watered it with our tears of loneliness... Yes, the only thing "Death" is allowed to create are flowers that bloom from blood and tears.

> **遐蝶：**这片花海生长在死地，你我用离别为它播种，又以孤独的泪水浇灌而成…是啊，「死亡」所能缔造的，莫过于血泪中绽放的鲜花而已。

**Castorice:** That's why Thanatos cannot create living beings at will, nor can the "Hand of Shadow" determine a person's remaining days...

> **遐蝶：**所以，塞纳托斯并不能随心所欲地塑造万物，「灰黯之手」也无法裁夺万物生灵的命运——

**Castorice:** Because the life and death of mortals should never be dictated by another's will.

> **遐蝶：**因为凡人的生与死，本就不该被任何意志主宰。

**Thanatos:** Are you saying there's no point in Thanatos' existence...?

> **塞纳托斯：**这听起来，反而是在否定塞纳托斯的意义……

**Castorice:** No. No single being can bear the scales of life and death on their own, not even a god.

> **遐蝶：**不。无人能独力担起生死的天平，哪怕是神明。

**Castorice:** Life and death weigh equally heavy... We both know that now, don't we?

> **遐蝶：**生与死的分量同样沉重…你我都再明白不过了，不是么？

**Thanatos:** ...

> **塞纳托斯：**……

**Thanatos:** You, too, must have had a difficult life...

> **塞纳托斯：**你这一生，想必也很辛苦吧……

**Thanatos:** We've taken quite the ridiculous journey together, haven't we?

> **塞纳托斯：**我们…真是一同走过了无比荒谬的旅途啊。

**Thanatos:** I had a feeling even back then that we would meet again one day...

> **塞纳托斯：**早在那时，我已有预感：总有一天，我们还会再相见……

**Thanatos:** Not to embrace one another after a long separation, but to come in search of retribution.

> **塞纳托斯：**不为久别重逢的拥抱，而为以手还手，实现报应……

**Thanatos:** Tell me the answer that's already been written... and let us draw this seemingly endless fate to a close.

> **塞纳托斯：**告诉我早已写就的答案…为漫长的命运画上句号。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Thanatos:** It's been far too long. I've waited here for centuries, watching day after day as desperate spirits are denied by this lonely wasteland...

> **塞纳托斯：**已经太久太久了。我一个人守候在这里，日复一日地望着走投无路的亡灵被这片孤独的荒原拒之门外……

**Thanatos:** I always hoped I would see you among them, but I was also afraid of seeing you. I didn't know how I could possibly face you again...

> **塞纳托斯：**在他们中，我始终期待着看到你的身影，却又害怕和你相认。我不知道要如何面对你……

**Thanatos:** But... more than anything, I was afraid that you might have forgotten all about me.

> **塞纳托斯：**我更害怕看见…曾经的至亲早已忘却了我的存在。

**Castorice:** ...I know. But don't you see, Polyxia? I made it back anyway.

> **遐蝶：**…我明白。但你看，玻吕茜亚，无论如何…我回到了这里。

**Castorice:** You don't have to shoulder Death's authority on your own anymore... It was always meant to be held in two hands anyway.

> **遐蝶：**你不必再独自肩负「死亡」的权柄…毕竟，它本就应该由两只手共同举起。

**Castorice:** So, let's give our Coreflame to (Trailblazer) and have him/her take it back to the mortal realm. Give humanity back their cycle of life and death.

> **遐蝶：**所以，就让开拓者带着我们的火种回到人间，让凡人取回生死流转的权利……

**Castorice:** Our responsibility as Thanatos was never about picking out favorite souls. We were only meant to watch over life.

> **遐蝶：**我们，塞纳托斯的职责，从来都不是拣选生命，只有看顾它们而已。

**Castorice:** Let's tend the field together and create a warm sea of flowers for all the souls to come.

> **遐蝶：**让我们一起…为众多灵魂照料这片属于他们的、温暖的花海吧。

**Thanatos:** ...

> **塞纳托斯：**……

**Castorice:** What do you think, Polyxia?

> **遐蝶：**好吗，玻吕茜亚？

**Thanatos:** Yes. Just... one last step.

> **塞纳托斯：**当然。现在…也只差最后一步了。

**Thanatos:** "You shall wither, and through that, the dead will sprout again from the remains, and be reborn with the dead flame"...

> **塞纳托斯：**「汝将凋零，令逝者自残余中发芽，一同死去的火新生」……

**Thanatos:** As foretold by the prophecy, Thanatos' authority is only destined for only one person to wield. As the Chrysos Heir of "Death," as their demigod, as a Titan...

> **塞纳托斯：**如神谕所示，塞纳托斯的神权注定只能由一人执掌。身为「死亡」的黄金裔，身为它的半神，身为泰坦……

**Thanatos:** ...Our fated moment has arrived once again.

> **塞纳托斯：**…我们命中注定的时刻，再次到来了。

**Castorice:** Just like before...

> **遐蝶：**就像从前那样……

**Thanatos:** Yes, just like before.

> **塞纳托斯：**是啊，就像从前。

**Thanatos:** But unlike that time, the ones undergoing the trial this time are prepared.

> **塞纳托斯：**但，与那次不同的是…这一次，试炼的双方都做好了准备。

**Castorice:** You've... made up your mind?

> **遐蝶：**你已经…下定决心了？

**Thanatos:** Yes... as dictated by fate. But I won't be afraid, as long as you're here with me.

> **塞纳托斯：**嗯…此乃命运使然。但只要有你在，我就不会害怕。

**Thanatos:** Complete your trial in this life. Grant me the embrace of "Death" and light the spark of "Life" ablaze.

> **塞纳托斯：**请你完成这一世的试炼，赐我以「死亡」的拥抱，点燃「生命」的火苗。

**Thanatos:** You've been tempered by life and death. I know you'll be able to give this wasteland its first breath of life...

> **塞纳托斯：**如果是你，在经历过生与死的锤炼之后，一定能为这片荒原带来第一抹生机……

**Thanatos:** Like a raven butterfly flying swiftly into the nether realm, perching on the twig of death.

> **塞纳托斯：**就像奔赴冥界的蝴蝶，落在死亡的枝头。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** ...I will. After this, I will remain permanently in the nether realm. The River of Souls shall return here and rain down from the sky, scattering souls across the earth...

> **遐蝶：**…我会的。在那之后，我会永居冥界，让冥河回到这里，化作天上的细雨，将灵魂播撒向这片冥土……

**Castorice:** The cycle of life and death will flow once again. This sea of flowers you sowed shall bear countless joyous or sorrowful souls, and bloom evermore.

> **遐蝶：**生死将再度流转。你亲手播下的这片花海，今后将承载无数或悲或喜的灵魂，永远焕发生机。

**Thanatos:** ...Thank you.

> **塞纳托斯：**…谢谢你。

**Thanatos:** Just one last thing. Can you... grant me one wish?

> **塞纳托斯：**最后的最后，能请你…为我实现一个心愿么？

**Castorice:** Of course. What is it?

> **遐蝶：**当然可以。是什么？

**Thanatos:** ...

> **塞纳托斯：**……

**Thanatos:** Please... don't forget me.

> **塞纳托斯：**请…不要忘记我。

**Castorice:** ...I promise you.

> **遐蝶：**…我答应你。

**Castorice:** When we meet in the next world... Even if I have endured another reincarnation...

> **遐蝶：**即便我将再度跨越轮回…当我们在来世再见时，我也一定会像最初诞生时那样……

**Castorice:** ...Even if we do not know each other's names...

> **遐蝶：**即便，我们尚不知道对方的名字……

**Castorice:** ...I will still hold you tightly in my arms, just like what I did when we were born.

> **遐蝶：**…我也一定会紧紧地、紧紧地抱住你。

**Thanatos:** Castorice... Castorice...

> **塞纳托斯：**遐蝶…遐蝶……

**Thanatos:** You're as beautiful... as a fleeting butterfly.

> **塞纳托斯：**人如其名…多美呀。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Castorice:** I will bring rest to Death.

> **遐蝶：**我将为「死亡」带去安息。

**Castorice:** And from now on... stagnant life will once again flow.

> **遐蝶：**从此，停滞的生命…将再次开始流转。

*(Cutscene ends)*

Switching to Anaxa's POV...

Knowledge is a scholar's second sun, and it is only right that they shine its light equally upon all the fools seated before them...

## 123　Citizen, Listen to Those Roaring Tides　公民啊，倾听那汹涌潮声

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Demigod Council" Dawncloud　|　角色：Anaxa; Cerces; Phainon; Caenis; Military Runner; Lygus; Aglaea; Empedocles; Castorice; Polyxia　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls'' completed

### Speak with Cerces

> *The citizens' assembly on the matter of "Pausing the Flame-Chase Journey" is coming to an end. Soon, everyone will witness what "vote-decided tomorrow" they will experience. Your fifteen Entry Hours are done. The last performance is about to begin.*

At the same time, in Okhema

*Loud cheers from afar*

**Cerces:** Time flies... In the blink of an eye, the fifteenth Entry Hour is already at hand.

> **瑟希斯：**时光飞逝哪…转眼间，第十五个门扉时已近在眼前了。

**Cerces:** When this farce is over, your soul will be placed on the scales of life and death. Are you ready?

> **瑟希斯：**待到这场闹剧结束，汝之魂息也将置上生死的天平。准备好了么？

**Anaxa:** No need to ask so many times—of course I am.

> **那刻夏：**用不着这么反复确认，当然。

**Cerces:** Then why do you drag your feet? Is your fear of crowds holding you back again?

> **瑟希斯：**那末，为何依旧踯躅不前？是汝那忌惮人群的毛病又犯了？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, I simply find them too noisy. Paying attention to conceited fools like them would be an utter waste of my time.

> **那刻夏：**只是觉得太吵了。那些愚蠢又自负的笨货，多看一眼都是浪费我的时间。

**Cerces:** Such harsh words. As I see it, you are no less arrogant than they are.

> **瑟希斯：**真是不近人情哪。依吾看，汝妄自尊大的程度也不遑多让呢。

**Anaxa:** I couldn't agree more.

> **那刻夏：**对此，我深表赞同。

**Cerces:** Oh? I did not expect to hear that from you. Are you not going to refute me?

> **瑟希斯：**哦？这反应真叫人意外，汝竟不反驳吾么？

**Anaxa:** I know myself better than you think I do... At the very least, better than a stowaway like you.

> **那刻夏：**自己是什么样，我难道还不清楚么？至少比你这位「房客」清楚吧。

**Cerces:** And they say men whose ends are nigh have but kind words.

> **瑟希斯：**真是人之将死，其言也不善哪。

**Cerces:** Well that is exactly why I chose you.

> **瑟希斯：**但也正因如此，吾才会选中了你，不是么？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, should I thank you for the compliment?

> **那刻夏：**哼，承蒙谬赞。

**Cerces:** The hour of the trial has come. Do you have an answer for me? And... how will you prove the veracity of your argument?

> **瑟希斯：**所以，试炼的时刻已至，汝打算如何证明吾的问题哪？

**Anaxa:** I suppose there is no point in putting this off any longer. Do you recall where we stopped the last time?

> **那刻夏：**既然都到这时候了…还记得我上次讲到哪里了么？

**Cerces:** You were saying that Amphoreus' history is cyclical...

> **瑟希斯：**汝声称：「翁法罗斯历史乃是『轮回』」——

**Anaxa:** Indeed. The demigods that bear the Coreflames eventually become the new Titans at the world's end...

> **那刻夏：**没错。背负「火种」的半神，将在世界尽头成为新的泰坦……

**Anaxa:** Remember what happened on the holy mountain? Calypso, Gnaeus... As their destined ends approached, one by one, they left this world... leaving only the twins, who were fated to succeed Death.

> **那刻夏：**记得在神山上的见闻吗？卡吕普索、格奈乌斯…那些英雄临到命运的尽头，次第离去，只留下递补「死亡」的双子。

**Anaxa:** Unfortunately, while their journey is consistent with ours — you and Calypso as well as Nikador and Gnaeus act as supporting evidence — I can only come up with a rough sketch for my theory of a cyclical history.

> **那刻夏：**可惜，就算他们的旅途与我们庶几相似，还有你与卡吕普索，尼卡多利与格奈乌斯从旁佐证，也只能为「轮回」的猜想勾勒蓝图。

**Anaxa:** For all the pains I took to reach this point, I had already lost hope of ever obtaining definitive proof. After all, the past is in the past. Proving that we are the legacies of heroes of the past is a hopeless endeavor.

> **那刻夏：**虽然大费周章走到这一步，但我对证明本来已不抱任何期望。毕竟，过去的皆已过去，要想证明我们是往日英雄的传承，无异于痴人说梦……

**Anaxa:** That is, until I met a girl named Polyxia and her sister.

> **那刻夏：**直到…我们见到了那名叫玻吕茜亚的少女和她的姐姐。

**Anaxa:** If Castorice was truly sent from the past to the present for the Titan's self-serving reasons, then having lived through two cycles and with Styxia as the pivot, she will no doubt be the link to connect all of this together.

> **那刻夏：**遐蝶——若她果真是泰坦出于私心，从过去送到现在的生灵——那么，以斯缇科西亚为「结点」，亲身走过两个轮回的她定能成为联结一切的「纽带」。

**Anaxa:** If the twins of Death can bridge two cycles to reunite at the shores of the Sea of Souls... then my hypothesis will no longer be just a hypothesis, and you, too, will get the answer to your question.

> **那刻夏：**如果「死亡」的双子能够跨越两世，在冥海边重逢——我的猜想便不再是一纸空谈，你的问题也能得到解答了。

**Cerces:** That is all well and good, but how can you be so sure? If there is even the slightest error in your hypothesis, the girl will be shattered.

> **瑟希斯：**那敢情好。可是，汝为何能如此断定？倘若这猜想出了差池，那女孩岂不是要败兴而归？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, it matters not. The most important quality a scholar must possess is foolhardiness. If you are overly cautious, then you'll never have the gumption to make the first step, and change will never come.

> **那刻夏：**哼，错了又如何？身为学者，最重要的品质正是不假思索的鲁莽。如果永远谨小慎微，无法大胆迈出第一步，那一切变化也无从谈起——

**Anaxa:** Besides, I, Anaxagoras, am destined to overturn this farcical world.

> **那刻夏：**更何况，我，阿那克萨戈拉斯，注定要颠覆这个有悖常理的世界。

**Anaxa:** Come, let us go and teach those pathetic worms one final lesson.

> **那刻夏：**走，我们去给那帮可怜虫们上最后一课。

### Head to the Demigod Council and witness the results of the citizens' assembly

*(Approach the top of the stair)*

**Anaxa:** Phainon... So, they are leaving the final debate to him.

> **那刻夏：**白厄…最后一辩交给他了啊。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Caenis:** Let those Chrysos Heirs who started this terrible war hear you!

> **凯妮斯：**让他们听听吧——那些发动了残暴战争的黄金裔——！

**Caenis:** Let them hear the voices of the people!

> **凯妮斯：**让他们听见人民的呼声——！

*fanatic cheering*

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Anaxa:** As always, that woman knows how to stir up a crowd.

> **那刻夏：**那女人还是一如既往，善于煽动人心。

**Phainon:** Professor Anaxa...? I thought you'd pass on this.

> **白厄：**那刻夏…老师？我还以为你不打算到场了。

**Anaxa:** Everyone must cast their vote, or this assembly would serve no purpose.

> **那刻夏：**票总是要投的，否则这场大会不就毫无意义了？

**Phainon:** Prof... Do you truly think it's wise to entrust the fate of the world to an assembly like this?

> **白厄：**老师，你当真觉得…将世界的命运托付在一场大会上，是正确的么？

**Anaxa:** Okhema has stood strong under this system for a thousand years. It stands to reason that there is purpose in its existence.

> **那刻夏：**奥赫玛在这一制度下屹立千年不倒，自然能证明其存在的合理性。

**Anaxa:** But that is not what you're asking, is it? What you really want to know is: Is Aglaea making the right decision in entrusting the fate of the world to you?

> **那刻夏：**但我认为，你想问的不是这个。真正令你迷茫的是…阿格莱雅将决定世界命运的机会托付给你，是否正确。

**Phainon:** ...Nothing ever escapes you.

> **白厄：**…永远这么一针见血啊。

**Anaxa:** I didn't spend all those years teaching you for nothing. Most people only take four years to graduate, if I recall.

> **那刻夏：**毕竟教了你那么久，别人可是最多四年就毕业了。

**Phainon:** Haha... To tell the truth, I feel like I'm on my way to an execution chamber instead of a podium.

> **白厄：**哈哈…坦率地讲，我感觉不像要上演讲台，反而像上刑场。

**Phainon:** So... do you have an answer to that question?

> **白厄：**所以，答案是？

**Anaxa:** No, I don't.

**Phainon:** I thought so...

> **白厄：**也是……

**Anaxa:** Because that question is not yours to ask in the first place.

> **那刻夏：**——因为这问题和你没有半点关系。

**Anaxa:** Is "Aglaea entrusting the world to you" the right decision? Don't make me laugh. That is a question that woman must answer herself. Why are you fretting about this on her behalf? Or have you been seeing yourself as her pawn all this time?

> **那刻夏：**「阿格莱雅将机会托付给你」是否正确？别引我发笑了，这不是那女人自己的问题么？为什么你要替她考虑这些？还是说，你一直都把自己当成她的工具？

**Anaxa:** Listen up, I'll say this only once. Flip your perspective around and ask yourself this instead, "What should I do to ensure that the world doesn't stray from the correct path?" That is what you should be thinking about.

> **那刻夏：**听好了，把思路逆转过来——「『我』要如何决定世界命运，才是正确的选择？」——这才是你真正应该思考的。

**Anaxa:** You still have time, so give it some thought. Oh, and don't bother telling me the answer.

> **那刻夏：**还有时间，好好想想你会如何作答吧，也别说给我听……

**Anaxa:** Tell it to those begging for salvation from the gods.

> **那刻夏：**去说给那群向诸神乞求拯救的人听。

**Phainon:** Got it.

> **白厄：**…明白了。

**Phainon:** Thank you, Professor Anaxa.

> **白厄：**谢谢你，那刻夏老师。

**Anaxa:** That's Anaxagoras to you.

> **那刻夏：**叫我阿那克萨戈拉斯。

**Phainon:** Who... who'll you be voting for?

> **白厄：**你…会把票投给哪边？

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** That'll depend on how well you perform... May Cerces safeguard your thoughts.

> **那刻夏：**看你的表现…愿瑟希斯捍卫你的思想。

### Sit down and listen to Phainon's speech to the public

*crowd chatter*

**Lygus:** Elders and citizens, silence, if you please. As the Theoros, I present to you:

> **来古士：**元老们，公民们，请肃静。以神礼观众之名，我见到——

**Lygus:** Phainon of Aedes Elysiae, virtuous citizen of Okhema and the last speaker of the assembly.

> **来古士：**——奥赫玛正直的公民，哀丽秘榭的白厄，将为本场大会带来最后的演说！

**Phainon:** Much obliged, Lord Lygus.

> **白厄：**感谢您，高贵的来古士阁下。

**Arrogant Citizen:** I remember him — he's the penniless lad working for Aglaea!

> **倨傲的公民：**我记得这家伙，给阿格莱雅提鞋的穷小子！

**Confused Citizen:** Where's Lady Goldweaver? Why is she sending this guy to give the last speech? Is this really the end of the Flame-Chase Journey?

> **困惑的公民：**「金织」女士呢？为什么派这家伙来压台？逐火之旅真要结束了？

**Calm Citizen:** Can this dude really face down Caenis...?

> **冷静的公民：**一介武夫？这小子能镇得住凯妮斯么……

*crowd chatter*

**Phainon:** By Kephale above, dear citizens of Okhema...

> **白厄：**刻法勒在上，各位奥赫玛的公民——

**Phainon:** As you look upon me, so too do I see you.

> **白厄：**——我看到了，你们所有人的目光都注视着我。

**Phainon:** I see your fear and misgivings, your apprehension about the discord between the Chrysos Heirs and the Council of Elders... and your uneasiness about the future of the holy city.

> **白厄：**你们的眼里饱含忧虑，不仅忧虑黄金裔与元老院之间滋生的裂隙，更忧虑这座圣城将要步入的明天。

**Phainon:** Yet, before you contemplate something as grand as the fate of the world, I ask that you look closer... Look, beyond the horizon illuminated by Kephale, at the crisis threatening to overwhelm us!

> **白厄：**现在，我想敬请各位：在思考「世界的命运」这般宏大的命题以前，请你们先擦亮自己的双眼…越过被刻法勒照亮的地平线，看清我们身处的危机！

**Calm Citizen:** What...?

> **冷静的公民：**这……

**Phainon:** Aedes Elysiae, my beloved hometown, is a village forgotten by time. It is a place with the most invigorating breeze and wheat fields that spread all the way to the sea... I once thought that there, I would enjoy a lifetime of peace and quiet with my friends and family.

> **白厄：**我的故乡哀丽秘榭，那是一处被时间遗忘的村落。那里的好风如薄荷般清新，麦浪宽广到能连上海洋…我曾坚信，自己会在那与邻里亲友永享安宁。

**Phainon:** How I wish I could invite all of you to that paradise far away from the noise and chatter of the world! Alas, I can't...

> **白厄：**我多想邀请诸位前去一睹那与世隔绝的好地方啊！可我做不到——

**Phainon:** ...Because she... and all that she nurtured... all that I loved... they have long been devoured by the black tide. My beloved home... is no more.

> **白厄：**因为，她和她哺育的一切，我所深爱的一切，早已被黑潮吞噬——我无家可归！

**Phainon:** I will never forget the day the black tide struck. My father, Hieronymus, fought with a broken sword to his last breath to protect his child. My mother, Audata... She was ripped apart by monsters, all so I might live to stand before you here and now.

> **白厄：**我永远都记得黑潮来临的那天：我的父亲希洛尼摩斯，为了他的孩子，用断剑力战到最后一刻；我的母亲奥妲塔，她为我能站在这里向诸位乞求，被怪物破开了胸膛！

**Phainon:** Galba... He was a hunter. But in the end, those arms that once drew a bow instead embraced Piso, his beloved son, as they both breathed their last. Pythias, the teacher, risked her life to save her students, but a burning beam fell on her, crushing her...

> **白厄：**猎户伽尔巴，他张弓的双臂，最终怀抱着他挚爱的幼子披索，二人双双死去；皮西厄斯，孩子们的老师，她拼命救下了所有学生，燃烧的梁木却压垮了她的脊梁……

**Phainon:** And what of Livia, her daughter? Did she make it out alive? ...No, she didn't. Livia, Piso, and so many other children... My childhood friends... They were all swallowed by the black tide and turned into monsters.

> **白厄：**可她的女儿莉维娅呢？她逃出生天了吗？没有：她、披索，还有其他许多孩子，我童年时最亲密的玩伴们…他们全都被黑潮吞没，变成了扭曲的造物！

**Phainon:** They kept saying the same thing over and over... even up until the final moment I finally steeled my heart and granted them eternal peace with my sword:

> **白厄：**直到我下定决心向他们挥剑，为他们带去体面的死亡，他们还在对我喃喃道——

**Phainon:** "Why, Phainon? Aren't we the best of friends?"

> **白厄：**「你怎么了，白厄？难道我们不是最好的朋友吗？」

**Aglaea:** ...

> **阿格莱雅：**……

**Phainon:** That is the black tide — a looming threat that can bring Okhema to its knees anytime. Caenis may have promised that, with the Three Titans of Calamity gone, Okhema will return to an Era Chrysea once more, but...

> **白厄：**这就是黑潮，奥赫玛如今迫在眉睫的威胁。凯妮斯或许承诺了，灾厄三泰坦皆已陨落，奥赫玛必将重返「黄金世」——

**Phainon:** Much as it pains me, I must disappoint you: While the demigod of Strife has done everything in his power to hold them back, "they" are still coming.

> **白厄：**但现在，我要让各位失望了。因为，我会对你们说：即便「纷争」之半神已为我们挡下许多，但「它」就要来了。

**Phainon:** By now, the name of that black-robed swordmaster... the Flame Reaver, has likely echoed throughout the city already. The roaring thunder is a message from the Strife demigod: The Flame Reaver has risen from death, and they hunger for the Coreflames of the gods.

> **白厄：**那黑袍剑士…「盗火行者」的名字，恐怕早已传遍大街小巷。现在，「纷争」用他的惊雷送来信号，告诉我们：它已自死灰中复活，并渴求着众神的火种。

**Arrogant Citizen:** Lies! If such a powerful enemy is indeed approaching, why is Okhema still a picture of peace?

> **倨傲的公民：**扯谎！如果这么恐怖的敌人近在眼前，奥赫玛岂能继续享受眼前的和平？

**Phainon:** Exactly! The city of Okhema shows no signs of pandemonium or chaos. And why is that? I beg you, spare a moment to think about this. Are we able to stand here and hold this assembly in peace today because the danger has passed?

> **白厄：**正是！奥赫玛美好的公民生活没有停歇的迹象。仔细想想吧，各位，这场公民大会能够和平召开，难道是因为危机消失了吗？

**Phainon:** No! This peace we enjoy is no gift of fate. We are able to stand here today only because the Chrysos Heirs have stepped up and shielded Okhema from calamity! With her golden threads, Aglaea has preserved — and continues to preserve — these idyllic days that all of you hold dear!

> **白厄：**不！那是因为黄金裔们挺身而出，将灾厄挡在了奥赫玛的城墙之外——阿格莱雅用她的金线，维系并捍卫了你们所能享用的一切！

**Anaxa:** Heh...

> **那刻夏：**呵……

**Phainon:** The Council of Elders claims that Aglaea will one day forsake the people because her divinity is eroding her body and soul. But ask yourselves this: Rather than offering her aid and support, why do they choose to watch and push Okhema away from the Flame-Chase Journey?

> **白厄：**元老院说，她会抛下所有人，只因神性正在蚕食她的身心。但各位可曾想过，那些贵人本能向她伸出援手——他们为何选择了冷眼旁观，并将奥赫玛推离逐火的事业？

**Phainon:** The answer that no one can bring themselves to speak of... A truth stained in blood and buried in greed, fear, and vanity... Allow me to lay it bare before you.

> **白厄：**那无人能说得出口的事实…那血淋淋的，被贪婪、恐惧和虚荣所埋没的真相，就由我来掘地三尺，将它呈现在你们眼前吧。

**Phainon:** It is because the Council of Elders, which bears Okhema's history and honor, along with this citizens' assembly we once took so much pride in, are now infested with vermin!

> **白厄：**承载着奥赫玛悠久历史和威严的元老院，我们曾引以为豪的公民大会——如今已经遍地虫豸！

**Angry Citizen:** You insolent whelp! How dare you—

> **恼火的公民：**放肆！你怎么敢在圣地——

**Phainon:** It is true that Aglaea has reached her limit. Why? Because she has been working tirelessly to weave the defenses of our holy city against the encroaching evil — all while these vermin are making up baseless accusations about her and scheming in the shadows!

> **白厄：**对！阿格莱雅现在独木难支了——因为，在虫豸们于光天化日下望风捕影、酝酿阴谋之时，她正呕心沥血地编织圣城的防御网，以对抗世间无孔不入的恶毒！

**Phainon:** Not once has she ever failed Okhema, yet these vermin attempt to tear her apart, tarnishing her name with unfounded allegations! She may have been blessed by divinity, but hasn't her heart always stayed true to humanity?

> **白厄：**她从未辜负奥赫玛的期待，却只因虫豸的围攻撕咬、就该蒙受莫大的羞辱吗？除却那后至的「神性」…她的本心，难道不是始终与「人」同在吗？

**Phainon:** They attempt to mislead you and reduce this assembly to a crude standoff between gods and humans, but citizens of Okhema! Once more, I implore you to open your eyes and look at those around you!

> **白厄：**他们想欺骗你们，把这场大会粗暴地归结成「人」与「神」的对立。还记得我的请求吗？奥赫玛的公民们，请擦亮双眼，仔细端详一下站在你们身边的人吧！

**Phainon:** Examine them closely! Beneath all that finery, do you see the good heart of a human... or the poisonous glands of a pest?

> **白厄：**看得再仔细些！看看被那些华贵的衣袍所遮盖的，是人的热肠？还是害虫的毒腺？

**Phainon:** Make your judgment, then turn your gaze to the demigods whom so many of you regard as cold and merciless — do you not see the light of humanity in them? And those who wear the mask of humanity yet utter only hurtful, slanderous words — can you not see that their souls have been corrupted by power?

> **白厄：**待你们在心中下完了判断，再把目光投向她们吧——看看那你们以为冷漠的半神，是否还闪耀着人性的光；而那披着人皮恶语中伤的，又是否着了权欲的魔！

**Phainon:** Yet, no matter what your answer is, and no matter who your vote goes to... rest assured that you have nothing to fear.

> **白厄：**但无论你们得出了什么样的答案，最终将手中的陶片投给了谁…你们都不必有后顾之忧。

**Phainon:** For Aglaea, Tribios, and I will remain your faithful guardians and pledge our lives to upholding your decision.

> **白厄：**因为我、阿格莱雅、缇里西庇俄丝，我们仍会是你们忠诚的护墙，誓死捍卫你们所有人的抉择——

**Phainon:** And this vow will stay true until the end of our days, where it will be passed on to future Flame-Chasers...

> **白厄：**如此承诺，将一直延续至我们生命的终点，并被传递给未来的逐火之人——

**Phainon:** ...A steadfast and unwavering promise that shall endure through the ages!

> **白厄：**万世不息，永不动摇！

*crowd chatter*

In an uproar, representative leaders from different cities in Okhema throw pieces of pottery into the dolium...

**Lygus:** At the present time, on the scales of Talanton, those who call for the continuation of the Flame-Chase Journey and those who demand its suspension...

> **来古士：**截至目前，「继续逐火之旅」者与「暂停逐火之旅」者，双方民意在塔兰顿的天平上……

**Lygus:** Each faction holds an equal share of the votes, resulting in the perfect balance.

> **来古士：**票数相等，形成了绝佳的平衡。

**Anaxa:** As expected, this is the moment when the Flame-Chase Journey is the closest to its end.

> **那刻夏：**不出所料，这是逐火之旅距离「中止」最近的一刻。

**Lygus:** And now, it's your turn to vote, Lord Anaxagoras.

> **来古士：**现在，轮到您了，阿那克萨戈拉斯阁下。

**Anaxa:** Is there anyone else who hasn't voted?

> **那刻夏：**还有其他人没有投票么？

**Lygus:** No, you're the last. Cast your vote with care, for your decision will break this impasse.

> **来古士：**您是最后一位。慎重思考吧，您的庄严一票将打破这场完美的僵局。

**Anaxa:** I have long since made a decision.

> **那刻夏：**我早有把握。

**Lygus:** Very well. May you bring much-needed change to this farcical world.

> **来古士：**甚好。衷心祝愿您为这有悖常理的世界带来真正的变革。

### Head to the dolium and place the last vote

> *The citizens' assembly on the matter of "Pausing the Flame-Chase Journey" is coming to an end. Soon, everyone will witness what "vote-decided tomorrow" they will experience. You hold the last ceramic shard that will decide Amphoreus's fate. Where will your vote fall?*

**Adult female:** Who will he vote for?

**Adult male:** Hmph, always the performer.

**Adult female:** Talk about a grand entrance...

**Adult male:** Vote for Caenis!

**Adult female:** The die is cast...

**Caenis:** I trust you haven't forgotten the world we've envisioned together.

> **凯妮斯：**别忘了你我共同筹划的愿景，阁下。

*(Talk to Aglaea, optional)*

**Aglaea:** Heh...

> **阿格莱雅：**呵……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Anaxa:** You've done well, Phainon. Given the circumstances, delivering a rousing speech to stir up emotions was a prudent move.

> **那刻夏：**你干得很好，白厄。在那种形势下，用激烈的言论激发起情绪是一步妙招。

**Anaxa:** Thank you all for your patience. On behalf of the Seven Sages of the Grove of Epiphany and the scholars of Amphoreus, I, Anaxagoras, am ready to cast my vote.

> **那刻夏：**感谢诸位耐心等待。我，阿那克萨戈拉斯，谨代表神悟树庭七贤人与全体学者，投下庄严一票。

**Anaxa:** But before that, in the interest of fairness, Lady Goldweaver, I pose a question to you before all who are gathered here:

> **那刻夏：**不过为了公平起见，「金织」女士，我会在所有人面前向你提问——

**Anaxa:** Will you, as Phainon has promised, stand by the decision made in the assembly, regardless of outcome?

> **那刻夏：**请你告诉我：你确实如白厄所说，无论结果如何，都将捍卫本次大会庄严的决议么？

**Aglaea:** In the interest of fairness... I make no promises, lest it should influence your decision.

> **阿格莱雅：**为公平起见…我不会向你做出任何承诺，以免干扰你的判断。

**Anaxa:** Heh, very well. I thank you for your honesty.

> **那刻夏：**呵，那敢情好。感谢您的肺腑之言。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Confused Citizen:** What's he doing?

> **困惑的公民：**这家伙干嘛呢？

**Calm Citizen:** The stakes are high. Let's be patient.

> **冷静的公民：**事关重大，给他一点时间吧。

**Arrogant Citizen:** Cut the act! Who do you think you are, wasting our time like this?

> **倨傲的公民：**别摆谱了！你以为自己是什么东西，要我们陪你在这儿浪费时间？

**Caenis:** Lord Anaxa, please do not forget that I—

> **凯妮斯：**那刻夏阁下，别忘了……

**Anaxa:** Rule number one: Do not call me Anaxa. Rule number two: Never interrupt me. Silence is golden.

> **那刻夏：**第一，别叫我那刻夏；第二，别打断我——沉默是金。

*crowd chatter*

Citizens' Assembly: Just vote already! What are you waiting for?

Citizens' Assembly: Cast... your... vote...

**Cerces:** I cannot help but wonder: Is every hardship in the world something you will exploit and use to your advantage?

> **瑟希斯：**吾不禁在想，是否世间一切磨难都会被汝当作物尽其用的手段。

**Anaxa:** Heh, how else am I supposed to make use of this situation where my body and soul are separated?

> **那刻夏：**呵呵，身体和灵魂一分为二，不就该这么用吗？

**Anaxa:** O Cerces — or should I say, Calypso — accompany me on this final journey.

> **那刻夏：**吾师瑟希斯，亦或卡吕普索，最后陪我走一遭吧——

**Anaxa:** Let us journey to the nether realm and see how she will substantiate the answer to your question and validate my decision.

> **那刻夏：**走，到冥界去——去看看她将如何给你的提问、我的抉择，带来完美的证明。

**Anaxa:** We need only to follow the sound of the tides...

> **那刻夏：**只要，循着潮声……

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** You... you are...

> **那刻夏：**你是……

**Empedocles:** Well done, child. Just as I foresaw, you have achieved great things.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**很好，孩子…我早就说过，你会大有作为的。

**Anaxa:** Teacher...

> **那刻夏：**吾师……

**Empedocles:** What a twist of fate... A blasphemer raised in the doctrines of the Venerationists. It's like a foal kicking its mother in the belly after taking all her milk.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**真是奇妙的际遇啊…「敬拜学派」教出了一名渎神者，简直就像是小毛驴，吸干了妈妈的奶，反倒踢了妈妈一脚。

**Anaxa:** I respect you immensely, sir, but—

> **那刻夏：**我敬爱你，老师。但你明白……

**Empedocles:** You cherish the truth even more.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**你更加爱真理。

**Empedocles:** So, child, do not be troubled by the criticisms I have borne. Do not let those voices hinder you as you forge ahead.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**所以，孩子，不必为我所承受的非议感到遗憾。别让那些噪音掣住你前进的步伐。

**Empedocles:** As the Sage of the Venerationists, I am duty-bound to oppose you. But as your friend and mentor, Anaxagoras, I say:

> **恩贝多克利斯：**作为「敬拜学派」的贤人，世俗要求我不能认同你；但是，作为阿那克萨戈拉斯的友人与导师，我会说……

**Empedocles:** Go forth, for the truth is already in your hands.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**「去做吧，真理已尽在你手中了。」

**Anaxa:** ...I will keep that in mind.

> **那刻夏：**…我会铭记于心。

**Empedocles:** Go now, child. Your lonely pursuit is about to welcome a new chapter...

> **恩贝多克利斯：**好了，前进吧，孩子。你孤独的求索路即将迎来新的起点——

**Empedocles:** And when you reach the end of this journey, may we meet again with the truth before us.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**而当你抵达其尽头时，愿我们在真理前相会。

**Anaxa:** You have my word, sir. May Cerces safeguard your thoughts.

> **那刻夏：**我向你保证，老师。愿瑟希斯捍卫你的思想。

**Empedocles:** May Cerces safeguard your thoughts.

> **恩贝多克利斯：**愿瑟希斯捍卫你的思想。

> **那刻夏：**……

**Anaxa:** You are right, Empedocles. My lonely pursuit is about to welcome a new chapter. I must be on my way now...

**Anaxa:** May we meet again with the truth before us, Empedocles.

> **那刻夏：**——愿我们在真理前相会，吾师。

### Complete the Great Performer's performance

> *The citizens' assembly on the matter of "Pausing the Flame-Chase Journey" is coming to an end. Soon, everyone will witness what "vote-decided tomorrow" they will experience. How foolish, trying to decide the fate of the world through votes when the apocalypse looms... Your professional ethics demand that you give these fools an education.*

> **那刻夏：**看吧，瑟希斯——此刻，在世界的中心，我将写下最后一步证明……

> **那刻夏：**——此处距离真理，只有一步之遥。

> **那刻夏：**我毫无疑问——

> **那刻夏：**会选择「逐火之旅」！

**Anaxa:** I have deciphered the truth of this world —

> **那刻夏：**世界的真理，我已解明——

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Lygus:** With the balance shattered, the result is clear. By Kephale and Talanton above, as the Theoros, I have observed...

> **来古士：**平衡已被打破，结果已经明朗。刻法勒与塔兰顿在上，以神礼观众之名，我见到——

**Caenis:** No! Lygus, you can't...!

> **凯妮斯：**不…来古士，等等…！

**Caenis:** I hope you are prepared for the harsh punishment awaiting you! Mark my words, I will make sure that clever head of yours never forgets the price of betrayal!

> **凯妮斯：**我会准备好与你相称的酷刑…而你得用那颗聪明脑袋好好记住，什么是背叛的代价…！

**Anaxa:** Have you forgotten? I told you, that happens to be one of my rare talents.

> **那刻夏：**还记得吗？我说过，这恰恰是我最宝贵的天赋之一。

**Caenis:** You put on such a grand performance... just to aid their preposterous Flame-Chase Journey!?

> **凯妮斯：**你不惜演到这份上…就为了帮助他们完成愚蠢的逐火之旅…？！

**Anaxa:** Performance? No, you are quite mistaken. I may be paranoid by nature, but I've always been known for my honesty.

**Anaxa:** If you really understood me, Lady Caenis, you'd realize that I never had any interest in your pathetic little game of politics. Factions, standpoints... they are jokes in my eyes. I have never once switched sides...

> **那刻夏：**是你误会了，凯妮斯阁下。你不了解我，我从不关心你们那可怜的政治游戏。什么阵营、立场，无一例外，全都是笑话。我的选择从未变过……

**Anaxa:** Because I, and I alone, am the truth.

> **那刻夏：**只有我自己，才是唯一确凿的真理。

> **那刻夏：**告诉我！在座各位，有多少人敢对刻法勒起誓，「再创世」的尽头必将如诸位黄金裔所说，是一片没有灾厄与疯狂的美丽新世界？
